## ME-JEPA System Documentation — Master Index

> **Purpose.** Authoritative technical reference for the **ME-JEPA-Code Reality Truth System** as it exists in the ContextGraph repository today AND as the futurebuild plan specifies it when complete. Every section is sourced from the live tree (`crates/`, `docs/futurebuild/`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/`, `.claude/`, `memory/`) — no aspirations beyond what those documents commit us to.
>
> **Audience.** Engineers reading the codebase cold, operators running the daemon, researchers comparing the system to JEPA/SSL literature, future contributors picking up a TASK-* file.
>
> **Authority order** (when docs conflict): `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md` → `docs/futurebuild/00_INDEX.md` → `CLAUDE.md §11` → these docs. These docs **describe**; they do not **decide**.

---

### 1. The single sentence

ME-JEPA-Code is a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture that, given (code change + context), outputs a calibrated, multi-head prediction of what the deterministic reality oracle (Docker test runner) would say if the change were actually executed — and answers four additional questions (existence, edge cases / latent bugs / tech debt / perf / security / accuracy / cost concerns, reality impact) about every change, every time.

### 5. Glossary (one-line definitions)

| Term | Definition |
|---|---|
| **ME-JEPA-Code** | The predictive truth system being built. JEPA-class with **frozen externally-defined target instruments** (structural collapse immunity). |
| **Panel** | A 5,120-d fixed-shape vector assembled from 15 instrument slots (per chunk × per shift). Persisted to `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS`. |
| **Instrument** | A frozen, deterministic function from a typed event → a fixed-dim feature subvector. 15 of them, summing to 5,120-d. |
| **DDA** | Diagonal-Dominance-Augmentation: every input × N frozen embedders → n·(N + N²) structured signals (cosines + pairwise cosines + cached MI). |
| **TCT** | Teleological Constellation Training: per-(category × entity × language × embedder) centroids + per-embedder threshold τ_m → deterministic geometric guard. |
| **Gτ guard** | The TCT runtime test: `cos(embedder_m(predicted_panel), centroid_m) ≥ τ_m` for every embedder. Failure → `Verdict::GuardRejected`. |
| **Shift** | One (file, before_sha, after_sha, line_diff, cargo_check_result) tuple. Captured by hooks; appended to `cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl`. |
| **RealityPrediction** | The unified output of `mejepa_verify` / `mejepa_predict_latest`. Carries verdict + Q1–Q5 surfaces + provenance. |
| **Verdict** | `Pass` / `Fail` / `OutOfDistribution` / `Abstain` / `GuardRejected`. |
| **SurpriseEvent** | An agent-side feedback record bound to a `prediction_id`. `Confirmed` / `Surprise` / `Omission` / `Calibration`. |
| **UTML** | The four-factor learning formula `L_step = ΔP × ΔK × ΔΩ × ΔΞ`. All factors clamped to [0, 1]; multiplicative gating. |
| **Witness chain** | Append-only SHAKE-256 73-byte chain + ed25519 segment signing + Stage 0/F/Merkle certificates. The audit substrate. |
| **SMAP** | Shared Mind Agent Protocol. `./memory/` 8-namespace tree (`context/decisions/discoveries/handoff/journal/blockers/patterns/reports/`). |
| **FSV** | Forensic Source Verification. The per-task evidence protocol: read source-of-truth back, ≥3 boundary cases, no mocks. Defined by `docs/futurebuild/specs/FSV-PROTOCOL.md`. |
| **Reality-shift principle** | "No alteration without current state. No alteration without a delta back into perception." Hooks enforce. |
| **GENERIC-ONLY** | No per-task or per-language hard-coding in the trained model. Mental check: "would this primitive be load-bearing for every SWE-bench task in every language?" |
| **D-drive policy** | All durable state lives under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/`. Nothing growing on C:\. Locked decision. |
| **Daemon mode** | `cargo run --release -p context-graph-mcp -- --daemon` — long-running supervised process hosting the shift subscriber, self-optimization scheduler, nightly GC, and weekly eval as tokio tasks. |

### 7. The 8 mutation categories (Phase 0 corpus)

For cross-reference: every category × language × entity-type × embedder combination forms a constellation cell (≈ 13,728 cells at full scale). Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-corpus/src/mutation/*` and `docs/futurebuild/04_DDA_TCT_CODE_ENRICHMENT.md §4`.

| # | Category | Purpose | Operator behavior |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `KnownGood` | Baseline positive class | Echoes the upstream gold patch verbatim |
| 2 | `SubtleFlip` | Logic-flip class | Swaps `==`↔`!=`, `<`↔`>=`, `&&`↔`\|\|` per language |
| 3 | `OffByOne` | Boundary class | Perturbs integer literals on integer-typed sites |
| 4 | `SwapVariable` | Variable confusion | Renames a local variable; introduces use of wrong name |
| 5 | `DeleteTestCall` | Test deletion | Removes one `assert`/`assert_eq`/`expect` call |
| 6 | `WrongFile` | Cross-file misdirection | Applies the gold patch to an adjacent file |
| 7 | `OverEngineer` | Useless complexity | Adds an unused helper function/method |
| 8 | `CompileError` | Hard failure | Introduces a parse error |

---

### 8. The 5 verdict classes

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/types.rs` (via `docs/futurebuild/15_PREDICTION_TAXONOMY.md §2`).

| Verdict | Trigger | Remediation hint |
|---|---|---|
| `Pass` | conformal lower bound ≥ ship-gate AND no high-severity Q4 AND every claim reconciles | trust the agent's done signal |
| `Fail` | ≥1 predicted_failed_test at confidence ≥ failure threshold | agent must address predicted failure path |
| `OutOfDistribution` | `ood_score ≥ ood_threshold` OR conformal interval width > `width_threshold` | re-attempt with smaller scope; flag to active-learning queue |
| `Abstain` | conformal interval too wide to commit | request more context; do not block but do not pass |
| `GuardRejected` | any embedder's cosine to its constellation centroid < `τ_m` | predicted panel lies outside the manifold of acceptable predictions |

### 9. The 13 active embedders (code-domain)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/routing.rs` (`docs/futurebuild/05_EMBEDDER_SIGNAL_MAP.md §1`).

E1, E2, E3, E4, E6, E7, E8, E9, E10, E12, E13, E14, E17 = 13 active. E5 (causal asymmetric) and E11 (KEPLER entity) are **retired** post-2026-05-09 and fail closed with `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_RETIRED`. E15, E16, E19, E21 are non-code learner-state slots reserved for ME-JEPA-X domains. E18 and E20 carry partial signal at low weight.

### 13. The 8 implementation phases

Source: `docs/futurebuild/12_IMPLEMENTATION_PHASES.md`. Effort estimates assume single engineer.

| Phase | Goal | Effort | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| A — Operational Unblock | Daemon + prediction-injection + agent-feedback + D-drive enforcement | 5–7d | partial (code/FSV shipped; fresh-host install proof pending) |
| B — Multi-Head Extension | 8 new prediction heads + label sources + per-head deltas | 10–14d | futurebuild |
| C — Corpus Completion | 300×8 SWE-bench Lite + 10 per-language operators | 7–10d | partial (Python 1,200/2,400 rows; non-Python pending) |
| D — Neural Embedder Forwards | Replace deterministic placeholders with real Candle CUDA forwards | 10–15d | partial (wrappers exist for E1/E6/E7/E8/E10/E12/E13/E14) |
| E — Continuous Self-Optimization | Scheduler policy + per-cell drift + dissipation + λ ramp | 5–7d | partial (skeleton + 7 tickers shipped; full autonomous policy pending) |
| F — Production Observability | Weekly eval cron + MI heatmap + WSL GPU telemetry + dashboard | 5–7d | futurebuild |
| G — Ship-Gate Convergence | Run continuously until per-cell correlation ≥ 0.95 stable | ongoing | n/a — operational |
| H — Inner-Loop Residue Cleanup | Apply deletion list; supersede SMAP files; retire legacy tools | 1–2d | partial |

---

### 14. The reality-loop contract (one-liner)

Each `/loop` firing is one Claude Code turn. The turn (a) reads SMAP context + futurebuild docs + spec status, (b) picks the highest-priority open work item (build or capture), (c) executes end-to-end with FSV evidence, (d) records `optimizer_record_recommendation` + a SMAP `./memory/reports/...md` report, (e) ends. The daemon's continuous self-optimization scheduler consumes every shift / oracle outcome / agent-feedback event automatically — no manual `observe()` calls.

Full text: [`21_loop_contract.md`](21_loop_contract.md) + `.claude/loop.md`.

## 01 — System Overview

Source files covered: `Cargo.toml`, `crates/*/Cargo.toml`, `crates/*/src/lib.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/main.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/protocol.rs`, `crates/context-graph-cli/src/main.rs`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md`, `docs/futurebuild/00_INDEX.md`, `docs/futurebuild/01_VISION_TRUTH_SYSTEM.md`, `docs/futurebuild/02_CURRENT_STATE_HONEST.md`, `docs/futurebuild/03_ARCHITECTURE_BLUEPRINT.md`, `CLAUDE.md`.

---

### 1. What the system does

ME-JEPA-Code is a **deterministic predictive truth system** for AI-generated code. It accepts a (code change + context) tuple and returns a calibrated, multi-head **`RealityPrediction`** answering five operator questions (Q1–Q5 in `docs/futurebuild/01_VISION_TRUTH_SYSTEM.md §3`): does the agent's claim **EXIST** on disk, does the code **WORK**, **WHY** would it fail, **WHAT ELSE** could go wrong (edge cases / tech debt / perf / security / accuracy / cost), and how does the patch **IMPACT REALITY**. The system runs as an **MCP (Model Context Protocol) server** locally on operator hardware. AI agents (Claude Code, Cursor, etc.) call it via JSON-RPC stdio for predictions and feedback. Underneath, a **continuous self-optimization daemon** consumes every Edit shift, every test outcome, and every agent-feedback event to retrain itself online, making the predictor measurably better the more it is used.

The system is JEPA-class. The architectural innovation that distinguishes ME-JEPA-Code from prior JEPA work is **structural collapse-immunity on the target side**: the target representation is produced by a panel of **frozen externally-defined instruments** (deterministic functions of typed events — AST chunks, oracle outcomes, witness chain entries, type-graph features, etc.), with zero trainable parameters. Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/11_MEJEPA_LEARNING_FORMULA.md §5.2`: `ΔΞ.target_collapse = 0` by construction.

The ship-gate threshold is `prediction_oracle_correlation ≥ 0.95` stable over four consecutive rolling windows on the 300×8 SWE-bench Lite mutation corpus, stratified per (mutation_category × language). When that's met, human code review of AI-generated patches is statistically unjustified.

---

### 2. Architecture (process / port / technology / purpose)

| Process | Port / Channel | Technology | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`context-graph-mcp`** (MCP server) | stdio JSON-RPC; optional `--daemon-port 3100` / `--http-port 3101` | Rust 1.84+ / Tokio | Exposes 19+ `mcp__cgreality__mejepa_*` tools + the broader `cgreality` family; owns path-policy + edit-lock + ledger; gates all on-disk reality access |
| **`mejepa-daemon` (supervised tasks)** | inherits the MCP process; supervised via `tokio::JoinSet` | Rust / Tokio | Runs the shift subscriber + continuous self-optimization scheduler + nightly GC + weekly evaluation as long-running supervised tasks |
| **Shift subscriber** | tails `/mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl` | tokio file watcher | Materializes a Panel within 250ms of each Edit/Write; triggers `verify()` → persists `RealityPrediction` to `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` |
| **Self-optimization scheduler** | 7 internal tickers (250ms / 60s / 600s / 3600s / 1800s) | tokio intervals | Drains pending shifts → `observe()`; classifies drift; surfaces active-learning queue; triggers LoRA refresh / A/B/C promotion / continual backprop |
| **Predictor forward** | CUDA queue (single GPU; CUDA 13.2; sm_120) | candle 0.9.x + cudarc | 6-layer transformer trunk (hidden 1024, 8 heads) + Panel head + Oracle head + 8 futurebuild auxiliary heads |
| **`context-graph-cli`** | command-line (operator + hook scripts) | Rust + clap | Operator surface: `mejepa active-learning`, `dynamicjepa`, `learning`, `utl`, `warmup`, `watch`, `setup`, `ccreality record-harness-transition`, `blackbox` (BlackBoxFix), `backfill-e14`, etc. |
| **Claude Code session** (the agent) | invokes MCP via Anthropic CLI | Anthropic Claude Code | The outer-loop reality-loop driver per `CLAUDE.md §11`. Edits land via Edit/Write/Bash; hook scripts append shift entries |
| **Hook scripts** | `.claude/settings.json` SessionStart / PreToolUse / PostToolUse / UserPromptSubmit / Stop / PreCompact | bash + cli helpers | Inject reality context per prompt; record pre/after SHAs; require Stop-hook recommendation |
| **RocksDB store** | unix domain / direct mmap | `rocksdb 0.21.x` | Persistent storage; 101 CFs total, 28+ ME-JEPA-specific. Lives under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/contextgraph-rocksdb/` |
| **Docker oracle** | invoked by Phase 0 corpus generator + capture-mode test runs | `swebench.harness.run_evaluation` (Python) | Authoritative test outcome for SWE-bench Lite; produces `OracleVerdict { resolved, F2P / P2P / F2F / P2F }` |
| **Embedder model artifacts** | local files under `/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/` | GGUF / safetensors / ONNX | Per-embedder SHA-pinned weight files; lazy-loaded by E1/E7/E10/E12/E13/E14/E17/E8/E11(retired) |
| **Witness chain** | append-only | SHAKE-256 73-byte / ed25519 | Cryptographic provenance for every panel, training cert, prediction, feedback event |
| **SMAP shared mind** | `./memory/` 8-namespace tree | markdown + YAML frontmatter | Cross-turn team narrative; replaces ephemeral context |
| **WSL host** | Linux 6.6 on Windows + RTX 5090 | WSL2 + CUDA 13.2 | Single-node deployment surface |

### 3. Technology stack

Source: `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §tech_stack` + the per-crate `Cargo.toml` files.

| Layer | Technology | Version constraint | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Language | Rust | 1.84+, edition 2021 | Workspace; no nightly required |
| Runtime | Tokio | 1.x (full features) | Async + supervised tasks via JoinSet |
| ML framework | candle | 0.9.x (alpha) | Pure-Rust GPU (`candle-core`, `candle-nn`, `candle-transformers`); CUDA via `cudarc` |
| CUDA target | sm_120 (Blackwell / RTX 5090) | CUDA 13.2 | Production predictor is CUDA-only; CPU construction is rejected, not silently down-graded |
| AST parser | tree-sitter | 0.23.x | 11 grammars: rust, python, javascript, typescript, go, java, c, cpp, c-sharp, ruby, php |
| Storage | RocksDB | 0.21.x | Hybrid backend with AgentDB (per CLAUDE.md §4); 101 CFs; tier-compressed cold storage |
| Hashing | sha3 | 0.7.x | SHAKE-256 for the 73-byte witness chain |
| Signing | ed25519-dalek | 2.x | Feature-gated `ed25519` segment signing in `context-graph-witness` |
| Tokenizers | HuggingFace `tokenizers` | 0.4.x | E1, E7, E10, E14 tokenizers |
| Serialization | serde, serde_json | 1.0.x | JSON for provenance / SourceMetadata; **never bincode + skip_serializing_if** (locked) |
| Quantization | clean-room | crate-local | 8/7/5/3-bit groupwise linear scalar quantization (`context-graph-tier-compression`) |
| Mincut | clean-room | crate-local | Stoer-Wagner O(V³) global mincut (`context-graph-mincut`) |
| Mutation operators | clean-room | crate-local | 8 Python operators shipped; 10 non-Python pending (Phase C) |
| MCP protocol | JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio | spec-compliant | Single network surface per `CLAUDE.md §11.9`. No HTTP from runtime |
| Docker oracle | upstream `swebench` | unmodified | Reality boundary per `CLAUDE.md §8`. **NEVER edit `swebench/**`.** |
| Test runner | `cargo test` | std | Real synthetic data only; no mocks |
| Lint | `cargo clippy` | std | Required before commit |

### 4. Complete public API / tools / endpoints

#### 4.1 ME-JEPA MCP tool surface (19 live tools at HEAD)

Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/_README.md`. Full details in [`18_mcp_tool_reference.md`](18_mcp_tool_reference.md).

| Tool | One-line behavior |
|---|---|
| `mejepa_verify` | Synchronous: take a patch + context, return a full `RealityPrediction` (Q1–Q5) |
| `mejepa_predict_latest` | Read the latest `RealityPrediction` for a session from `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` |
| `mejepa_constellation_inspect` | Inspect TCT centroids; per-chunk-type nearest-cell lookup; force refit |
| `mejepa_heal_status` | Current drift level, queued interventions, last A/B/C result |
| `mejepa_rollback_to` | Operator-forced rollback to a previous model variant (active pointer change) |
| `mejepa_load_learner_state` | Load saved learner-state (E15–E21) snapshot |
| `mejepa_routing_lookup` | Lookup the per-(Language × EntityType) embedder routing table |
| `mejepa_vram_budget_report` | GPU VRAM accounting vs budget; per-model headroom |
| `mejepa_gc_run` | Force nightly GC run (demote + compact + evict + prune + verify); supports `--force-eviction` |
| `mejepa_quota_status` | Disk usage vs 115 GB hard quota; per-slice breakdown |
| `mejepa_witness_compress` | Force Merkle compression on witness chain entries > 90 days |
| `mejepa_observe_shift` | Manually trigger an observe() over a specific shift_id (daemon does this automatically) |
| `mejepa_subscriber_status` | Shift subscriber lag, latency p50/p99, last panic info |
| `mejepa_capture_audit` | Bind an outer-loop SWE-bench attempt to the corpus (write audit entry) |
| `mejepa_eval_run` | Force a holdout-eval run; per-(category × language) correlation |
| `mejepa_ship_gate_status` | Per-cell ≥0.95 ship-gate status with shrinkage; pass/fail cells |
| `mejepa_active_learning_queue` | High-priority queue contents (uncertain predictions, agent surprises) |
| `mejepa_eval_build_graph` | Rebuild patch-similarity graph for cross-task transfer |
| `mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi` | Weekly pairwise-MI audit across embedder outputs; CSV at `/mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/pairwise-mi/` |

| Entry | How invoked | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `cargo run --release -p context-graph-mcp` | from any directory | Foreground MCP stdio server (typical for Claude Code MCP attach) |
| `cargo run --release -p context-graph-mcp -- --daemon-server-only --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph --daemon-port 3100 --http-port 3101` | `scripts/ensure_daemon.sh` or systemd | Long-running daemon: validates D-root, opens RocksDB, starts supervised tasks |
| `context-graph-cli <subcommand>` | operator / hooks | CLI surface (15 groups) |
| `context-graph` | symlink | Alias for `context-graph-cli` |
| `blackbox` | (separate binary) | BlackBoxFix ledger + API server |
| `context-graph-mejepa-corpus generate/verify/stats/prepare-images/cleanup` | operator | Phase 0 mutation corpus CLI |
| `context-graph-mejepa-train` (binary in `mejepa-train`) | operator | Training driver (cert chain, holdout eval) |
| `mejepa-heal-drill` (binary in `mejepa`) | operator / tests | Synthetic drift drill |
| `mejepa-verify` | operator / tests | Inference CLI: `verify` → `Verdict` |
| `.claude/settings.json` hooks | Claude Code | `SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreToolUse`, `PostToolUse`, `Stop`, `PreCompact` (Claude Code-managed) |
| `scripts/cc/hook_*.sh` | invoked by Claude Code hooks | SHA recording, shift-log append, cargo check, prediction injection |
| `scripts/ensure_daemon.sh` | operator | Bootstraps the warm daemon + stdio proxy |
| `scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service` | `systemctl --user enable --now` | Systemd user-unit running daemon mode |

### 6. Directory structure (runtime)

Source: `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`.

```
/mnt/d/contextgraph/                            # ALL durable bulk state lives here (locked)
├── storage/
│   ├── contextgraph-rocksdb/                   # RocksDB CFs (101 total, 28+ ME-JEPA)
│   ├── replay/                                 # replay-buffer materializations
│   ├── cold/                                   # 3-bit / 1-bit frozen tier blobs
│   ├── witness/                                # witness chain segments + Merkle bundles
│   ├── train-certs/                            # per-step training certs (mirrored from CF)
│   ├── live-predictions/                       # mirrors CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS
│   └── embedder-cache/                         # per-(embedder, chunk_sha) cache
├── runtime/
│   ├── cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl     # per-session shift-log JSONL (append-only)
│   └── test-outcomes/                          # parsed pytest/cargo-test/jest TestOutcomeEvent
├── logs/
│   └── mejepa-daemon.stdout.log                # rotated daily; 30-day retention
├── state/
│   ├── locks/
│   │   ├── context-graph-daemon.pid
│   │   ├── mcp.sock                            # MCP unix domain socket (daemon mode)
│   │   └── reality-loop-stdio.lock             # singleton lock (replaces stale MCP)
│   ├── cgreality/
│   │   └── active_runtime_root                 # active runtime root pointer
│   └── gold-labels/                            # operator-approved gold labels (permanent)
├── fsv/<crate>-fsv/                            # FSV evidence files (mode 0600)
├── corpus/<corpus-id>/                         # SWE-bench mutation corpus runs
│   ├── index.json
│   ├── stats.json
│   ├── verify-evidence.json
│   └── oracle-store/                           # RocksDB with CF_MEJEPA_MUTATION_CORPUS, etc.
└── exports/
    ├── active-learning/<date>.json
    ├── eval/<date>/weekly.md                   # weekly operator report
    └── pairwise-mi/<date>.csv

/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/              # SHA-pinned embedder model artifacts (separate budget)
└── <embedder_id>/                              # GGUF / safetensors / ONNX + tokenizer
```

Stays on C:\ (under `/home/cabdru/contextgraph/`):

```
crates/                # source tree (25 crates)
docs/                  # design docs (futurebuild/, ruvectorfindings/, ccreality/ historical, mejepasystem/)
config/                # governed paths, model pins, reward specs
configs/               # alternate config tree
scripts/               # cli helpers, hook scripts, systemd unit
tasks/swebench-lite/   # 300 SWE-bench Lite task manifests (KEEP)
memory/                # SMAP shared mind (8 namespaces)
.claude/               # settings.json, agents/*, skills/, loop.md
target/                # cargo build artifacts (reproducible)
```

Ephemeral test scratch stays in `/tmp/` (must wrap in `tempfile::TempDir`).

---

### 7. Storage tier classification

Per `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md §5`. Quota = 115 GB hard cap on `/mnt/d/contextgraph/`.

| Tier | What | Examples | Treatment |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Sacred** | reality-bound; never auto-mutated | Witness chain segments, training certs, gold-standard operator labels | Append-only; Merkle compress > 90 days; survive disasters via backup |
| **Hot** | growing, queried often | `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS`, `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS`, `CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS`, latest shift logs | 8-bit groupwise quantization where applicable; daily-active |
| **Warm** | 30-day window | recent training certs, recent feedback events | 7-bit |
| **Cool** | older but not frozen | aging panels, dormant predictions | 5-bit |
| **Cold** | rarely touched, long-tail | frozen panel slabs, archived shift logs > 30 days | 3-bit groupwise; ttl per CF |
| **Frozen** | provably immutable | constellation cells past freshness check, locked baseline | 1-bit / mark `frozen=true` |
| **Regenerable** | rebuild from source | `target/release/`, `models/` (recoverable via re-download with SHA), corpus from mutation operators | Wipe and rebuild on demand |
| **Ephemeral** | survive one operation | `/tmp/*` test scratch, intermediate computation buffers | Must wrap in `tempfile::TempDir` |

### 8. Exception / error hierarchy

Source: `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §failure_taxonomy` + per-crate `error.rs` files.

#### 8.1 Top-level error namespaces

| Namespace | Where | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `MEJEPA_*` | all ME-JEPA crates | ME-JEPA-specific failures (observe, infer, retrain, calibration, chunker, corpus) |
| `CCREALITY_*` | `context-graph-mcp` | ccreality MCP-tool-layer failures (engine retired, governed-path violations) |
| `CONTEXTGRAPH_*` | `context-graph-core`, `context-graph-storage` | Storage / domain failures |
| `WITNESS_*` | `context-graph-witness` | Witness-chain integrity failures |

#### 8.2 Major specialized error codes

| Code | Trigger | Recovery |
|---|---|---|
| `MEJEPA_OBSERVE_BATCH_NAN` | gradient/activation non-finite during `observe()` | rollback to prior checkpoint; surface to operator |
| `MEJEPA_OBSERVE_DRIFT_DETECTED` | empirical conformal coverage < 0.85 | trigger A/B/C retrain |
| `MEJEPA_OBSERVE_INTEGRITY_VIOLATION` | witness-chain replay broken | halt inference; restore from last verified checkpoint |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_OOD_REFUSE` | `ood_score > 0.5` | refuse to predict; fail-closed |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_DIM_MISMATCH` | panel dimensionality wrong | reject input; do not silently pad or truncate |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_INSTRUMENT_MISSING` | required instrument unavailable | fail-closed; report missing toolchain |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_CONSTELLATION_VIOLATION` | Gτ rejected the predicted panel | persist with `violating_embedders` + `violating_chunks` |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_GTAU_STALE_CONSTELLATION` | constellation > max_age | refuse without operator override |
| `MEJEPA_RETRAIN_HOLDOUT_REGRESSION` | Mode B/C didn't beat Mode A on holdout | do not promote |
| `MEJEPA_RETRAIN_QUOTA_EXCEEDED` | disk/RAM quota hit during retrain | pause; trigger eviction |
| `MEJEPA_CALIBRATION_INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES` | < N samples in calibration window | refuse calibration; fall back to last-known threshold |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_PARSE_FAILED` | tree-sitter ERROR or MISSING node | emit byte-offset + line + node-kind diagnostics; never fall back to text chunking |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_UNSUPPORTED_LANGUAGE` | language not in 11-language matrix | reject fail-closed |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_EMPTY_SOURCE` | empty source | no empty chunks emitted |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_MIXED_LANGUAGE` | embedded language detected (HTML+JS, PHP+HTML) | reject; future work routes per-block |
| `MEJEPA_CORPUS_INVALID_INPUT` | source has parse errors | mutation operators refuse |
| `MEJEPA_CORPUS_OPERATOR_FAILED` | mutation site bounds invalid | refuse |
| `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_DIGEST_MISMATCH` | SHA-256 digest mismatch on model load | refuse to start daemon |
| `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_RETIRED` | call to E5 or E11 | fail-closed; do not return placeholder |
| `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK` | non-zero `target_collapse` in `compute_delta_xi` | critical bug: an instrument is accidentally trainable |
| `MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE` | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/` missing or not writable | daemon refuses to start |
| `MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING` | > 5 supervised-task restarts in 5 min | operator alert; manual cooldown |
| `MEJEPA_SHIFT_SUBSCRIBER_LAG_ALERT` | > 500 unprocessed shifts / 1 min sustained | escalate after 5 min |
| `MEJEPA_FEEDBACK_PREDICTION_NOT_FOUND` | `prediction_id` not in retention window | reject feedback |
| `MEJEPA_FEEDBACK_RATE_LIMITED` | > 100 feedback events / hour / agent | anti-DoS |
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_INVALID_PATCH` | patch could not be parsed | reject |
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_OUT_OF_DISTRIBUTION_HARD` | OOD score exceeds even-prediction-untrustworthy threshold | verdict returned but flagged |
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_GUARD_REJECTED_HARD` | every chunk fails Gτ | alert operator |
| `MEJEPA_DISSIPATION_DETECTED` | training loss falls but holdout doesn't | trigger LoRA refresh + EWC re-anchor |
| `CCREALITY_ENGINE_RETIRED` | `reality_run_attempt` called | retired binary; do not respawn |

### 9. Major subsystem summary

One paragraph each, what each subsystem does + key algorithm + inputs/outputs. Deep dives in [`05`](05_ast_chunker_subsystem.md) – [`21`](21_loop_contract.md).

#### 9.1 AST Chunker (`context-graph-core::memory::ast`)

11-language tree-sitter-driven chunker producing AST-perfect atomic chunks bounded by syntactic entities (function / class / impl / module-decl). Inputs: source text + `Language`. Outputs: `Vec<Chunk { chunk_id, language, kind, byte_range, line_range, sha }>`. Chunk size target ~500 non-whitespace chars per Qodo / cAST methodology. SHA-256 chunk IDs; fails closed on `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_PARSE_FAILED` / `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_MIXED_LANGUAGE`. The reformat-invariant chunk-ID audit is tracked in `[#12]`.

#### 9.2 Embedder Substrate (`context-graph-mejepa-embedders`, `context-graph-embeddings`)

21 declared embedder slots; **13 active for code-domain** (E1, E2, E3, E4, E6, E7, E8, E9, E10, E12, E13, E14, E17); E5 and E11 retired post-2026-05-09. SHA-256 digest verification on every model load; VRAM budget gate; E17 requires calibration cert. **`EmbedderForward` / `PretrainedEmbedderForward` wrappers exist for E1/E6/E7/E8/E10/E12/E13/E14 with fail-closed artifact checks**; remaining work is full per-embedder FSV + cache + production integration ([#3](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/3)). Routing table = 143 rows mapping (Language × EntityType) → embedder set.

#### 9.3 Instruments + Panel (`context-graph-mejepa-instruments`)

15 instrument slots summing to 5,120-d. Each slot is a deterministic, fail-closed encoder from a typed event → fixed-dim subvector (e.g., `E_Oracle` 128-d encodes pass/fail/skip/error fractions + 10-class exception one-hot). `Panel` and `PanelBuilder` enforce: dim-check, finite-check, double-fill rejection, JSON round-trip via `try_from` invariant gate. `PanelStore` persists to `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS`. Linear-probe gate validates panel composition.

#### 9.4 DDA Enrichment + TCT Constellation (`context-graph-mejepa-tct`)

For each chunk × N embedders, produces `n · (N + N²)` signals: N per-embedder cosines-to-centroid + N² pairwise cosines + N² cached pairwise mutual information. For N=13 (active code-domain), that's ~169 signals per chunk; for ~30 chunks per patch, ~5,070 signals per training example. `TctConstellation` holds per-(category × entity × language × embedder) centroids (~13,728 cells max); Bayesian shrinkage when `N < 50`. Gτ guard = deterministic O(N · d_max) test: `cos(embedder_m(predicted), centroid_m) ≥ τ_m` for every m. Failure → `Verdict::GuardRejected` with `violating_embedders` + `violating_chunks`.

#### 9.5 Predictor (`context-graph-mejepa::predictor`)

6-layer transformer trunk (hidden 1024, 8 heads, GeLU, residual + LayerNorm). Inputs: `(panel_t-1, panel_t)` concat = 10,240-d (futurebuild: 8,192-d with action). Output: predicted 5,120-d panel + per-head logits. **Shipped heads**: Panel (VICReg-targeted), Oracle (per-test pass/fail). **Futurebuild Phase B heads**: Failure-mode, Edge-case, Tech-debt, Perf, Security, Accuracy, Cost, Reasoning — each a thin Linear projection on the shared trunk. `FrozenTargetAdapter` reads panel[t=2] from `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS`.

#### 9.6 Training Loop + UTML (`context-graph-mejepa-train`)

AdamW + cosine LR (`lr=5e-4`, `wd=0.04`, batch 64, 10 epochs). `Trainer` orchestrates: `BatchSampler` (with sampling_weight = base × L_step × (1 + 5·operator_override) × (1 + 2·agent_surprise_severity) × decay^age), `Trunk + Heads` forward, multi-loss aggregation (panel + pairwise + action + constellation + anti-pole + uncertainty + invariant + per-head λ_h losses). Every step writes a **TrainingCertificate** with ΔP / ΔK / ΔΩ / ΔΞ + components + grad_norm + drift_coverage + Merkle root, hash-chained via `parent_witness_hash → self_hash` into `CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS`. UTML invariant: `ΔΞ.target_collapse == 0` enforced via `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK`. `compute_delta_xi` is multiplicative non-collapse. PairwiseMI auditor + dormant-unit detector + state-transfer T detector all shipped (REQ-UTML-06/07/08).

#### 9.7 Inference (`context-graph-mejepa::compiler`)

`MeJepaCompiler::{compile, verify, observe, compile_with_panel}`. `verify(patch, context)` → `Verdict { Pass, Fail, OutOfDistribution, Abstain, GuardRejected }`. Forward path: chunk → embed → DDA signals → Panel → predictor → conformal interval wrap → Gτ guard → assemble `RealityPrediction`. Conformal: SplitConformal with target 90% coverage; OOD via reconstruction-error / σ²; threshold 0.5. Live latency p50 < 100ms (cached instruments). MCP surface: `mejepa_verify`.

#### 9.8 Self-Healing Scheduler (`context-graph-mejepa::heal`)

18-module tree: `drift`, `ewc`, `promote`, `full_retrain`, `integrity`, `lora_refresh`, `calibration`, `distill`, `regulate`, `pipeline`, `drill`, `store`, `cf`, `fsv`, `scheduler`, `scheduler_state`. Long-running tokio scheduler runs 7 tickers (250ms observe / 60s drift / 600s active-learning / 3600s promote / 1800s continual_backprop / 3600s constellation_freshness / 600s emergency_eviction). Drift classes: Soft (88-85%), Hard (85-80%), Catastrophic (<80%). A/B/C promotion gate: `score = holdout_correlation - 0.05 · inference_latency_multiplier`. EWC++ Fisher with per-head matrices (futurebuild). Phase A scheduler skeleton shipped; full autonomous policy is Phase E.

#### 9.9 Shift Subscriber (`context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber`)

Tokio task tailing `cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl`. `ShiftLogTail` cursor-based; `WatermarkWriter` persists last-consumed offset per session to `CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK`. `InstrumentCache` keyed by `(slot, file_sha)`. `ShiftSubscriber::{run_until_idle, run_until_shutdown}` drives `shift_to_inference → observe → prediction`. Latency: p50 < 5s, p99 < 30s. Lag alert > 500 unprocessed shifts / 1 min. Three MCP tools: `mejepa_observe_shift`, `mejepa_subscriber_status`, `mejepa_capture_audit`.

#### 9.10 Hygiene (`context-graph-mejepa-hygiene`)

Tier transitions (`tier_for_score(access_count_30d) → Hot/Warm/Cool/Cold/Frozen`), quota + emergency eviction (115 GB hard cap), witness Merkle compression of segments > 90 days, nightly GC orchestrator. 3 MCP tools (`mejepa_gc_run`, `mejepa_quota_status`, `mejepa_witness_compress`) + `mcp.rs` dispatch. JSON codec for the GC report. Path discipline enforced via `context-graph-paths` (no `/tmp`, no `$HOME`, no C-drive fallback).

#### 9.11 Phase 0 Corpus (`context-graph-mejepa-corpus`)

8 Python mutation operators: `KnownGood`, `SubtleFlip`, `OffByOne`, `SwapVariable`, `DeleteTestCall`, `WrongFile`, `OverEngineer`, `CompileError`. Pure-Rust, deterministic (SplitMix64 PRNG), fail-closed. Includes `python_lex.rs` (triple-quote / f-string-aware Python masker). `swebench.rs` invokes `python -m swebench.harness.run_evaluation` for single-instance grading; parses per-instance `report.json` → `OracleVerdict`. Docker-name-safe run_id validator. Repo-atomic + Instance-atomic split via `split.rs` (Stoer-Wagner mincut + LPT pre-sort + bin-pack). 10 non-Python languages pending (Phase C).

#### 9.12 Witness Chain (`context-graph-witness`)

Clean-room reimplementation: 73-byte SHAKE-256 chain entry, ed25519 segment signing (feature-gated `ed25519`), Stage 0 / Stage F / Merkle compression certificates. Every panel, training cert, prediction, feedback event binds to a `witness_hash`. `optimizer_witness_chain_verify` / `_diff` / `_repair_legacy` for integrity.

#### 9.13 Solver Primitives (`context-graph-solver`, `context-graph-mincut`, `context-graph-tier-compression`)

`solver`: MMR (top-k diversified retrieval), conjugate gradient, PageRank, conformal predictor, Granger F-test (OLS + Lentz-method incomplete beta + Lanczos lgamma), causal masking. `mincut`: Stoer-Wagner global min-cut (O(V³) dense) for repo-atomic / A-B split with min cross-set edge weight. `tier-compression`: 8/7/5/3-bit groupwise linear scalar quantization with magic+version header (compression ratios 3.89× / 4.43× / 6.12× / 9.91×).

#### 9.14 Storage Layer (`context-graph-storage`)

RocksDB with 101 column families total, including the 28+ ME-JEPA CFs. Centralized CF registry in `context-graph-mejepa-cf` (dependency-free) so runtime / hygiene / MCP / train share names without cycles. `column_families.rs` enumerates all CFs; `indexes.rs` manages secondary indexes; `graph_edges/` + `code/` + `dynamicjepa/` + `teleological/` sub-modules.

#### 9.15 MCP Server (`context-graph-mcp`)

JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio. `handlers/tools/` houses every dispatcher; `protocol.rs` owns the JSON-RPC wire format. `daemon.rs` + `daemon_validate.rs` host the supervisor for daemon mode. Singleton flock at `~/.cache/cgreality/reality-loop-stdio.lock` evicts stale instances on `/mcp reconnect`. Path-policy gate **removed 2026-05-07**; path discipline is behavioral per `CLAUDE.md §8`.

#### 9.16 CLI (`context-graph-cli`)

15 top-level commands; ~150 sub-subcommands. Three binaries: `context-graph-cli` (main), `context-graph` (symlink alias), `blackbox` (BlackBoxFix ledger). Notable: `dynamicjepa` group (44 ops covering domain packs, panels, trajectories, datasets, training, prediction, constellation, MI audit, cross-domain transfer); `hooks` group (Claude Code hook entrypoints, one per hook type, each timeout-bounded); `warmup` (preload 14 embedding models in 20-30s on RTX 5090).

#### 9.17 Hooks Layer (`.claude/settings.json` + `scripts/cc/hook_*.sh`)

6 hook types: `SessionStart` (active runtime root injection), `UserPromptSubmit` (last 3 shifts + latest `RealityPrediction` markdown), `PreToolUse` Edit/Write (pre-edit SHA), `PostToolUse` Edit/Write (after_sha + cargo check + shift-log append), `Stop` (`optimizer_record_recommendation` required), `PreCompact` (SMAP narrative dump). Hook scripts shell out to `context-graph-cli hooks <subcommand>`. Path-policy gate has been REMOVED; the pre-edit hook records SHAs for provenance only.

#### 9.18 Daemon Supervisor (`context-graph-mcp::daemon`)

`run_supervised_tasks(tasks: Vec<SupervisedTask>) -> Result<()>` using `tokio::JoinSet`. Each task has exponential backoff (cap 60s), 5-restart-in-5-min flap detection → `MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING` alert, watch-channel shutdown signal. Supervised tasks: shift subscriber, self-optimization scheduler, nightly GC scheduler (cron-driven at 03:00 local), weekly evaluation scheduler. D-root validator at startup → fails closed with `MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE`.

#### 9.19 Eval Harness (`context-graph-mejepa::eval`)

`EvalRunner::run_holdout_eval` + `metrics.rs` (Pearson correlation per cell, conformal health, OOD AUC, Gτ pass rate, Bayesian shrinkage), ship-gate failure analysis, `ActiveLearningQueueState`, `PatchSimilarityGraph`, weekly report writer, `RocksDbEvalStore` with 9 dedicated CFs. 4 MCP tools (`mejepa_eval_run`, `mejepa_ship_gate_status`, `mejepa_active_learning_queue`, `mejepa_eval_build_graph`). `phase8_eval_fsv` writes 17 evidence files.

#### 9.20 SMAP Shared Mind (`./memory/`)

8-namespace tree (`context/`, `decisions/`, `discoveries/`, `handoff/`, `journal/`, `blockers/`, `patterns/`, `reports/`). Every `.md` file requires YAML frontmatter (`namespace`, `agent_id`, `timestamp`, `status`, `tags`). Per CLAUDE.md §6: resolved blockers stay (`status: resolved`); superseded decisions stay (`status: superseded`); reports are append-only. Lifecycle every agent follows: READ → ORIENT → WORK → WRITE → REPORT.

---

### 10. What is NOT covered

- **Web UI dashboard**: out of scope per `docs/futurebuild/17 §O1` (CLI + weekly markdown reports cover operator needs).
- **Multi-region / cloud deployment**: local single-machine is the design point; cloud is future work.
- **Federated training across operators**: requires multi-org consent; deferred.
- **Concrete ME-JEPA-Voice / -Image implementations**: template documented in [`28_future_domains_mejepa_x.md`](28_future_domains_mejepa_x.md); concrete builds wait for operator priority.
- **Training the underlying embedders**: embedders are frozen by design (constitution `<anti_patterns>`); fine-tuning is an explicit future-domain choice.
- **LLM-augmented label generation**: per the no-inner-LLM rule; only deterministic / analyzer-based labels.
- **Real-time web/Slack integration**: CLI + weekly report sufficient for v1.

---

### 11. Falsifiable end-state commitments

Source: `docs/futurebuild/01_VISION_TRUTH_SYSTEM.md §8`.

| Commitment | Falsifier |
|---|---|
| `prediction_oracle_correlation ≥ 0.95` stable over 4 consecutive rolling windows on 300×8 SWE-bench Lite | any window drops < 0.92 without coincident known incident |
| Per-language conformal coverage stays within 88–92% of stated 90% ± 2% | any language exceeds ±5% deviation for two consecutive cycles |
| OOD AUC ≥ 0.85 between in-distribution and synthetic OOD | any week < 0.80 |
| Subscriber p50 < 5s, p99 < 30s under production traffic | two weeks at p99 ≥ 60s |
| Self-healing detects soft drift within 1,000 observations and promotes mode-winner without operator intervention | operator forced to manually trigger A/B/C in any non-catastrophic case |
| Disk footprint ≤ 110 GB under nightly GC with 115 GB hard quota | quota exceeded > 24h without emergency eviction |
| Inner-LLM residue absent from build surface (no llama-cpp-2, no Qwen pinning, no Mode B drip) | any commit reintroduces these without explicit operator override |

### 4. Module dependency graph (high-level)

Direction: `crate_a → crate_b` means a depends on b.

```
context-graph-mcp
  ├─→ context-graph-cli (sometimes; for blackbox dispatch)
  ├─→ context-graph-core (TeleologicalMemoryStore, traits)
  ├─→ context-graph-storage (RocksDB access)
  ├─→ context-graph-embeddings (E1-E14)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa (compiler, eval, heal)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-tct (constellation_inspect)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-hygiene (gc, quota, witness_compress)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber (Phase 7 tools)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf (canonical names)
  ├─→ context-graph-witness (chain verify/diff/repair)
  └─→ context-graph-paths (D-root resolver)

context-graph-mejepa
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-train (Trainer, cert, sampler)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-instruments (Panel, instruments)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-tct (Gτ guard, GuardRejected)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-embedders (routing, forward)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-hygiene (CF storage opens)
  ├─→ context-graph-embeddings (E1-E14 backbones)
  ├─→ context-graph-solver (mmr, conformal)
  ├─→ context-graph-witness (witness_hash on every persisted)
  ├─→ context-graph-storage (RocksDB)
  └─→ context-graph-cuda (predictor forward)

context-graph-mejepa-train
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-instruments (panel construction)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-embedders (routing)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-tct (constellation loss)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf
  ├─→ context-graph-witness (training cert chain)
  └─→ context-graph-storage

context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa (compiler::observe)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-instruments (panel build)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-embedders
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf (CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK)
  └─→ context-graph-storage

context-graph-mejepa-corpus
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf (CF_MEJEPA_MUTATION_CORPUS, CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS)
  ├─→ context-graph-mincut (Stoer-Wagner for split)
  ├─→ context-graph-paths (D-corpus root enforcement)
  ├─→ context-graph-witness (corpus provenance)
  └─→ context-graph-storage

context-graph-mejepa-tct
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-embedders
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf (CF_MEJEPA_CONSTELLATION)
  ├─→ context-graph-witness
  └─→ context-graph-storage

context-graph-mejepa-hygiene
  ├─→ context-graph-tier-compression
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf
  ├─→ context-graph-witness (witness_compress)
  ├─→ context-graph-paths
  └─→ context-graph-storage

context-graph-mejepa-instruments
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-embedders (EmbedderBackedInstrumentHead)
  ├─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf
  └─→ context-graph-witness

context-graph-cli
  ├─→ context-graph-mcp (mcp_client)
  ├─→ context-graph-core
  ├─→ context-graph-embeddings (warmup)
  ├─→ context-graph-storage
  ├─→ context-graph-dynamicjepa
  └─→ context-graph-mejepa-cf

context-graph-core
  ├─→ context-graph-cuda (clustering)
  ├─→ context-graph-embeddings
  ├─→ context-graph-storage
  └─→ context-graph-witness

context-graph-embeddings
  ├─→ context-graph-cuda
  ├─→ context-graph-core (traits, types)
  └─→ context-graph-storage

context-graph-storage
  └─→ (no internal deps beyond serde/rocksdb)

context-graph-mejepa-cf
  └─→ (NO INTERNAL DEPS — dependency-free by design)

context-graph-mincut, context-graph-solver, context-graph-tier-compression, context-graph-paths, context-graph-witness, context-graph-cuda
  └─→ (foundation crates; only external deps)
```

The dependency-free `context-graph-mejepa-cf` is the keystone preventing CF-name drift across crates without creating cycles.

---

### 5. Entry-point trace (MCP daemon mode)

```
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mcp -- --daemon-server-only --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph
   │
   ▼
crates/context-graph-mcp/src/main.rs::main()
   │
   ▼
parse args (--daemon-server-only, --d-root, --daemon-port 3100, --http-port 3101)
   │
   ▼
crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon_validate.rs::validate_d_drive_root()
   │   (fails closed MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE if /mnt/d/contextgraph missing or unwritable)
   ▼
flock(~/.cache/cgreality/reality-loop-stdio.lock) — singleton enforcement
   │   (SIGTERM(2s) → SIGKILL evicts stale prior MCP)
   ▼
open RocksDB at /mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/contextgraph-rocksdb/ with all 101 CFs registered
   │
   ▼
crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon.rs::run_supervised_tasks([
   ShiftSubscriberTask,                # context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber
   SelfOptimizationSchedulerTask,      # context-graph-mejepa::heal::scheduler
   NightlyGcSchedulerTask,             # context-graph-mejepa-hygiene::gc
   WeeklyEvalSchedulerTask,            # context-graph-mejepa::eval::runner
])
   │   (tokio::JoinSet supervisor; exponential backoff; flap detection > 5 restarts/5min → MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING)
   ▼
serve JSON-RPC 2.0 over stdio (or daemon-port for daemon mode)
   │
   ▼
handlers/tools/dispatch.rs — 206 tools registered
```

---

### 6. Entry-point trace (CLI `context-graph-cli`)

```
context-graph-cli <subcommand>
   │
   ▼
crates/context-graph-cli/src/main.rs::main() — global flags (-v/--verbose), Cli enum
   │
   ▼
match Cli::Subcommand to crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/<subcommand>.rs::handle()
   │
   ▼
each subcommand opens its own resources (storage if needed, MCP client if needed)
```

---

### 7. Entry-point trace (one Edit shift, end-to-end)

```
Claude Code calls Edit
   │
   ▼ (PreToolUse hook fires)
.claude/settings.json::hooks.PreToolUse  →  context-graph-cli hooks pre-tool
   │   (records pre-edit SHA via crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/pre_tool.rs)
   ▼
Edit returns
   │
   ▼ (PostToolUse hook fires)
.claude/settings.json::hooks.PostToolUse  →  context-graph-cli hooks post-tool
   │   (records after_sha, runs cargo check, appends shift-log JSONL entry at
   │    /mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl)
   ▼
~250ms later, daemon's shift subscriber consumes the line
   │
   ▼
crates/context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber/src/runner.rs::run_until_idle()
   ├─→ ShiftLogTail::poll_next_line()
   ├─→ ShiftSubscriber::open() reconstructs from CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK
   └─→ for each shift:
       ├─→ memory::ast::chunk_with_options(file, language, opts) → Vec<Chunk>
       ├─→ for each chunk × embedder in routing: EmbedderForward::forward(chunk)
       ├─→ compute DDA signals → CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS
       ├─→ PanelBuilder::build() → Panel(5,120-d) → CF_MEJEPA_PANELS
       ├─→ shift_to_inference():
       │   ├─→ MeJepaCompiler::verify(panel_t-1, action, panel_t)
       │   ├─→ predictor forward → predicted_panel + per-head outputs
       │   ├─→ ConformalPredictor::wrap() → interval
       │   ├─→ Gτ guard via mejepa_tct::gtau::gtau_check_chunks()
       │   └─→ assemble RealityPrediction → CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS
       └─→ update CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK
   │
   ▼ (on agent's next prompt)
.claude/settings.json::hooks.UserPromptSubmit  →  (futurebuild) prediction-injection hook
   │   reads CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS, renders markdown, injects into agent context
   ▼
agent sees the prediction; may call mejepa_record_agent_feedback if disagrees
   │
   ▼ (when tests run)
PostToolUse on Bash → test-outcome capture hook → CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS
   │
   ▼ (scheduler's next 250ms tick)
crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/heal/scheduler.rs::run_self_optimization_scheduler
   ├─→ observe_tick: drain pending oracle outcomes → MeJepaCompiler::observe()
   │   ├─→ EWC++ regularized SGD step
   │   ├─→ compute ΔP/ΔK/ΔΩ/ΔΞ → L_step
   │   ├─→ write TrainingCertificate → CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS (witness-chain bound)
   │   └─→ update BatchSampler weights
   ├─→ drift_tick: classify Soft/Hard/Catastrophic
   ├─→ active_learning_tick: drain queue
   ├─→ promote_tick: A/B/C if pending
   ├─→ continual_backprop_tick: re-init dormant units
   ├─→ constellation_freshness_tick: refit stale cells
   └─→ emergency_eviction_tick: quota check
```

---

### 8. Build / package configuration

#### 8.1 Workspace manifest (`Cargo.toml`)

Lists all 25 member crates; workspace-wide profile defaults (`release` with LTO + codegen-units=1 for the predictor crates).

#### 8.2 Build targets

| Target | Command | Output |
|---|---|---|
| Full workspace | `cargo build --release --workspace` | every binary; ~5-10 min on cold cache |
| MCP server | `cargo build --release -p context-graph-mcp` | `target/release/context-graph-mcp` |
| CLI | `cargo build --release -p context-graph-cli` | `target/release/context-graph-cli`, `target/release/context-graph` (symlink), `target/release/blackbox` |
| Corpus CLI | `cargo build --release -p context-graph-mejepa-corpus` | `target/release/mejepa-corpus` |
| Training driver | `cargo build --release -p context-graph-mejepa-train` | `target/release/mejepa-train` |
| Heal drill | `cargo build --release -p context-graph-mejepa` (bin) | `target/release/mejepa-heal-drill` |
| Verify CLI | `cargo build --release -p context-graph-mejepa --bin mejepa-verify` | `target/release/mejepa-verify` |

#### 8.3 Feature flags

| Flag | Crate | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `ed25519` | `context-graph-witness` | Enable ed25519 segment signing |
| `cuda` | `context-graph-cuda` | Enable real CUDA path (default off uses stub) |
| `e11_entity` | `context-graph-mcp` | Enable E11 entity tools (retired post-2026-05-09) |
| `e5_causal` | `context-graph-mcp` | Enable E5 causal tools (retired post-2026-05-09) |

#### 8.4 What gets installed

- Binaries land under `target/release/`. Operator installs via `cp ... ~/.local/bin/` or via the systemd unit's `ExecStart` pointing at the workspace target.
- Models: NOT bundled. Operator places under `/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/<embedder>/` with SHA-pinned digests per `models_config.toml`.
- Hooks: written by `context-graph-cli setup --generate-hooks` to `.claude/settings.json` + `.claude/hooks/*.sh`.
- Systemd unit: `scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service` copied to `~/.config/systemd/user/`.

#### 8.5 Reproducibility

- Rust toolchain pinned: `rust-toolchain.toml` (workspace root).
- Cargo.lock committed; deterministic dep graph.
- Tree-sitter grammars version-pinned at 0.23.x.
- Witness chain entries are byte-deterministic; same code + same model versions = byte-equal predictions.
- Phase 0 corpus is fully deterministic via SplitMix64 PRNG seed.

Source files covered: `Cargo.toml` (workspace), `rust-toolchain.toml`, `config/`, `configs/`, `.claude/settings.json`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/config.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon_validate.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/quota.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/config.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/config.rs`, `scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service`, `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`, `docs/futurebuild/19_OPERATOR_RUNBOOK.md`.

### 1. Where configuration lives

| Surface | File / location | Format | Loaded by | Lifecycle |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Workspace toolchain | `rust-toolchain.toml` | TOML | rustup | per-build |
| Cargo workspace | `Cargo.toml` (root) | TOML | cargo | per-build |
| Per-crate features | `crates/*/Cargo.toml` | TOML | cargo | per-build |
| MCP daemon args | CLI flags + env | argv | `context-graph-mcp::main` | startup |
| Daemon TOML | `~/.config/mejepa/daemon.toml` (futurebuild) | TOML | daemon at startup | hot-reload not yet — restart required |
| Path policy / governance | `config/governed_paths.json` (mostly gutted post-2026-05-07) | JSON | MCP server | startup |
| Embedder routing / digests | `config/models_config.toml` | TOML | `context-graph-mejepa-embedders` | startup |
| Model file location | `/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/<embedder_id>/` | filesystem | embedder loaders | first-use lazy load |
| Embedder calibration | `data/utl_calibration/` (E17 + others) | binary fixtures | embedder loaders | first-use |
| Reward specs (RETIRED inner-loop) | `crates/*/src/reward_specs/**` | code | `crates/context-graph-benchmark` | build-time |
| Hook config | `.claude/settings.json` | JSON | Claude Code | session-start |
| Hook scripts | `scripts/cc/*.sh` | bash | Claude Code via settings.json | per-event |
| systemd unit | `scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service` | systemd | `systemctl --user enable --now` | host startup |
| BlackBoxFix ledger | `~/.blackbox/` | SQLite + JSON | `context-graph-cli blackbox` | per-call |
| Active runtime root pointer | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/state/cgreality/active_runtime_root` | filesystem (single file) | SessionStart hook | session start |

### 5. Hook configuration (`.claude/settings.json`)

Source: `.claude/settings.json` + `scripts/cc/hook_*.sh`.

#### 5.1 Hook timeouts

| Hook | Timeout | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `SessionStart` | 5,000 ms | Inject active runtime root |
| `PreToolUse` (Edit/Write) | 500 ms (FAST PATH — no DB) | Record pre-edit SHA |
| `PostToolUse` (Edit/Write) | 3,000 ms | Record after_sha + cargo check + shift-log append |
| `UserPromptSubmit` | 2,000 ms | Inject latest `RealityPrediction` markdown |
| `SessionEnd` | 30,000 ms | Final summary generation |
| `PreCompact` | 20,000 ms | SMAP narrative dump |
| `TaskCompleted` | 20,000 ms | Learnings extraction |
| `Stop` | (synchronous) | Require `optimizer_record_recommendation` |

#### 5.2 Hook script paths (project-relative)

```
scripts/cc/hook_session_start.sh
scripts/cc/hook_pre_tool_use_edit.sh        # records pre-edit SHA; gate REMOVED 2026-05-07
scripts/cc/hook_post_tool_use_edit.sh       # runs cargo check, appends shift-log
scripts/cc/hook_user_prompt_submit.sh       # injects last 3 shifts + (futurebuild) latest RealityPrediction
scripts/cc/hook_stop.sh                     # validates optimizer_record_recommendation was called
scripts/cc/hook_pre_compact.sh              # SMAP narrative dump
```

Each shell script invokes `context-graph-cli hooks <subcommand>` per `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/args.rs:325-372`.

| E6 | (sparse codebook) | 0 |
| E7 | `Qodo/Qodo-Embed-1-1.5B.gguf` | 1.5 GB |
| E8 | (adapter on E7) | <1 GB |
| E9 | (HDC codebook) | <0.1 GB |
| E10 | `intfloat/multilingual-e5-large.gguf` | 0.7 GB |
| E12 | `colbert-ir/colbertv2.0/` | 0.5 GB |
| E13 | `naver/splade-v3/` | 0.4 GB |

| E17 | `cardiffnlp/twitter-roberta-base-sentiment-latest/` | 0.5 GB |
| E18, E20 | (small adapters) | <0.2 GB |

### 7. Config validation rules

| Rule | Source | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| D-root must exist and be writable | `daemon_validate.rs` | Refuse start with `MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE` |
| All required subdirs auto-created on first start | `daemon_validate.rs` | `mkdir -p` for each path in §5 of `16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md` |
| Embedder digests must match `models_config.toml` | `context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/digest.rs` | Refuse load with `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_DIGEST_MISMATCH` |
| E17 requires calibration cert | `context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/calibration.rs` | Lazy-load rejects |
| VRAM budget gate | `context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/vram.rs` | Refuse start over budget; `mejepa_vram_budget_report` MCP tool |
| Constellation freshness ≤ 90 days | `context-graph-mejepa-tct/src/freshness.rs` | Refuse without operator override (`MEJEPA_INFER_GTAU_STALE_CONSTELLATION`) |
| Panel dimensionality must equal 5,120 | `context-graph-mejepa-instruments/src/panel_json.rs` | Reject with `MEJEPA_INFER_DIM_MISMATCH` |
| All floats finite | per-instrument encoders | Reject with namespace-specific errors |
| Corpus output path must be under `<d-root>/corpus/` | `context-graph-mejepa-corpus/src/generate.rs` | Reject `/tmp` + C-drive paths |
| FSV evidence path must be under `<d-root>/fsv/` | `crates/*/examples/*_fsv.rs` (per `TASK-OBS-006`) | Lint enforces |
| C-drive paths in code forbidden | CI lint `TASK-OBS-005` | grep-asserts no `/home/`, `~/.cache/`, `/tmp/contextgraph-` |

---

### 8. Configuration loading order

Source: `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/main.rs` + `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/config.rs`.

```
1. Hardcoded defaults (each crate's config.rs)
2. (futurebuild) Daemon TOML at ~/.config/mejepa/daemon.toml — overrides defaults
3. CLI flags — override TOML
4. Environment variables — override CLI flags for specific keys (CG_MEJEPA_DISK_QUOTA_GB, RUST_LOG)
5. SHA-pinned model digests (models_config.toml) — REJECT on mismatch, no override
6. Constellation max-age — REJECT on stale unless --force flag from operator
```

No silent fallback. Every load is fail-closed with a structured error.

---

### 9. systemd unit

Source: `scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service`.

```ini
[Unit]
Description=ME-JEPA-Code daemon (MCP server + supervised tokio tasks)
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStart=/home/cabdru/contextgraph/target/release/context-graph-mcp \
    --daemon-server-only \
    --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph \
    --daemon-port 3100 \
    --http-port 3101
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10s
StandardOutput=append:/mnt/d/contextgraph/logs/mejepa-daemon.stdout.log
StandardError=append:/mnt/d/contextgraph/logs/mejepa-daemon.stderr.log

# Resource limits
MemoryMax=64G
TasksMax=infinity

[Install]
WantedBy=default.target
```

Operator command:

```bash
cp scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service ~/.config/systemd/user/
systemctl --user daemon-reload
systemctl --user enable --now mejepa-daemon
systemctl --user status mejepa-daemon
```

---

### 10. What configuration does NOT control

- **Frozen instruments**: per-instrument output is fully determined by the typed-event input + the constitution. No knob can alter `E_Oracle`'s output format, `E_Witness`'s sequence transformer, etc.
- **Frozen embedder weights**: model files are SHA-pinned. Training does not modify them.
- **Constellation cells once constructed**: requires operator force-refit; no automatic mutation.
- **Witness chain**: append-only; signing keys live in operator keyring; no config controls integrity.
- **The 8 mutation categories**: hardcoded enum; cannot be configured away.
- **Mode A / Mode B switching**: retired post-pivot; no mode flag.


<!-- ===== END: 03_configuration.md ===== -->

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 04_database_schema.md (sha256:2c5e1a3e8d9f5b0eaa466e6d0f57591dc2c997610cfd2fedea7a9c724b0dc1c4, 409 lines) ===== -->

## 04 — Database Schema (RocksDB Column Families)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-storage/src/column_families.rs`, `crates/context-graph-storage/src/lib.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-cf/src/lib.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/storage.rs`, `crates/context-graph-storage/src/indexes.rs`, `crates/context-graph-storage/src/teleological/`, `crates/context-graph-storage/src/dynamicjepa/`, `crates/context-graph-storage/src/graph_edges/`, `crates/context-graph-storage/src/code/`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/eval/store.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber/src/watermark.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-tct/src/store.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/03_ARCHITECTURE_BLUEPRINT.md §5`, `docs/futurebuild/07_TRAINING_DATA_FLYWHEEL.md §9`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/_README.md`.

> **Total column families: 101** across the workspace (28+ ME-JEPA-specific). The canonical CF name registry lives in `crates/context-graph-mejepa-cf/src/lib.rs` (dependency-free crate). Other CFs are declared in `crates/context-graph-storage/src/column_families.rs`.

---

### 1. Storage backend

| Property | Value |
|---|---|
| Engine | RocksDB 0.21.x |
| Hybrid backend | RocksDB + AgentDB per `CLAUDE.md §4` |
| HNSW indexes | enabled (15 indexes: E1 + E1Matryoshka128 + E2/E3/E4 + E5+E5Cause+E5Effect (retired) + E7 + E8 + E9 + E10 + E10Multimodal + E11(retired) + E14) |
| Path | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/contextgraph-rocksdb/` (per `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`) |
| Tier compression | hot 8-bit / warm 7-bit / cool 5-bit / cold 3-bit / frozen 1-bit per `context-graph-tier-compression` |
| Per-CF access | direct via `db.cf_handle(name).ok_or(...)` — fail-closed on missing CF |
| Quota | 115 GB hard cap on entire `/mnt/d/contextgraph/` |
| Permissions | `0600` on persisted JSON files |

---

### 2. Connection management

Source: `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/main.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/storage.rs::open_hygiene_rocksdb`.

```rust
// Pseudocode of daemon startup
let opts = rocksdb::Options::default();
opts.create_if_missing(true);
opts.create_missing_column_families(true);

let cf_descriptors: Vec<ColumnFamilyDescriptor> = ALL_CF_NAMES
    .iter()
    .map(|name| ColumnFamilyDescriptor::new(*name, rocksdb::Options::default()))
    .collect();

let db = rocksdb::DB::open_cf_descriptors(&opts, path, cf_descriptors)?;
```

**No CF auto-create at runtime**. Schema is fully known at compile-time via the registry crate. RocksDB requires ALL CFs in the on-disk store to be opened on every connection — adding a CF requires a workspace-wide rebuild AND a one-time first-start that creates the new CF.

**No connection pooling**: RocksDB DB is wrapped in `Arc<rocksdb::DB>` and shared across tokio tasks. Per-CF write batches via `WriteBatch` for atomic multi-CF writes.

---

### 3. Schema versioning strategy

Per `CLAUDE.md` + `memory/feedback_no_backwards_compat.md`:

- **Pre-production**: synthetic data only. **Wipe DB on schema change.** No migration shims.
- **Schema changes are tracked** via the `EMBEDDING_REGISTRY` CF (per-fingerprint model-version tags) so stale embeddings can be re-embedded.
- **`provenance.predictor_version`** on every `RealityPrediction` and `TrainingCertificate` enables version-aware downstream consumption.
- **Post-1.0**: explicit migration tools required (`TASK-API-002` in futurebuild Phase F).

---

### 5. Indexes

Source: `crates/context-graph-storage/src/indexes.rs`.

#### 5.1 HNSW vector indexes (15)

Each HNSW index is stored separately as a `.hnsw` file alongside RocksDB:

| Index | Dim | M (graph degree) | efConstruction | efSearch (default) | MAX_VECTORS |

| E1 | 1024 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2,000,000 |
| E1Matryoshka128 | 128 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2,000,000 |
| E2 (sinusoidal) | 512 | 16 | 100 | 30 | 2M |
| E3 (recurrence) | 512 | 16 | 100 | 30 | 2M |
| E4 (session) | 512 | 16 | 100 | 30 | 2M |
| E5CausalCause (retired) | 768 | 32 | 200 | 50 | — |
| E5CausalEffect (retired) | 768 | 32 | 200 | 50 | — |
| E7 (Qodo code) | 1536 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2M |
| E8 (graph) | 1024 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2M |
| E9 (HDC) | 1024 | 16 | 100 | 30 | 2M |
| E10 (paraphrase) | 1024 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2M |
| E10MultimodalParaphrase | 1024 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2M |
| E10MultimodalContext | 1024 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2M |
| E11 (retired) | 768 | 32 | 200 | 50 | — |
| E14 (BGE-M3 / CodeT5+) | 1536 | 32 | 200 | 50 | 2M |

| Index CF | Key | Maps to |

| `CF_TYPED_EDGE_SECONDARY_INDEX` | `[type(1)][source(16)][target(16)]` | edges (multi-edge-safe; 33-byte key prevents collisions when same source has multiple same-type edges) |
| `CF_TOOL_CALL_INDEX` | `tool_use_id` | memory list |
| `CF_FINGERPRINT_INDEX` | misc secondary keys | UUIDs |

### 6. Special features

#### 6.1 Tier compression (per CF policy)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/tier.rs`.

| Tier | Bits | Compression ratio | Cosine retention (768-d Gaussian) | Trigger |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hot | f32 raw | 1× | 1.000 | `access_count_30d > 100` |
| Warm | 8-bit groupwise | 3.89× | 0.99999 | `30 < access_count_30d ≤ 100` |
| Cool | 5-bit groupwise | 6.12× | 0.99960 | `5 < access_count_30d ≤ 30` |
| Cold | 3-bit groupwise | 9.91× | 0.99192 | `access_count_30d ≤ 5` |
| Frozen | 1-bit or marked | n/a | n/a | constellation cells past freshness; locked baselines |

Header: 22 bytes (magic + version + min/max/scale per group). `decode_from_tier` reverses; round-trip verified in FSV. Source: `crates/context-graph-tier-compression/src/lib.rs`.

#### 6.2 Witness chain (append-only)

Every persistent artifact (panel, training cert, prediction, feedback event) binds a `witness_hash`. Per `crates/context-graph-witness/`:

- 73-byte SHAKE-256 chain entries
- Ed25519 segment signing (feature-gated `ed25519`)
- Stage 0 / Stage F certificates
- Merkle compression for chains > 90 days
- Hash chain: `entry_hash_n+1 = SHAKE-256(entry_hash_n || payload_n+1)`

#### 6.3 Source-of-truth metadata

Every JSON in the storage tree includes:

```json
{
  "source_of_truth": "file:/mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/.../entry.json",
  "sha256": "<sha>",
  "ts": "<iso8601>",
  "witness_hash": "shake256:73:<hex>",
  "predictor_version": "v0.4.2-bf16-cuda13.2",
  "code_version": "<git-sha>"
}
```

Error messages and recommendations always begin path references with `file:` (or `sqlite:` / `git:`).

#### 6.4 Path-policy gate (REMOVED 2026-05-07)

`crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/reality_loop/path_policy.rs::ensure_path_allowed` is gutted: it now rejects ONLY empty paths or paths containing control characters. The deny-list (which previously blocked writes to many paths) is GONE. Path discipline is now **behavioral** per `CLAUDE.md §8` — the operator trusts the agent not to write to `.env`, `swebench/**`, `tests/oracle_**/**`, etc.

---

### 7. Query helper functions

Source: `crates/context-graph-storage/src/lib.rs`.

| Function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `get_memory_by_uuid(db, uuid) -> Option<TeleologicalFingerprint>` | Primary key lookup |
| `iter_cf_prefix(db, cf, prefix) -> Iter` | Prefix scan |
| `batch_write_atomic(db, batch: WriteBatch)` | Multi-CF atomic write |
| `cf_count(db, cf) -> u64` | Per-CF row count |
| `cf_handle(db, cf_name) -> ColumnFamily` | Fail-closed CF handle resolver |
| `cf_iter_decoded::<T: DeserializeOwned>(db, cf) -> impl Iterator<Item=Result<T>>` | Type-safe decoding |

Per ME-JEPA CF lifecycle:

| Function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `open_hygiene_rocksdb(d_root) -> rocksdb::DB` | Daemon's standard RocksDB open with all CFs |
| `persist_panel(db, panel)` | Write Panel + meta + DDA signals atomically |
| `read_latest_prediction(db, session_id) -> Option<RealityPrediction>` | Per-session latest |
| `enqueue_active_learning(db, entry)` | Priority-queue enqueue |
| `record_agent_feedback(db, surprise_event)` | Multi-CF write: feedback + sampling-weight bump + queue entry |

---

### 8. Migrations (when needed)

Per `CLAUDE.md` pre-production: **wipe DB on schema change**. Post-1.0 (`TASK-API-002` in `docs/futurebuild/24_API_STABILITY.md`):

| Migration class | Algorithm |
|---|---|
| New CF added | Auto-created via `create_missing_column_families=true` on first start |
| CF dropped | Remove from registry crate; old data orphaned (RocksDB tolerates extra CFs on disk if `error_if_exists=false`) |
| CF schema change (key or value layout) | Wipe DB; document in `memory/decisions/<date>--<schema-change>.md` per CLAUDE.md §10.3 |
| Embedder version change | Re-embed via `CF_MEJEPA_EMBEDDING_REGISTRY` stale-detection + `backfill-e14` style backfill |
| Constellation rebuild | Force-refit via `mejepa_constellation_inspect --force-refit` |

---

### 9. Storage budget per CF (illustrative)

Per `docs/futurebuild/04_DDA_TCT_CODE_ENRICHMENT.md §9` at 30k panels.

| Data class | Per-entity size | At scale | Compressed | After 90d cold |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Panel (5,120-d × f32) | 20 KB | 600 MB | 8-bit hot 5 KB / 5-bit cool | 150 MB cold |
| DDA signals per chunk | ~240 B | ~30k × 30 chunks × 240 B = 216 MB | 5-bit cool 67 B | 60 MB cold |
| Per-embedder vectors per chunk (cached) | 5 × 1024 × f32 = 20 KB | 18 GB | 5 KB / chunk cool | 4.5 GB cold |
| Constellation cells (13,728 max) | ~1 KB | 13 MB | n/a | 13 MB |
| Training certs | ~2 KB / step | 100k × 2 KB = 200 MB | n/a | 200 MB |
| Weight blobs (deduplicated) | ~30 MB / variant | 10 variants = 300 MB | n/a | 300 MB |
| Witness chain | 73 B / step | 100k × 73 = 7 MB | Merkle compress @ 90d | 1 MB |
| Live predictions | ~5 KB | bounded by session count | tier_demote(promote) | 100 MB warm |
| Agent feedback events | ~1 KB | bounded ~10k | n/a | 10 MB |
| **Total** | — | ~19 GB hot | — | ~5.4 GB cold |

Headroom: 30 GB hot + 70 GB cold = ~100 GB under the 115 GB hard quota.

---

### 10. Locked schema rules

1. **CF names live in `context-graph-mejepa-cf`** (dependency-free) to prevent drift.
2. **JSON, NOT bincode**, for any value that uses `serde::Serialize` with `skip_serializing_if` (per past bug: `bincode + skip_serializing_if = broken`).
3. **Soft delete writes to `CF_SYSTEM` prefix** (per past bug: in-memory HashMap was lost on restart).
4. **Multi-edge keys must include target** (`[type(1)][source(16)][target(16)]` = 33 bytes per BUG-3 fix in `graph_link_tools.rs`).
5. **Always sort inverted-index lists** on update (per past bug: concurrent ops corrupted unsorted lists in `remove`).
6. **Zero-norm vectors rejected** (`STOR-M1`).
7. **PQ-8 must store actual min/max** (per past bug: hardcoded `[-1, 1]` corrupted dequantization).
8. **Importance clamping at handler level** (constructor clamp bypassed by serde deserialization).
9. **session_id filtering happens in post-filter** (not in store layer) via `source_metadata.session_id`.
10. **AgentDB CFs share the same `/mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/contextgraph-rocksdb/`** in the hybrid backend; do not split.

---

### 11. What is NOT a CF

- The shift log JSONL (`/mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl`) — append-only file, not a CF.
- Embedder model artifacts (`/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/`) — filesystem, not CF.
- FSV evidence (`/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/`) — filesystem, mode 0600.
- Operator-facing exports (`/mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/`) — filesystem (markdown / CSV / JSON).
- BlackBox ledger (`~/.blackbox/`) — separate SQLite.
- HNSW index files — separate `.hnsw` files alongside RocksDB.
- Daemon logs (`/mnt/d/contextgraph/logs/`) — rotated daily.


<!-- ===== END: 04_database_schema.md ===== -->
