<a id="05-ast-chunker-subsystem"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 05_ast_chunker_subsystem.md (sha256:4579837bbd26f837662cd975a7fe7dd9630a75e64816a95bfd26c0c82fa5802f, 164 lines) ===== -->

## 05 — AST Chunker Subsystem

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-core/src/memory/ast/lib.rs`, `crates/context-graph-core/src/memory/ast/chunker.rs`, `crates/context-graph-core/src/memory/ast/classify.rs`, `crates/context-graph-core/src/memory/ast/entity_types.rs`, `crates/context-graph-core/src/memory/ast_chunker.rs` (legacy), `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/dynamicjepa.rs` (multi-language semantic extractor), `docs/ruvectorfindings/14_AST_AWARE_CHUNKING_FOR_EMBEDDERS.md`, `docs/futurebuild/04_DDA_TCT_CODE_ENRICHMENT.md §2`.

> **Status**: New 11-language API **shipped** at `context_graph_core::memory::ast`. Legacy `AstCodeChunker` (Rust-only) is being converged ([#6](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/6)). Reformat-invariant chunk-SHA audit pending ([#12](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/12)).

---

### 1. Purpose

Produce **AST-perfect atomic chunks** bounded by syntactic entities (function / class / impl / module-decl / trait / macro / interface / type-alias / ...). Every code-domain ME-JEPA instrument (E_AST, E_CFG, E_DataFlow, E_TypeGraph, E_Diff, E_Test) consumes these chunks — **never raw text, never fixed-size windows**.

Target chunk size: **~500 non-whitespace characters**, bounded by AST node, per the Qodo / cAST methodology in `docs/ruvectorfindings/14_AST_AWARE_CHUNKING_FOR_EMBEDDERS.md §3`.

---

### 2. Supported languages (11)

Each backed by a `tree-sitter` grammar at version 0.23.x.

| # | Language | tree-sitter crate | EntityTypes recognized |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Rust | `tree-sitter-rust` | Function, Method, Impl, Trait, Struct, Enum, Module, Macro, TypeAlias, Const, Static |
| 2 | Python | `tree-sitter-python` | Function, Method, Class, Module, AsyncFunction, Decorator |
| 3 | JavaScript | `tree-sitter-javascript` | Function, ArrowFunction, Class, Method, Module |
| 4 | TypeScript | `tree-sitter-typescript` | Function, ArrowFunction, Class, Method, Interface, TypeAlias, Enum, Module |
| 5 | Go | `tree-sitter-go` | Function, Method, Type, Package, Interface, Struct |
| 6 | Java | `tree-sitter-java` | Class, Method, Interface, Enum, Package |
| 7 | C | `tree-sitter-c` | Function, Struct, Typedef, Enum, Macro |
| 8 | C++ | `tree-sitter-cpp` | Function, Class, Struct, Namespace, Method, Template, Enum |
| 9 | C# | `tree-sitter-c-sharp` | Class, Method, Interface, Struct, Namespace, Property, Enum |
| 10 | Ruby | `tree-sitter-ruby` | Method, Class, Module, Block |
| 11 | PHP | `tree-sitter-php` | Function, Class, Method, Trait, Interface, Namespace |

---

### 3. Public API

#### 3.1 Types

```rust
pub enum Language {
    Rust, Python, JavaScript, TypeScript, Go, Java,
    C, Cpp, CSharp, Ruby, Php,
}

pub enum EntityType {
    Function, Method, Class, Impl, Trait, Module,
    Struct, Enum, Interface, TypeAlias, Macro,
    Namespace, Package, Property, Const, Static,
    AsyncFunction, Block, Template, Decorator, Other(String),
}

pub struct Chunk {
    pub chunk_id: Sha256,            // SHA-256 of (language, kind, text)
    pub language: Language,
    pub kind: EntityType,
    pub byte_range: (usize, usize),
    pub line_range: (u32, u32),
    pub text: String,                // chunk content
    pub sha: Sha256,                 // same as chunk_id for now
}

pub struct ChunkOptions {
    pub target_chars: usize,         // default 500 non-whitespace
    pub max_chars: usize,            // default 800 hard cap
    pub include_doc_comments: bool,  // default true
    pub include_imports_as_module: bool, // default true
    pub reject_on_parse_error: bool, // default true
}
```

#### 3.2 Functions

```rust
// Primary entry point.
pub fn chunk_with_options(
    source: &str,
    language: Language,
    opts: ChunkOptions,
) -> Result<Vec<Chunk>, ChunkError>;

// Quick classifier without chunking.
pub fn classify(source: &str, language: Language)
    -> Result<Vec<(EntityType, u32, u32)>, ChunkError>;

// Language detection by file extension.
pub fn detect_language(path: &Path) -> Option<Language>;

// Detect mixed-language files (HTML+JS, PHP+HTML); rejects.
pub fn detect_mixed_language(source: &str, language: Language) -> bool;
```

#### 3.3 Errors

| Error | Trigger |
|---|---|
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_PARSE_FAILED` | tree-sitter ERROR or MISSING node; emits `(byte_offset, line, node_kind)` diagnostics. Never falls back to text chunking. |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_UNSUPPORTED_LANGUAGE` | language not in the 11-language matrix |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_EMPTY_SOURCE` | empty source; no empty chunks emitted |
| `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_MIXED_LANGUAGE` | embedded language detected (HTML+JS, PHP+HTML); reject for now; future work routes per-block |

---

### 4. Algorithm

For each call to `chunk_with_options(source, language, opts)`:

1. **Parse**: `Parser::new().set_language(grammar)?; parser.parse(source, None).ok_or(...)`. Fail closed on `ERROR` / `MISSING` nodes.
2. **Mixed-language detection**: per-language pattern check (e.g., `<script>` blocks in PHP; `<?php` in HTML). If detected, return `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_MIXED_LANGUAGE`.
3. **Walk the AST**: depth-first traversal using `tree-sitter::TreeCursor`. For each node, ask the per-language classifier (`classify.rs`) whether it's a chunkable entity (Function, Class, Impl, etc.).
4. **Entity boundary selection**: at each chunkable node, emit a chunk if its size ∈ [target_chars × 0.3, max_chars]. If too large, recursively walk into sub-entities (e.g., methods inside a class). If too small, merge with adjacent siblings at the parent level (configurable; default merges trivial siblings).
5. **Capture doc comments**: leading doc comments are absorbed into the chunk (per language: `///` for Rust, `"""..."""` for Python, `/** */` for JSDoc/Javadoc).
6. **Module preamble**: leading imports / `use` / `package` statements form a single `Module` chunk per file (if `include_imports_as_module=true`).
7. **Compute SHA-256**: `chunk_id = sha256(format!("{lang}|{kind:?}|{text}"))`. **Reformat-invariant audit pending** ([#12](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/12)).
8. **Return** `Vec<Chunk>` in source-byte order.

---

### 5. Python-specific notes

- `python_lex.rs` (in `context-graph-mejepa-corpus`, shared with the chunker by intent) provides a **triple-quote / f-string-aware masker** so docstrings and f-strings don't get mistakenly mutated by mutation operators or chunked as ordinary code.
- Decorators are absorbed into the following function chunk.
- `async def` becomes `EntityType::AsyncFunction` to distinguish concurrency-related chunks for `E_CFG`.

### 6. Rust-specific notes

- `impl <Type>` blocks become `EntityType::Impl` chunks. Methods inside are emitted as sub-chunks if the impl block is large.
- `mod foo { ... }` becomes one chunk for the mod-decl line + sub-chunks for inner items if large.
- `macro_rules!` becomes `EntityType::Macro`.

### 7. JS/TS-specific notes

- Both `function foo() {}` and `const foo = () => {}` become chunks; the arrow form maps to `EntityType::ArrowFunction`.
- TypeScript-only: `interface`, `type alias`, `enum` are chunked separately.
- Class methods are sub-chunks.

### 8. Cross-language entity-type matrix (per `docs/ruvectorfindings/14`)

Routing in `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/routing.rs` uses this `(Language, EntityType)` 2-tuple for the **143-row** embedder-set lookup. Languages × EntityTypes that are physically impossible (e.g., `Impl` for Python) are absent from the routing table.

### 9. FSV evidence

| FSV | Source | Output |
|---|---|---|
| Multi-language chunking | `cargo run -p context-graph-core --example ast_chunker_multilang_fsv` | `/tmp/contextgraph-ast-chunker-multilang-fsv/aggregate-evidence.json` + per-language dirs |
| Per-language toolchain check | `cargo run -p context-graph-{rust,python,c,cpp,java,csharp,go,js,ts,ruby,php}-ast-chunker-fsv` | per-language `ast-chunker-evidence.json` |
| Mixed-language rejection | included in multilang FSV | PHP/HTML mixed-language edge case readback passes |

---

### 10. What this subsystem commits to

1. Atomic chunks bounded by AST entities; never text-window or fixed-size.
2. SHA-256 chunk IDs computed from `(language, kind, text)`; reformat-invariance is **partial** ([#12](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/12)).
3. Fail-closed on parse errors with byte-offset diagnostics. Never fall back to text chunking.
4. Reject mixed-language sources rather than partial-handle.
5. 11 languages × ~13 entity types per language ≈ 143 routing rows.
6. Cross-references: [`06_embedder_substrate.md`](06_embedder_substrate.md) (routing), [`07_instruments_panel.md`](07_instruments_panel.md) (chunk consumers), [`14_corpus_phase0.md`](14_corpus_phase0.md) (mutation operators on chunks).

### 11. Convergence work

- **Legacy `AstCodeChunker` (Rust-only)** in `crates/context-graph-core/src/memory/ast_chunker.rs` needs to converge to the new API or be deleted. Tracked: [#6](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/6).
- **Reformat-invariant chunk-ID audit**: equivalent AST node IDs across reformatting must produce equivalent chunk SHAs. Tracked: [#12](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/12).


<!-- ===== END: 05_ast_chunker_subsystem.md ===== -->

## 06 — Embedder Substrate (E1–E21)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/{lib,routing,digest,vram,calibration,ensemble,forward,embedder_id,types,config}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-embeddings/src/{models/mod,traits/mod,provider/,gpu/,training/}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-core/src/types/`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/12_EMBEDDER_INTEGRATION.md`, `docs/futurebuild/05_EMBEDDER_SIGNAL_MAP.md`, `config/models_config.toml`.

---

### 1. The 21 embedder slots

| ID | Name | Dim | Family | Active for code? | VRAM (fp16) | Source family |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | e5-large-v2 (general semantic) | 1024 | dense general-purpose | YES | 0.6 GB | `intfloat/e5-large-v2` |
| E2 | sinusoidal time-PE | 512 | temporal positional | partial | 0 | deterministic |
| E3 | recurrence time-PE | 512 | temporal cycle | partial | 0 | deterministic |
| E4 | session-position-PE | 512 | session ordering | partial | 0 | deterministic |
| E5 | causal asymmetric | 768 | **RETIRED** | NO | — | post-2026-05-09 retire |
| E6 | sparse-keyword (BM25-ish) | 30k sparse | sparse lexical | YES | 0 | codebook |
| E7 | Qodo-Embed (code-specific) | 1536 | dense code | YES | 1.5 GB | `Qodo/Qodo-Embed-1-1.5B` |
| E8 | graph-attention (code graph) | 1024 | graph | YES | <1 GB | adapter on E7 |
| E9 | HDC (hyperdimensional) | 1024 | binary high-dim | YES | <0.1 GB | codebook |
| E10 | multimodal-paraphrase (doc/query asym) | 1024 | dense paraphrase | YES | 0.7 GB | `intfloat/multilingual-e5-large` |
| E11 | KEPLER entity | 768 | **RETIRED** | NO | — | post-2026-05-09 retire |
| E12 | ColBERT (token-level multi-vec) | 128/token | late-interaction | YES (selective) | 0.5 GB | `colbert-ir/colbertv2.0` |

| E16 | learner — focus | 256 | learner-state | NO (non-code) | <0.2 GB | adapter |
| E17 | learner — affect-text (E_Reasoning input) | 768 | learner-state | YES | 0.5 GB | `cardiffnlp/twitter-roberta-base-sentiment-latest` |
| E18 | learner — context novelty | 256 | learner-state | partial | <0.2 GB | adapter |

**Active set for ME-JEPA-Code**: {E1, E2, E3, E4, E6, E7, E8, E9, E10, E12, E13, E14, E17} = **13 embedders**. E18/E20 in routing at low weight. **Total active VRAM at fp16 ≈ 6.5 GB** (RTX 5090 has 32 GB; ample headroom).

---

### 2. Unique contribution per embedder

Source: `docs/futurebuild/05_EMBEDDER_SIGNAL_MAP.md §2`.

| Embedder | Unique contribution | Example concern surfaced |
|---|---|---|
| E1 | What the code does in NL terms | docstring↔body drift |
| E2 | Recency | weight recent edits higher |
| E3 | Cyclic timing pattern | rushed-Friday-commit detection |
| E4 | Position within agent session | over-thinking around edit 15-20 |
| E6 | Exact identifier match (typo-resistant via sparse) | new identifier `_walk_compaund` has no callsite |
| E7 | Code semantics (Qodo) | implementation matches buggy exemplar at 0.91 cosine |
| E8 | Call graph / def-use / control flow | new function calls helper with wrong arg order |
| E9 | Surface-form fingerprint | duplicated code block at 0.97 surface |
| E10 | Comment ↔ code paraphrase | commit message disagrees with diff |
| E12 | Per-token fine-grained matching | `Model.coeff_` symbol resolution |
| E13 | Term expansion | finds 12 related callsites needing update |
| E14 | Ensemble diversity vs E7 | blind-spot when E7 vs E14 disagree |
| E17 | Tone of agent prose | hedging detection for reasoning class |

---

### 3. Per-failure-mode embedder contribution (cross-reference)

Source: `docs/futurebuild/05_EMBEDDER_SIGNAL_MAP.md §3`. Full table in [`17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md §3`](17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md). Highlights:

| Failure / concern | Leading embedders |
|---|---|
| WrongAlgorithm / OffByOne / NullDeref | E7, E8, E14 |
| TypeError / SignatureMismatch | E8 + E12 |
| ImportMissing | E6 + E12 |
| SyntaxError | E_AST instrument (deterministic; embedder-independent) |
| ResourceLeak / RaceCondition | E8 + E_StaticAnalysis |
| EdgeCase | E_DataFlow + E8 + symbolic-execution proxy |
| ShadowedVariable / InconsistentNullCheck | E_StaticAnalysis + E_DataFlow |
| DuplicatedCode | E7 + E14 + E9 |
| Perf regression | scalar bench delta + E8 (loop pattern) |
| Security (SQL/Cmd/XSS injection) | E_StaticAnalysis + E12 |
| Reasoning class | E17 + pairwise[E_Reasoning][E_Diff] / [E_Oracle] |

---

### 4. Routing table

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/routing.rs`. **143 rows** covering 11 languages × ~13 entity types. Example rows:

| Language | EntityType | Active embedder set |
|---|---|---|
| Python | Function | {E1, E7, E10, E12, E14} |
| Python | Class | {E1, E7, E8, E10, E14} |
| Python | Module | {E1, E10, E14} |
| Python | AsyncFunction | {E1, E7, E10, E12, E14, E18} (low-weight novelty) |
| Rust | Function | {E1, E7, E8, E10, E12, E14} |
| Rust | Impl | {E1, E7, E8, E14} |

| (any) | DocString | {E1, E10, E17} |

`mejepa_routing_lookup` MCP tool exposes this table. Retired slots (E5, E11) fail closed with `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_RETIRED` if ever called.

---

### 5. Public API

#### 5.1 `Instrument` trait (used by ME-JEPA-instruments)

```rust
pub trait Instrument: Send + Sync {
    fn slot(&self) -> InstrumentSlot;
    fn output_dim(&self) -> usize;
    fn encode(&self, event: &TypedEvent) -> Result<Vec<f32>, InstrumentError>;
}
```

#### 5.2 `EmbedderForward` trait (in `forward.rs`)

```rust
pub trait EmbedderForward: Send + Sync {
    fn embedder_id(&self) -> EmbedderId;
    fn output_dim(&self) -> usize;
    fn forward(&self, input: &EmbedderInput) -> Result<Vec<f32>, EmbedderError>;
}

pub struct PretrainedEmbedderForward {
    embedder_id: EmbedderId,
    model_path: PathBuf,
    digest_sha256: [u8; 32],
    runtime: CandleRuntime,
    tokenizer: Tokenizer,
    vram_reserved_gb: f32,
}
```

Wrappers exist for E1, E6, E7, E8, E10, E12, E13, E14. E10 has full FSV. Remaining work: full per-embedder FSV + cache + production integration ([#3](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/3)).

#### 5.3 `EmbedderBackedInstrumentHead` (in `forward.rs`)

```rust
pub struct EmbedderBackedInstrumentHead {
    base: Box<dyn EmbedderForward>,
    projection: LinearProjection,
    target_slot: InstrumentSlot,
}
```

Used to map E7/E1/E14/E10 outputs into code/text/trace panel slots with source-id checks and fail-closed analyzer-feature requirements.

---

### 6. Sourcing, digest verification, retirement policy

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/digest.rs`.

- **Models live under `/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/<embedder_id>/`** (separate from the 115 GB hot quota).
- **`models_config.toml`** declares per-embedder: model path, SHA-256 digest, dim, family, tokenizer path, VRAM cost.
- **At daemon startup**, the digest is verified for every active embedder. Mismatch → `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_DIGEST_MISMATCH`; daemon refuses to start.
- **Retired slots (E5, E11)** are not loaded; calls to them fail closed with `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_RETIRED`.
- **E17 requires a calibration cert** at `data/utl_calibration/e17/` (per `calibration.rs`); load fails closed otherwise.
- **Clean-room rule** per `memory/decisions/agent-141-coordinator--upstream-reference-only-clean-room.md`: never vendor or path-dep on `ruvnet/agentdb` or `ruvnet/ruvector`. Reimplement clean-room only. Clones are read-only inspiration.

---

### 7. VRAM budget gate

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-embedders/src/vram.rs`.

```
VramBudget {
    available_gb: f32,    // measured from nvidia-smi at startup
    reserved_gb: f32,     // budget = available - reserved headroom
    per_embedder: HashMap<EmbedderId, f32>,
}
```

- Default reserved headroom: 3.0 GB (for predictor activations + KV-cache + workspaces).
- Refuse start if `sum(per_embedder × active) + reserved > available`.
- `mejepa_vram_budget_report` MCP tool returns the breakdown.
- **WSL GPU telemetry hardening** is futurebuild ([#10](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/10)).

---

### 8. Embedder cache (per-(embedder, chunk_sha))

Futurebuild Phase D, `TASK-ENRICH-004`. Cache size 5 GB default (`embedder.cache_size_gb`). 100% hit on re-embedding the same chunk → near-zero re-cost. Lives under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/embedder-cache/`.

---

### 9. Lazy loading

- **E15, E16, E19, E21** (non-code learner-state) are lazy-loaded — only loaded when a non-code domain prediction is requested.
- **E17, E18, E20** are loaded eagerly when code-domain routing requires them.
- **Active code-domain set** (13 embedders) loads at daemon startup; warmup CLI command (`context-graph-cli warmup`) pre-loads all 14 production models in 20-30 seconds on RTX 5090.

---

### 10. Per-embedder asymmetry (where applicable)

Source: `crates/context-graph-core/src/types/model_id/core.rs` + `crates/context-graph-core/src/similarity/`.

| Embedder | Asymmetry | Storage |
|---|---|---|
| E5 (retired) | cause / effect dual vector | dual CF (`CF_E5_CAUSAL_CAUSE`, `CF_E5_CAUSAL_EFFECT`) |
| E8 | source / target dual vector | dual storage |
| E10 | doc / query dual vector | dual storage |

E5 direction-mask was 1.2× boost for cause→effect, 0.8× for effect→cause (retired; CF kept for repair).

---

### 11. The cross-embedder blind-spot detection

Source: `docs/futurebuild/05_EMBEDDER_SIGNAL_MAP.md §6`.

```rust
fn cross_embedder_anomaly(chunk, panel) -> Vec<BlindSpot> {
    for (m1, m2) in unique_pairs(active_embedders) {
        let c = cosine(panel.per_chunk_emb[chunk][m1],
                       panel.per_chunk_emb[chunk][m2]);
        let mu = expected_pairwise[m1][m2];          // baseline on KnownGood corpus
        let z = (c - mu) / sigma_pairwise[m1][m2];
        if z.abs() > BLIND_SPOT_Z_THRESHOLD {
            yield BlindSpot { chunk, m1, m2, observed_cosine: c, expected_cosine: mu, z_score: z };
        }
    }
}
```

Surfaced via `search_cross_embedder_anomalies` MCP tool (shipped). Becomes a 144-d auxiliary input to the failure-mode head in futurebuild Phase B (`TASK-PREDICT-006-blind-spot-features`).

---

### 12. ModelId vs NUM_EMBEDDERS gotchas

Per `MEMORY.md`:

- `ModelId` has **14 variants**; `NUM_EMBEDDERS = 13`.
- `ModelId::Kepler` (E11) is the 14th ModelId but shares the E11 weight slot.
- `ModelId::Entity` (legacy MiniLM, 384-d) is NOT the same as the E11 KEPLER (768-d production); changing one breaks `EntityModel` tests.
- `MODEL_COUNT ≠ NUM_EMBEDDERS`. Always verify.

---

### 13. Pairwise MI audit (REQ-UTML-06, shipped)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/learning_signal/pairwise_mi_matrix.rs`.

- Recompute weekly across rolling holdout split.
- Persist CSV at `/mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/pairwise-mi/<date>.csv`.
- MCP tool: `mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi`.
- Heatmap renderer is futurebuild (`TASK-OBS-002`).
- **Redundancy ceiling**: `MI > 0.85` between slots → flag for operator decision (retire / recalibrate / accept).

---

### 14. What this substrate commits to

1. Keep all 13 active code-domain embedders in routing; drop none (pairwise diversity = blind-spot signal).
2. SHA-pinned model digests; daemon fails closed on mismatch.
3. VRAM budget gate; daemon fails closed on overspend.
4. Retired slots fail closed; do not return placeholders.
5. Cache layer for per-(embedder, chunk_sha) re-edits.
6. Cross-references: [`05_ast_chunker_subsystem.md`](05_ast_chunker_subsystem.md) (input chunks), [`07_instruments_panel.md`](07_instruments_panel.md) (Panel slots backed by embedders), [`08_dda_tct_enrichment.md`](08_dda_tct_enrichment.md) (DDA signals consume embedder outputs).


<!-- ===== END: 06_embedder_substrate.md ===== -->

<a id="07-instruments-panel"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 07_instruments_panel.md (sha256:d3a9c425e7fa25c947f56f9e2bf8b39d4288a96393eff29c29465f393f4c1ad8, 288 lines) ===== -->

## 07 — Instruments + Panel(5,120-d)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-instruments/src/{lib,e_oracle,e_witness,e_static_analysis,e_code,e_text,e_trace,e_scalars,e_runtime,e_reasoning,e_embedder_heads,features,frozen_hook,linear_probe,materialize,panel_json,panel_store,tests}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-cf/src/lib.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/03_ARCHITECTURE_BLUEPRINT.md §3`.

---

### 1. Purpose

The 15 frozen instruments are the **mathematical foundation** of ME-JEPA's structural collapse immunity. Each is a deterministic function from a typed event → fixed-dim feature subvector with **zero trainable parameters**. Their concatenation forms the 5,120-d **Panel** — the target representation `f_target(panel_t)` that the predictor learns to map to in latent space.

Because they're not trainable, **`ΔΞ.target_collapse ≡ 0` by construction** (UTML invariant per `docs/ruvectorfindings/11_MEJEPA_LEARNING_FORMULA.md §5.2`). Any code path that produces non-zero target_collapse is a critical bug — an instrument has accidentally become trainable. Enforced via `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK`.

---

### 2. The 15 instrument slots (Panel composition)

| # | Slot | Dim | Builder | Inputs | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `E_AST` | 384 | Qodo + AST-token transformer over chunks | `Vec<Chunk>` from `memory::ast` | partial (deterministic shipped; Qodo/router replacement pending) |
| 2 | `E_CFG` | 256 | E8 + graph-attention over basic blocks | CFG from chunker | partial (deterministic source-shape encoder shipped; E8 head pending) |
| 3 | `E_DataFlow` | 256 | E11 + graph-attention over def-use chains | def-use graph | partial (E11 retired; replacement deferred) |
| 4 | `E_TypeGraph` | 256 | E11 + Pyright/mypy/rust-analyzer/javac/dotnet lattice | type info | partial (analyzer-backed pending) |
| 5 | `E_Test` | 384 | E1 on test-fn descriptions | test functions | partial (E1 replacement pending) |
| 6 | `E_Trace` | 512 | E2/E3/E4 + sequence transformer over `(fn_id, line_no, val_hash)` events | runtime traces | partial (live harness capture pending) |
| 7 | `E_Diff` | 256 | Qodo + diff-edge encoder | before/after source | partial (Qodo head pending) |
| 8 | `E_Witness` | 256 | Sequence transformer over 73-byte chain entries | witness chain | **shipped** |
| 9 | `E_Oracle` | 128 | pass/fail/skip/error fractions + total + has_exception + 10-class exception one-hot | test outcomes | **shipped** |
| 10 | `E_Problem` | 1024 | E1 on SWE-bench problem text | problem statement | partial (E1 replacement pending) |
| 11 | `E_CommitMsg` | 384 | E1 on commit text | commit message | partial |
| 12 | `E_StaticAnalysis` | 256 | scalar aggregates over clippy/pylint/eslint/bandit/cargo-audit | analyzer outputs | **shipped** |
| 13 | `E_Runtime` | 256 | scalar walltime + RSS + coverage Δ | runtime aggregates | partial (live capture pending) |
| 14 | `E_Reasoning` | 384 | E17 AffectText on agent CoT | agent prose | partial (E17 replacement pending) |
| 15 | `Scalars` | 128 | BFS depth + blame age + churn + complexity + coverage Δ + repo health | scalar features | partial (production plumbing pending) |
| **Total** | | **5,120** | | | |

---

### 3. `Instrument` trait

```rust
pub trait Instrument: Send + Sync {
    fn slot(&self) -> InstrumentSlot;
    fn output_dim(&self) -> usize;
    fn encode(&self, event: &TypedEvent) -> Result<Vec<f32>, InstrumentError>;
}

pub enum InstrumentSlot {
    EAst, ECfg, EDataFlow, ETypeGraph,
    ETest, ETrace, EDiff,
    EWitness, EOracle,
    EProblem, ECommitMsg,
    EStaticAnalysis, ERuntime,
    EReasoning, Scalars,
}
```

Each `encode()` is:
- **Deterministic**: same input + same constitution → byte-equal output.
- **Fail-closed**: rejects non-finite floats, dim mismatches, malformed inputs with namespace-specific errors.
- **Stateless**: no internal mutable state across calls.
- **Bounded**: returns exactly `output_dim()` floats; never longer, never shorter.

---

### 4. The Panel

```rust
pub struct Panel {
    pub data: [f32; 5120],
    pub provenance: PanelProvenance,
}

pub struct PanelProvenance {
    pub corpus_sha: Sha256,
    pub embedder_versions: HashMap<EmbedderId, String>,
    pub frozen_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub code_version: String,
    pub witness_hash: WitnessHash,
}

pub struct PanelBuilder {
    slots: HashMap<InstrumentSlot, Vec<f32>>,
}

impl PanelBuilder {
    pub fn add_slot(&mut self, slot: InstrumentSlot, data: Vec<f32>) -> Result<(), PanelError>;
    pub fn build(self) -> Result<Panel, PanelError>;
}
```

#### 4.1 Invariants (enforced by `panel_json.rs::try_from`)

| Invariant | Error |
|---|---|
| Total dim = exactly 5,120 | `MEJEPA_INFER_DIM_MISMATCH` |
| All 15 slots populated (no missing) | `MEJEPA_INFER_INSTRUMENT_MISSING` |
| No slot filled twice (double-fill) | `MEJEPA_PANEL_DOUBLE_FILL` |
| Every float finite (no NaN, no Inf) | `MEJEPA_PANEL_NON_FINITE` |
| JSON round-trip succeeds (try_from re-validates) | `MEJEPA_PANEL_INVALID_JSON` |
| Provenance metadata present | `MEJEPA_PANEL_MISSING_PROVENANCE` |

#### 4.2 PanelStore

Source: `panel_store.rs`. Persists to `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS` keyed by `(session_id, panel_id)`. Per-panel tier policy:

```rust
fn tier_for_panel(meta: &PanelMeta) -> Tier {
    match meta.access_count_30d {
        0..=5     => Tier::Cold,    // 3-bit
        6..=30    => Tier::Cool,    // 5-bit
        31..=100  => Tier::Warm,    // 7-bit
        _         => Tier::Hot,     // 8-bit
    }
}
```

`CF_MEJEPA_PANEL_META` keyed by `panel_id` holds `{ tier, access_count_30d, created_at, last_accessed }`.

---

### 5. The 4 shipped slots in detail

#### 5.1 `E_Oracle` (128-d)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-instruments/src/e_oracle.rs`.

| Sub-slot | Dim | Computation |
|---|---|---|
| pass_fraction | 1 | `count(test == Pass) / total_tests` |
| fail_fraction | 1 | `count(test == Fail) / total_tests` |
| skip_fraction | 1 | `count(test == Skip) / total_tests` |
| error_fraction | 1 | `count(test == Error) / total_tests` |
| total_test_count | 1 | normalized log(1 + total_tests) |
| has_exception | 1 | 0.0 / 1.0 |
| exception_class_onehot | 10 | one-hot over `{AssertionError, AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError, NameError, ZeroDivisionError, FileNotFoundError, Other}` |
| reserved padding | 112 | zeros (for future test taxonomy expansion) |

**Failure → all zeros except `has_exception=1, exception_class_onehot[exception_class]=1`**. FSV at `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-instruments-fsv/e_oracle_*.json`.

#### 5.2 `E_Witness` (256-d)

Source: `e_witness.rs`. Sequence-transformer over the last K 73-byte witness-chain entries. Outputs 256-d aggregation. Frozen weights; no training.

#### 5.3 `E_StaticAnalysis` (256-d)

Source: `e_static_analysis.rs`. Scalar aggregates over analyzer output deltas (clippy / pylint / eslint / bandit / cargo-audit). Per warning class: `(count_added, count_removed, severity_max, severity_mean)`. Aggregated to 256-d via fixed bucketing.

#### 5.4 `Scalars` (128-d)

Source: `e_scalars.rs`. Six scalar features × 21 bins each = 126-d + 2 reserved:

| Scalar | Source |
|---|---|
| BFS depth | distance from package root |
| Blame age | days since last meaningful blame |
| Churn | edits per month (rolling) |
| Complexity | cyclomatic / cognitive (radon / lizard / clippy) |
| Coverage Δ | line + branch coverage diff vs baseline |
| Repo health | per-repo curated yaml (stars, recent activity, test ratio) |

---

### 6. `EmbedderBackedInstrumentHead`

Source: `e_embedder_heads.rs`. Maps validated E7/E1/E14/E10 outputs into code/text/trace panel slots with:

- Source-ID checks (linking the embedder output to the chunk it came from)
- Fail-closed analyzer-feature requirements
- Frozen linear projection per slot (no gradient flow back to the embedder)

```rust
pub struct EmbedderBackedInstrumentHead {
    base: Box<dyn EmbedderForward>,
    projection: LinearProjection,         // FROZEN parameters
    target_slot: InstrumentSlot,
}
```

The projection's parameters are part of the **frozen constitution** — they ship with the constitution version and are SHA-pinned, just like the embedder weights.

---

### 7. Linear-probe gate

Source: `linear_probe.rs`. Validates panel composition by running a small frozen linear probe over the panel and checking the output sits within the expected manifold. Used to:

1. Detect panel-corruption bugs at build time (e.g., slot wired into the wrong index).
2. Prove `frozen_hook` is intact (no accidental gradient flow).
3. FSV evidence at `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-instruments-fsv/linear_probe_evidence.json`.

---

### 8. `frozen_hook.rs` — instrument gradient gate

Source: `frozen_hook.rs` + `compute_delta_xi` in `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/learning_signal/delta_xi.rs`.

```
ΔΞ_h(t) = (1 − target_collapse_h(t))^0.5
        · (1 − predictor_redundancy_h(t))^0.3
        · (1 − constellation_violation_rate_h(t))^0.2
```

`target_collapse_h ≡ 0` for every head, by construction. If `compute_delta_xi` ever observes `target_collapse > 0` or non-finite, it fails closed with `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK`. **An instrument has accidentally become trainable. Fix at the trait level, not at the loss.**

---

### 9. PanelSource trait (in `mejepa::panel_source`)

```rust
pub trait PanelSource: Send + Sync {
    fn build_panel(&self, event: &TypedEvent) -> Result<Panel, PanelError>;
    fn build_panel_with_provenance(&self, event: &TypedEvent, prov: PanelProvenance) -> Result<Panel, PanelError>;
}
```

Implementations: `ChunkPanelSource` (from `Vec<Chunk>`), `ShiftPanelSource` (from a shift-log entry — used by the shift subscriber).

---

### 10. `FrozenTargetAdapter`

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/frozen_target.rs`. Reads `panel[t=2]` from `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS` and exposes it as the **target** for the predictor's VICReg loss.

The adapter is **read-only**; it never writes back through the instrument trait. Gradients from the predictor flow into the predictor's parameters only; the panel is held fixed.

---

### 11. Panel materialization end-to-end

Source: `materialize.rs`. Called by the shift subscriber per `docs/futurebuild/07_TRAINING_DATA_FLYWHEEL.md §5`:

```rust
fn materialize_shift(shift: &Shift) -> Result<PanelMaterializationOutcome, PanelError> {
    // 1. Read file at after_sha
    let source = storage.read_file(&shift.file, &shift.after_sha)?;
    
    // 2. Chunk
    let chunks = memory::ast::chunk_with_options(&source, shift.language, default_opts())?;
    
    // 3. For each chunk, route + forward + DDA signals
    for chunk in &chunks {
        let embedders = routing::lookup(chunk.language, chunk.kind);
        for emb in embedders {
            let vec = EmbedderForward::forward(emb, chunk)?;
            cache.put((emb, chunk.sha), vec);
        }
        let dda = compute_dda_signals(chunk, &cache);
        persist(CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS, (panel_id, chunk.id), dda);
    }
    
    // 4. Build panel
    let mut builder = PanelBuilder::new();
    for slot in InstrumentSlot::all() {
        let instrument = get_instrument(slot);
        let event = TypedEvent::from(&chunks, slot);
        let data = instrument.encode(&event)?;
        builder.add_slot(slot, data)?;
    }
    let panel = builder.build()?;
    
    // 5. Persist
    PanelStore::persist(&panel, session_id, panel_id)?;
    
    Ok(PanelMaterializationOutcome { panel, witness_hash })
}
```

---

### 12. FSV evidence

| FSV | Output |
|---|---|
| All 15 slots | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-instruments-fsv/panel.json` (46 KB) + per-slot evidence |
| E_Oracle 4 cases | `{all_pass, mixed, crash_assertion_error, crash_type_error}_{input,encoded}.json` |
| Boundary cases (empty, max, malformed) | per-slot |
| Linear probe gate | `linear_probe_evidence.json` |

---

### 13. What this subsystem commits to

1. 15 frozen instrument slots = 5,120-d Panel.
2. `ΔΞ.target_collapse ≡ 0` by construction; gradient leak is a critical bug.
3. Per-(session, panel_id) RocksDB persistence with tier policy.
4. Source-of-truth provenance metadata (`corpus_sha + embedder_versions + frozen_at + code_version + witness_hash`).
5. Fail-closed on dim mismatch, non-finite floats, double-fill, missing provenance.
6. Cross-references: [`08_dda_tct_enrichment.md`](08_dda_tct_enrichment.md) (DDA signals from panels), [`09_predictor_multi_head.md`](09_predictor_multi_head.md) (predictor consumes panels), [`10_training_loop_utml.md`](10_training_loop_utml.md) (frozen target via panel[t=2]).

<a id="08-dda-tct-enrichment"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 08_dda_tct_enrichment.md (sha256:383bd21e308efb5d39d8260f338e7633dc0cd2742671775225dce0696162c0aa, 266 lines) ===== -->

## 08 — DDA + TCT Enrichment (Gτ guard, constellation)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-tct/src/{lib,builder,calibrator,constellation,freshness,gtau,hinge,hinge_types,inspect,panel_slots,rate,rate_aggregator,shrinkage,shrinkage_engine,store,types,verdict}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/learning_signal/pairwise_mi_matrix.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/04_DDA_TCT_CODE_ENRICHMENT.md`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/13_TELEOLOGICAL_CONSTELLATION_TRAINING_MEJEPA.md`.

---

### 1. The signal-count math (DDA)

For input × N frozen embedders, every input yields **n · (N + N²)** structured signals at n aggregation points (chunks):

| Signal class | Per chunk | What it measures |
|---|---|---|
| Per-embedder cosine to centroid (N) | 13 (active code) | "this chunk looks anomalous to embedder m" |
| Pairwise cosine between embedders (N²) | 169 | "embedders m1, m2 agree or disagree" — blind-spot signal |
| Pairwise mutual information (cached) | 169 | structural redundancy across embedders → ΔΞ.predictor_redundancy |
| **Total per chunk** | **351** | |
| For ~30 chunks per patch | **~10,530** | dense signal budget per training example |

For N=21 (full ensemble), it's **n × 462 signals**.

---

### 2. The pipeline (Step 4 of `04_DDA_TCT_CODE_ENRICHMENT.md §2`)

```
For each chunk × N active embedders:
  - cosine_to_centroid[m] = cosine(emb_m(chunk), constellation[category, kind, lang, m].centroid)
  - pairwise_cosine[m1][m2] = cosine(emb_m1(chunk), emb_m2(chunk))
  - pairwise_mi[m1][m2] = cached MI matrix (recomputed weekly via mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi)
Persist to CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS keyed by (panel_id, chunk_id)
```

`DdaSignals` struct (futurebuild Phase A, **shipped 2026-05-11**):

```rust
pub struct DdaSignals {
    pub per_embedder_cosine: [f32; N],
    pub pairwise_cosine: [[f32; N]; N],
    pub pairwise_mi: [[f32; N]; N],  // cached; recomputed weekly
}
```

---

### 3. The TCT constellation

Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/13 §2`, the constellation is a per-(`category × entity × language × embedder`) centroid table built from the Phase 0 mutation corpus + KnownGood baseline.

#### 3.1 Cell schema

```rust
pub struct ConstellationCell {
    pub category: MutationCategory,          // KnownGood, SubtleFlip, OffByOne, ...
    pub entity_kind: EntityType,             // Function, Class, Impl, ...
    pub language: Language,
    pub embedder: EmbedderId,
    pub centroid: EmbeddingVec,              // mean of training set's embedder-m output
    pub bayesian_shrunk: bool,               // true when N < 50
    pub n_supporting: u32,                   // training examples in cell
    pub tau_m: f32,                          // per-embedder threshold (FP rate ≤ 5% on heldout KnownGood)
    pub freshness_ts: SystemTime,            // refresh policy: stale > 90 days → refuse without override
    pub corpus_provenance: CorpusProvenance, // corpus_sha + embedder_versions + frozen_at + code_version
}
```

Persisted to `CF_MEJEPA_CONSTELLATION` keyed by `(version_id, category, entity_kind, language, embedder_id)`.

#### 3.2 Cell math

| Dimension | Cardinality |
|---|---|
| Mutation categories | 8 |
| Entity types | ~13 per language |
| Languages | 11 |
| Active embedders (code-domain) | 13 |
| **Total max cells** | **≈ 13,728** |

Many cells are **physically impossible** (e.g., `Impl` for Python; `Trait` for Java) and absent. Many will have `N < 50` and be **Bayesian-shrunk** toward the language-aggregate or entity-aggregate centroid.

#### 3.3 Bayesian shrinkage

Source: `shrinkage.rs`, `shrinkage_engine.rs`. When `n_supporting < 50`:

```
centroid_shrunk = (n × centroid_observed + shrinkage_strength × centroid_marginal)
                 / (n + shrinkage_strength)
```

with `shrinkage_strength = 50 − n`. The marginal is computed at the language-aggregate level for that (category × embedder) pair.

#### 3.4 Threshold calibration

Source: `calibrator.rs`. Per-(category × language × embedder), choose `τ_m` such that false-positive rate ≤ 5% on heldout KnownGood examples. The calibration is **adversarial** to the training set: it uses held-out shards, not the cells' supporting set.

---

### 4. Constellation construction (Phase 4b builder)

Source: `builder.rs`. Pipeline:

1. **Read mutation corpus** from `CF_MEJEPA_MUTATION_CORPUS` for the selected `corpus_id`.
2. **For each (task, category)** in the corpus:
   - Read the source file at the after_sha.
   - Chunk via `memory::ast::chunk_with_options(...)`.
   - For each chunk × active embedder: compute the embedder vector (real neural forward).
3. **Aggregate by cell** `(category, entity_kind, language, embedder_id)`:
   - Append each chunk's vector to the cell's training set.
4. **Compute centroid** (mean) + variance.
5. **Bayesian shrink** if `n_supporting < 50`.
6. **Calibrate τ_m** on heldout split.
7. **Persist** to `CF_MEJEPA_CONSTELLATION` with provenance.

Total cells materialized scales with corpus size; for the 300×8 SWE-bench Lite (Python), expect ~1,000–3,000 cells initially, growing as additional languages and per-language corpora ship (Phase C).

---

### 5. Refresh policy

Source: `freshness.rs`. A cell is refit when **any** of:

| Trigger | Default |
|---|---|
| More than 100 new training examples since last refit | 100 |
| Per-embedder threshold τ_m calibration drifts > 3% on rolling validation | 0.03 |
| Cell's freshness ts is older than max_age_days | 90 days |
| Operator force-refit via `mejepa_constellation_inspect --force-refit` | n/a |

Stale cells → `MEJEPA_INFER_GTAU_STALE_CONSTELLATION` on every call until refit (no silent operation on stale data).

---

### 6. The Gτ guard

Source: `gtau.rs`. **Deterministic O(N · d_max) test**:

```rust
fn gtau_check_panel(predicted_panel: &Panel, constellation: &Constellation) -> Vec<GuardViolation> {
    let mut violations = vec![];
    for chunk in &predicted_panel.chunks {
        for embedder_m in active_embedders() {
            let cell = constellation.lookup(
                category = "predicted",
                entity_kind = chunk.kind,
                language = chunk.language,
                embedder = embedder_m,
            );
            let cosine = cosine_similarity(
                predicted_panel.per_chunk_emb(chunk, embedder_m),
                cell.centroid,
            );
            if cosine < cell.tau_m {
                violations.push(GuardViolation {
                    embedder: embedder_m,
                    chunk: chunk.id,
                    centroid_id: cell.id,
                    cosine,
                    threshold_tau_m: cell.tau_m,
                    deficit: cell.tau_m - cosine,
                });
            }
        }
    }
    violations
}
```

If `len(violations) > 0`, verdict = `Verdict::GuardRejected` with `violating_embedders: Vec<EmbedderId>` and `violating_chunks: Vec<ChunkId>`. Chunk-level localization via `gtau_check_chunks` (shipped, FSV-verified).

#### 6.1 Why this is structurally separate from the Oracle prediction

The Gτ guard asserts the predicted panel's per-embedder geometry sits inside the manifold of acceptable predictions for this chunk in this language. **The optimizer cannot Goodhart against this** — there's no gradient flowing through the constellation centroids; they're descriptive, not learned. Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/13 §1.4`.

---

### 7. The `L_constellation_centroid` loss (training-side use)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/loss/constellation.rs` + `hinge.rs`. Hinge loss term:

```
L_constellation = Σ_m max(0, τ_m − cos(g_φ(panel)_m, c_m))
```

This pushes the predictor's output panel back toward the constellation manifold during training. Coefficient `δ = 0.2` by default in the multi-head loss aggregator (`docs/futurebuild/10 §8`).

---

### 8. The pairwise MI matrix (REQ-UTML-06, shipped)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/learning_signal/pairwise_mi_matrix.rs`.

```rust
pub struct PairwiseMiMatrix {
    pub matrix: Vec<Vec<f32>>,           // N × N
    pub embedder_ids: Vec<EmbedderId>,
    pub computed_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub source_window: (StepId, StepId),
}

pub struct PairwiseMiAuditor {
    pub redundancy_ceiling: f32,         // default 0.85
}

impl PairwiseMiAuditor {
    pub fn compute(&self, storage: &Storage) -> Result<PairwiseMiMatrix, AuditError>;
    pub fn write_csv(&self, matrix: &PairwiseMiMatrix, path: &Path) -> Result<()>;
}
```

- **Recompute weekly** via the daemon's weekly tick.
- **Persist CSV** at `/mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/pairwise-mi/<date>.csv`.
- **MCP tool**: `mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi`.
- **Heatmap renderer** is futurebuild (`TASK-OBS-002`).
- **Flag pairs with MI > 0.85**: operator decides retire / recalibrate / accept.

---

### 9. Per-head DDA signal consumption

For the multi-head predictor (see [`09_predictor_multi_head.md`](09_predictor_multi_head.md)), each head reads its DDA-signal subset as auxiliary features:

| Head | DDA signals consumed | Aggregation |
|---|---|---|
| Panel | per-chunk embedder vectors (raw) | latent target |
| Oracle | cosine_to_centroid for E_Oracle, E_Test, E_Reasoning | direct correlation |
| Failure-mode | cosine for E_AST, E_CFG, E_DataFlow, E_TypeGraph + pairwise[E_TypeGraph][E_DataFlow] | type/flow disagreement |
| Edge-case | cosine for E_DataFlow + scalar coverage_delta | uncovered branch + flow signature |
| Tech-debt | scalars (complexity, length, nesting) + cosine for E_AST | structural smells |
| Perf | cosine for E_CFG (loops, hot paths) + scalar runtime | loop / call-stack signature |
| Security | cosine for E_StaticAnalysis + scalar SAST | direct binding to analyzer |
| Accuracy | cosine for E_Test + scalar metric deltas | ML test outputs |
| Cost | scalar (CI mins, token telemetry, dep count) | non-embedded scalars |
| Reasoning | pairwise[E_Reasoning][E_Diff], pairwise[E_Reasoning][E_Oracle] | prose ↔ change agreement |

Each head's projection layer learns a head-specific attention over the DDA signals it cares about. **The trunk parameter count doesn't grow with the signal count**; only the head-side projections do.

---

### 10. Constellation max-age + operator override

Per `freshness.rs` and `REQ-TCT-09`:
- Default `max_age_days = 90`.
- Reading a stale cell → `MEJEPA_INFER_GTAU_STALE_CONSTELLATION`.
- Operator override: `mejepa_constellation_inspect --force-allow-stale --category <c> --language <l>`.
- Force-refit: `mejepa_constellation_inspect --force-refit --category <c> --language <l>`.

---

### 11. FSV evidence

| FSV | Output |
|---|---|
| Constellation construction over Phase 0 corpus | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-tct-fsv/constellation-evidence.json` |
| Gτ check (chunk-level localization) | `cargo test -p context-graph-mejepa-tct chunk_gtau --lib` |
| Bayesian shrinkage | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-tct-fsv/shrinkage-evidence.json` |
| Threshold calibration FP ≤ 5% | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-tct-fsv/calibration-evidence.json` |

---

### 12. What this subsystem commits to

1. Per-chunk DDA signals computed for every active embedder; persisted to `CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS`.
2. Per-(category × entity × language × embedder) constellation cells with Bayesian shrinkage on N<50.
3. Per-embedder threshold τ_m calibrated for FP ≤ 5% on heldout KnownGood.
4. Deterministic Gτ guard with chunk-level localization (no gradient flow through centroids — collapse-immune by structure).
5. Pairwise MI auditor surfacing redundancy ceilings.
6. Cross-references: [`07_instruments_panel.md`](07_instruments_panel.md) (Panel composition), [`09_predictor_multi_head.md`](09_predictor_multi_head.md) (per-head DDA consumption), [`14_corpus_phase0.md`](14_corpus_phase0.md) (constellation training data).

<a id="09-predictor-multi-head"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 09_predictor_multi_head.md (sha256:83bb64c9e5f415e15ad4e95bc4f6183dc75f4203e41eae1fff475341a0447ef3, 262 lines) ===== -->

## 09 — Predictor + Multi-Head Stack

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/{predictor/,compiler,frozen_target,oracle_head,panel_source,vram,calibration,conformal,ood,types}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/{loss/,aux/,trainer/,sampler/,cert/}*`, `docs/futurebuild/03_ARCHITECTURE_BLUEPRINT.md §3`, `docs/futurebuild/10_LEARNING_FORMULA_MEJEPA_TRUTH.md`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/_README.md`.

> **Status**: Trunk + Panel head + Oracle head **shipped**. The 8 auxiliary heads (failure-mode, edge-case, tech-debt, perf, security, accuracy, cost, reasoning) are **futurebuild Phase B** per `TASK-PREDICT-005`.

---

### 1. Trunk architecture

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/predictor/`.

| Property | Value |
|---|---|
| Type | Transformer encoder |
| Layers | 6 |
| Hidden dim | 1,024 |
| Attention heads | 8 |
| FFN expansion | 4× (standard) |
| Activation | GeLU |
| Residual + LayerNorm | yes |
| Input shape | concat of `(panel_t-1, panel_t)` = 10,240-d (futurebuild adds action context = 8,192-d) |
| Output shape | 5,120-d (predicted next-panel latent) |
| Precision | bf16 |
| Hardware | CUDA-only sm_120 (RTX 5090 Blackwell) |
| Framework | candle 0.9.x + cudarc |
| VRAM steady-state | ≤ 8 GB at batch=64 |
| Forward latency p50 | < 50 ms / batch (batch=64) |

**CPU construction is rejected**, not silently down-graded. Production predictor is CUDA-only.

---

### 2. The 10 prediction heads

Each head is a **thin Linear projection on top of the shared trunk output**. Adding a head costs ~Linear<5120, output_dim> parameters — essentially zero relative to the trunk's ~50M.

| # | Head | Output | Loss | Label source | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Panel | predicted_panel[t=2] in 5,120-d latent | MSE + cosine + VICReg | frozen-instrument target on observed panel[t=2] | shipped |
| 2 | Oracle | per-test pass/fail logits + count | BCE + count loss | Docker oracle outcome | shipped |
| 3 | Failure-mode | logits over FailureModeClass per chunk | softmax × focal | rule-based extractor over pytest/cargo-test traceback | futurebuild Phase B |
| 4 | Edge-case | logits over EdgeCaseClass per chunk × `not_covered_by_tests` | sigmoid multilabel | coverage Δ + symbolic-execution proxy on AST | futurebuild Phase B |
| 5 | Tech-debt | logits over DebtClass per chunk | sigmoid multilabel | clippy/pylint/eslint warning delta + complexity metrics | futurebuild Phase B |
| 6 | Perf | (cpu_us_delta, rss_kb_delta, alloc_count_delta) per chunk | Huber regression | pytest-benchmark / cargo bench / time / massif | futurebuild Phase B |
| 7 | Security | logits over SecurityConcernClass per chunk | sigmoid multilabel | bandit / cargo-audit / eslint-security / semgrep | futurebuild Phase B |
| 8 | Accuracy | (accuracy_metric_delta, calibration_delta) | regression | ML-test metric regex extractor | futurebuild Phase B |
| 9 | Cost | (CI_minutes_delta, token_cost_delta) | regression | CI duration + token counter + dep counter | futurebuild Phase B |
| 10 | Reasoning | logits over ReasoningClass for agent's prose | softmax | weak supervision via E_Reasoning ⟂ E_Diff / E_Oracle | futurebuild Phase B |

---

### 3. Head taxonomy details

#### 3.1 Panel head (shipped)

Linear<5120, 5120> + VICReg-targeted regularization. Output is the predicted next panel in 5,120-d latent space. **Frozen target** = observed `panel[t=2]` from `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS` via `FrozenTargetAdapter`. Loss = MSE + cosine similarity + VICReg (variance + invariance + covariance per `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/loss/vicreg.rs`).

#### 3.2 Oracle head (shipped)

`OracleHead`: `Linear<5120, num_tests + 1>` per `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/oracle_head.rs`. Output: per-test pass/fail logits + a `+1` count slot for total tests. Loss: BCE on logits + count regression.

#### 3.3 Failure-mode head (futurebuild Phase B)

Logits over the `FailureModeClass` enum (`WrongAlgorithm`, `OffByOne`, `NullDeref`, `ResourceLeak`, `RaceCondition`, `DeadlockPotential`, `InfiniteLoop`, `StackOverflow`, `TypeError`, `SignatureMismatch`, `ImportMissing`, `SyntaxError`, `SemanticError`, `AssertionViolation`, `ContractViolation`, `ApiMisuse`, `ConfigDrift`, `EncodingError`, `DateTimeError`, `NumericalInstability`, `SerializationError`, `SchemaMigrationGap`, `DependencyVersionConflict`, `PlatformAssumption`, `CompilerVersionGap`, `PermissionsError`, `WrongTestUpdated`, `Other(String)`). Per-chunk; softmax + focal loss.

Label source: deterministic rule-based extractor over `pytest` / `cargo-test` / `jest` tracebacks (no LLM). Patch-similarity-graph retrieval for predict-before-tests-run path.

#### 3.4 Edge-case head (futurebuild Phase B)

Logits over `EdgeCaseClass` (`EmptyInput`, `SingleElement`, `BoundaryValue`, `UnicodeEdge`, `WhitespaceOnly`, `LeadingTrailingWhitespace`, `DuplicateKeys`, `CircularReference`, `SelfReference`, `DeepNesting`, `LargeInput`, `UninitializedField`, `OptionalNotPresent`, `DefaultValueLeakage`, `ConcurrentAccess`, `PartialFailure`, `CrossPlatform`, `TimezoneTransition`, `Locale`, `NetworkPartition`, `DiskFull`, `PermissionDenied`, `Other(String)`) × `not_covered_by_test` boolean. Sigmoid multilabel.

Label source: coverage delta + symbolic-execution proxy on AST + per-language pattern catalog. A 9th mutation category `MutationKind::EdgeCaseExploit` (proposed) would generate inputs targeting the predicted edge.

#### 3.5 Tech-debt head (futurebuild Phase B)

Logits over `TechDebtClass` (`HighCyclomaticComplexity`, `DeepNesting`, `LongFunction`, `LongParameterList`, `LongFile`, `DuplicatedCode`, `DataClumps`, `FeatureEnvy`, `ShotgunSurgery`, `GodObject`, `PrematureAbstraction`, `DeadFlagBranch`, `TodoWithoutOwner`, `CommentedOutCode`, `MagicNumber`, `StringLiteralForEnum`, `CopyOnWriteAbuse`, `SerdeUntagged`, `UnboundedRetry`, `UnboundedCache`, `Other`). Per-chunk; sigmoid multilabel.

Label source: complexity metrics (radon / lizard / clippy::cognitive_complexity) + duplicate detection (pmd / simian) + TODO regex.

#### 3.6 Perf head (futurebuild Phase B)

Regression over `PerfAxis` (`CpuMs`, `WallclockMs`, `RssKb`, `HeapAllocs`, `HeapBytes`, `StackBytes`, `BinarySize`, `StartupMs`, `P50LatencyMs`, `P99LatencyMs`, `Throughput`, `NetworkBytesIn/Out`, `DiskRead/WriteBytes`, `GpuMemoryMb`, `Other`). Predicts `(axis, delta_pct, baseline_value)` per chunk. Huber loss.

Label source: pytest-benchmark / cargo bench / time / valgrind massif deltas vs KnownGood baseline.

#### 3.7 Security head (futurebuild Phase B)

Logits over `SecurityConcernClass` (`SqlInjection`, `CommandInjection`, `PathTraversal`, `Xss`, `Csrf`, `Ssrf`, `XmlExternalEntity`, `Deserialization`, `InsecureRandom`, `HardcodedSecret`, `LoggingSecret`, `OverbroadException`, `InsecureCryptoAlgo`, `InsufficientCryptoKeyLength`, `MissingAuth`, `BrokenAccessControl`, `OpenRedirect`, `ClickjackingMissing`, `MissingTlsVerify`, `Other`). Per-chunk; sigmoid multilabel; optional CVSS estimate.

Label source: bandit / cargo audit + cargo deny / eslint-plugin-security / semgrep + CVE database via dep-version delta.

#### 3.8 Accuracy head (futurebuild Phase B)

Regression over `AccuracyMetric` (`F1`, `Precision`, `Recall`, `Auc`, `Accuracy`, `MeanAbsoluteError`, `MeanSquaredError`, `R2`, `PerplexitySnapshot`, `CalibrationError`, `BrierScore`, `EmbeddingSimilarity`, `DownstreamTaskScore`, `Other`). For ML / data-pipeline code.

Label source: test outputs that include accuracy metrics (regex extractor on common metric names).

#### 3.9 Cost head (futurebuild Phase B)

Regression over `CostAxis` (`CiMinutes`, `CiCpuSeconds`, `CiWallSeconds`, `EstimatedDollarsPerRun`, `LlmTokensPerCall`, `LlmDollarsPerCall`, `StorageBytes`, `EgressBytes`, `DependencyCount`, `BuildSeconds`, `Other`).

Label source: CI duration deltas + agent token telemetry + build-script timing + dep-graph counter.

#### 3.10 Reasoning head (futurebuild Phase B)

Logits over `ReasoningClass` (`Correct`, `MostlyCorrect`, `PlausibleButWrong`, `Hallucination`, `Hedging`, `Overclaiming`, `UnderClaiming`, `Mute`). For the agent's accompanying prose. Softmax.

Label source: weak supervision via E_Reasoning agreement with E_Oracle + E_Diff. When E_Reasoning ⟂ E_Diff → `Hallucination`; when E_Reasoning describes outcome that disagrees with E_Oracle's predicted outcome → `PlausibleButWrong`.

---

### 4. Loss aggregation

Per `docs/futurebuild/10_LEARNING_FORMULA_MEJEPA_TRUTH.md §8`:

```
L_total = α·L_panel + β·L_pairwise + γ·L_action
        + δ·L_constellation + ε·L_anti-pole + ζ·L_uncertainty + η·L_invariant
        + λ_oracle·L_oracle
        + λ_failure_mode·L_failure_mode
        + λ_edge_case·L_edge_case
        + λ_tech_debt·L_tech_debt
        + λ_perf·L_perf
        + λ_security·L_security
        + λ_accuracy·L_accuracy
        + λ_cost·L_cost
        + λ_reasoning·L_reasoning
```

**Defaults** (per §8 same doc):

| Coefficient | Default | Ramped to | Ramp trigger |
|---|---|---|---|
| α (panel) | 1.0 | fixed | n/a |
| β (pairwise) | 0.3 | fixed | n/a |
| γ (action) | 0.4 | fixed | n/a |
| δ (constellation) | 0.2 | fixed | n/a |
| ε (anti-pole VICReg) | 0.5 | fixed | n/a |
| ζ (uncertainty) | 0.1 | fixed | n/a |
| η (invariant) | 0.1 | fixed | n/a |
| λ_oracle | 0.5 | fixed once shipped | n/a |
| λ_failure_mode | 0.05 → 0.30 | F1 > 0.6 |
| λ_edge_case | 0.05 → 0.20 | uncovered-branch recall > 0.5 |
| λ_tech_debt | 0.05 → 0.10 | PR-comment alignment > 0.5 |
| λ_perf | 0.05 → 0.15 | benchmark MAE < 10% |
| λ_security | 0.05 → 0.20 | static-analyzer agreement > 0.7 |
| λ_accuracy | 0.05 → 0.10 | ML-test metric MAE < 5% |
| λ_cost | 0.02 → 0.05 | CI-time MAE < 20% |
| λ_reasoning | 0.05 → 0.10 | reasoning-class agreement > 0.6 |

**Ramp policy**: `+0.05 / week` once trigger crosses, until steady-state. The heal scheduler manages ramping automatically.

---

### 5. Per-head deltas (UTML extension)

Per `docs/futurebuild/10_LEARNING_FORMULA_MEJEPA_TRUTH.md`:

```
For each head h:
  ΔP_h(t)        = clamp_[0,1]( loss_h(predicted_h, target_h) / SNR_h(t) )
  ΔK_h(t)        = clamp_[0,1]( 0.5·cos_align_h + 0.3·(1−fisher_violation_h) + 0.2·(1−ECE_h) )
  ΔΞ_h(t)        = (1 − target_collapse_h)^0.5 · (1 − predictor_redundancy_h)^0.3 · (1 − constellation_violation_rate_h)^0.2
                   target_collapse_h ≡ 0 (frozen instruments)

Globally:
  ΔP_global(t)   = Σ_h(λ_h · ΔP_h(t)) / Σ_h(λ_h)
  ΔK_global(t)   = Σ_h(λ_h · ΔK_h(t)) / Σ_h(λ_h)
  ΔΩ(t)          = (effective_plasticity)^0.4 · (landscape_health)^0.4 · (stability_floor)^0.2
  ΔΞ_global(t)   = min_h(ΔΞ_h(t))                                   // any-head-collapse hard-gates global
  L_step(ex)     = ΔP_global · ΔK_global · ΔΩ · ΔΞ_global
```

**`ΔΞ_global = min_h(ΔΞ_h)`** — the MINIMUM, not the mean. Collapse in any single head halts global learning until the heal scheduler intervenes.

---

### 6. Per-head Fisher matrices

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/heal/ewc.rs`. Extended for multi-head:

```rust
pub struct FisherSnapshot {
    pub trunk_fisher: Tensor,                          // shared across heads
    pub per_head_fisher: HashMap<HeadId, Tensor>,      // head-specific projections
    pub witness_hash: WitnessHash,
    pub ts: SystemTime,
    pub source_examples_window: (StepId, StepId),
}
```

When a head's LoRA refresh fires, **only its per-head Fisher** is consulted for EWC++ regularization; the trunk Fisher protects the shared body.

---

### 7. Forward path

```rust
pub fn forward(panel_t_minus_1: &Panel, panel_t: &Panel) -> Result<HeadOutputs, PredictorError> {
    // 1. Concatenate inputs
    let input = concat(panel_t_minus_1.data, panel_t.data);  // 10,240-d
    
    // 2. Project to trunk hidden
    let h = input_projection(input);  // hidden 1024
    
    // 3. Transformer trunk forward
    let h = trunk.forward(h);  // 6 layers
    
    // 4. Trunk output (shared)
    let trunk_out = trunk_output_projection(h);  // 5,120-d
    
    // 5. Per-head projections (parallel)
    HeadOutputs {
        panel: panel_head(trunk_out),
        oracle: oracle_head(trunk_out),
        failure_mode: failure_head(trunk_out),     // futurebuild
        edge_case: edge_head(trunk_out),           // futurebuild
        tech_debt: debt_head(trunk_out),           // futurebuild
        perf: perf_head(trunk_out),                // futurebuild
        security: sec_head(trunk_out),             // futurebuild
        accuracy: acc_head(trunk_out),             // futurebuild
        cost: cost_head(trunk_out),                // futurebuild
        reasoning: reason_head(trunk_out),         // futurebuild
    }
}
```

---

### 8. Per-head saliency / explain path (futurebuild)

`TASK-PREDICT-004 mejepa_explain_prediction` exposes:

- **Contributing signals**: per-embedder + per-chunk cosines, weights
- **Saliency map**: attention weights from the head's projection layer
- **Closest exemplars**: from `CF_MEJEPA_FAILURE_EXEMPLARS` keyed by (failure_mode_class, embedder_violation_signature)
- **Counterfactual minimum edit**: smallest change that would flip the concern

<a id="10-training-loop-utml"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 10_training_loop_utml.md (sha256:faffb8bee68f25911d1001b89b3249806c0f4cdbff2c20844aba4806d0bcd279, 301 lines) ===== -->

## 10 — Training Loop + UTML

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/{lib,trainer/,sampler/,optim/,loss/,cert/,checkpoint,distill/,aux/,learning_signal/,lora_wiring,bin/mejepa-train,cli/}*`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-cf/src/lib.rs`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/11_MEJEPA_LEARNING_FORMULA.md`, `docs/futurebuild/10_LEARNING_FORMULA_MEJEPA_TRUTH.md`, `docs2/TheoryOfMachineLearning.md` (UTML v1.0 JEPA Edition).

---

### 1. The UTML formula

```
L_step = ΔP × ΔK × ΔΩ × ΔΞ           // all clamped to [0, 1]; multiplicative gating
```

| Factor | Meaning | Formula | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **ΔP** | Predictive efficiency | `clamp_[0,1]( loss / SNR + exploration_bonus )` | shipped |
| **ΔK** | Knowledge coherence | `0.5·cos_align(g_t, ḡ_prior) + 0.3·(1−fisher_violation) + 0.2·(1−ECE)` | shipped |
| **ΔΩ** | Optimization-state health | `effective_plasticity^0.4 · landscape_health^0.4 · stability_floor^0.2` | shipped |
| **ΔΞ** | Predictor health | `(1 − target_collapse)^0.5 · (1 − predictor_redundancy)^0.3 · (1 − constellation_violation_rate)^0.2` | shipped (target_collapse ≡ 0 enforced) |

**Multiplicative gating**: any one at zero zeros the whole product. ΔΞ.target_collapse ≡ 0 by construction (frozen instruments) — enforced via `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK`.

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/learning_signal/{delta_p,delta_k,delta_omega,delta_xi,l_step}.rs`.

---

### 2. Multi-head extension

Per `docs/futurebuild/10_LEARNING_FORMULA_MEJEPA_TRUTH.md`:

```
Per head h:
  ΔP_h, ΔK_h, ΔΞ_h computed independently
  ΔΩ shared (one optimizer state)

Globally:
  ΔP_global   = Σ_h(λ_h · ΔP_h) / Σ_h(λ_h)
  ΔK_global   = Σ_h(λ_h · ΔK_h) / Σ_h(λ_h)
  ΔΞ_global   = min_h(ΔΞ_h)       // ANY-head collapse hard-gates global
  L_step      = ΔP_global · ΔK_global · ΔΩ · ΔΞ_global
```

Per-head Fisher matrices (`heal::ewc::FisherSnapshot::per_head_fisher`) so a head's update can be regularized against its own consolidated knowledge.

---

### 3. Sampling weight

```
sampling_weight(ex) = base_weight
                    · L_step(ex)
                    · (1 + 5 · operator_override_flag)
                    · (1 + 2 · agent_surprise_severity_score)      // futurebuild Phase A
                    · decay^age_in_days
```

- `base_weight = 1.0`
- `decay = 0.995` (half-life ~140 days)
- `operator_override_flag ∈ {0, 1}`; sets `+5× weight bump` for gold-standard examples
- `agent_surprise_severity_score ∈ {Low=0.2, Medium=0.5, High=0.8, Catastrophic=1.0}`
- Examples with `sampling_weight < 0.05` drop from current epoch
- Examples with `sampling_weight > 1.0` are guaranteed inclusion

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/sampler/`.

---

### 4. Optimizer

| Property | Value |
|---|---|
| Algorithm | AdamW |
| Learning rate | 5e-4 |
| Weight decay | 0.04 |
| Batch size | 64 |
| Num epochs (full retrain) | 10 |
| LR schedule | Cosine decay |
| LR floor | 1e-6 |
| Grad clip | 10× running mean of grad_norm (per-head clip) |

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/optim/`.

---

### 5. Trainer orchestration

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/trainer/`.

```rust
loop {
    // 1. BatchSampler produces sampled (panel_t-1, panel_t, action) tuples
    let batch = sampler.sample_batch(batch_size);
    
    // 2. Forward through trunk + heads
    let outputs = predictor.forward(batch);
    
    // 3. Compute multi-head loss
    let loss = compute_loss(outputs, batch.targets, lambdas);
    
    // 4. Backward
    loss.backward();
    
    // 5. Optimizer step with gradient norm tracking
    let grad_norm = compute_grad_norm();
    optimizer.step();
    
    // 6. Compute ΔP/ΔK/ΔΩ/ΔΞ + L_step
    let deltas = compute_deltas(outputs, gradients);
    
    // 7. Write TrainingCertificate (hash-chained)
    let cert = TrainingCertificate {
        step, loss_global, loss_per_head, delta_p_global, delta_p_per_head,
        delta_k_global, delta_k_per_head, delta_omega, delta_omega_components,
        delta_xi_global, delta_xi_per_head, L_step_global, sampling_weight,
        agent_surprise_severity_score_for_this_ex,
        lambda_h, predictor_redundancy_pairwise_mi, predictor_redundancy_pairwise_mi_source,
        grad_norm_per_head, drift_coverage, merkle_root,
        parent_witness_hash, self_hash,
    };
    persist(CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS, step, cert);
    
    // 8. Witness chain entry
    witness_chain.append(witness_entry(cert.self_hash));
    
    // 9. Update sampling weight for the involved examples (over-sample hard ones)
    sampler.update_weights(batch, deltas);
    
    // 10. Periodic holdout eval
    if step % 1000 == 0 {
        let holdout_metrics = run_holdout_eval();
        persist(CF_MEJEPA_EVAL_REPORTS, ...);
    }
}
```

---

### 6. Training certificate (CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS)

Each step writes:

```jsonc
{
  "step": 1234,
  "loss_global": 0.0234,
  "loss_per_head": {
    "panel": 0.0210,
    "oracle": 0.043,
    "failure_mode": 0.067,
    "edge_case": 0.085,
    "tech_debt": 0.052,
    "perf": 0.041,
    "security": 0.038,
    "accuracy": 0.029,
    "cost": 0.023,
    "reasoning": 0.061
  },
  "delta_p_global": 0.42,
  "delta_p_per_head": { ... },
  "delta_k_global": 0.71,
  "delta_k_per_head": { ... },
  "delta_omega": 0.83,
  "delta_omega_components": { "effective_plasticity": 0.85, "landscape_health": 0.90, "stability_floor": 1.0 },
  "delta_xi_global": 0.997,
  "delta_xi_per_head": { ... },
  "L_step_global": 0.247,
  "sampling_weight": 0.247,
  "agent_surprise_severity_score_for_this_ex": 0.0,
  "lambda_h": { "panel": 1.0, "oracle": 0.5, ... },
  "predictor_redundancy_pairwise_mi": 0.07,
  "predictor_redundancy_pairwise_mi_source": "rolling_window_2026_05",
  "grad_norm_per_head": { ... },
  "drift_coverage": 0.91,
  "merkle_root": "...",
  "parent_witness_hash": "shake256:73:...",
  "self_hash": "shake256:73:..."
}
```

Hash chain: `self_hash = SHAKE-256(parent_witness_hash || serialize(cert_body))`. Validation: `optimizer_witness_chain_verify` MCP tool.

---

### 7. UTML carriers (REQ-UTML-01..09 — all shipped)

| Carrier | Function | File |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-UTML-01: ΔP | `compute_delta_p(predicted, target, snr, exploration_bonus)` | `learning_signal/delta_p.rs` |
| REQ-UTML-02: ΔK | `compute_delta_k(grad, ema_grad, fisher, ece, embedder_coherence)` | `delta_k.rs` |
| REQ-UTML-03: ΔΩ | `compute_delta_omega(plasticity, landscape, stability, agent_state)` | `delta_omega.rs` |
| REQ-UTML-04: ΔΞ | `compute_delta_xi(target_collapse, redundancy, violation)` (target_collapse ≡ 0) | `delta_xi.rs` — fails closed `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK` on leak |
| REQ-UTML-05: EMA gradient | running mean of recent batch gradients | inline |
| REQ-UTML-06: Pairwise MI audit | `PairwiseMiAuditor` + CSV writer | `pairwise_mi_matrix.rs` + `mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi` MCP tool |
| REQ-UTML-07: Continual backprop | `DormantUnitDetector` + `reinit_dormant_units` | `continual_backprop.rs` + `heal/regulate.rs` |
| REQ-UTML-08: State-transfer T | `compute_state_transfer` + `performance_deploy` | `state_transfer.rs` |
| REQ-UTML-09: FSV corner cases | 100 synthetic examples spanning corner cases | `train_edge_cases_fsv` example |

---

### 8. Consolidation integral M(t)

Per UTML §VII:

```
M(t) = ∫₀ᵗ L_step(τ) · ΔΞ(τ) · k_replay(τ) · e^(−(t−τ)/τ_decay(τ)) dτ
```

Operational mapping:

| Term | Value in ME-JEPA |
|---|---|
| `L_step(τ)` | per-step formula above |
| `ΔΞ(τ)` | 1 × predictor health × constellation violation rate (frozen instruments → target_collapse=0) |
| `k_replay(τ)` | 1 normally; 3-4 during periodic full-retrain |
| `τ_decay(τ)` | 7 days for un-replayed weights; 365 days for high-Fisher consolidated examples |

Product `L_step · ΔΞ` is the "did this step learn anything generalizable" signal. The scheduler keeps it high via:
- Soft drift → LoRA refresh (raises L_step for the violating head)
- Plasticity loss → continual backprop (raises ΔΩ.effective_plasticity)
- Calibration drift → recalibrate (raises ΔK.ECE)
- Constellation staleness → refit (lowers ΔΞ.constellation_violation_rate)

---

### 9. State-transfer T

Per UTML §VIII:

```
T_global(train, deploy)   = exp(−d(P_train, P_deploy) / λ)
performance_deploy        = M(t) · T_global

Per-cell (multi-head extension):
T_cell(c, l)              = exp(−d(P_train_cell(c, l), P_deploy_cell(c, l)) / λ_cell)
performance_deploy_cell   = M(t) · T_cell(c, l)
```

Computed per (`category`, `language`) cell. Persisted to `CF_MEJEPA_STATE_TRANSFER`. Surfaced in weekly eval report. Active-learning queue prioritizes cells with low T (deploy distribution drifted from training).

---

### 10. The "Dissipating ML" failure mode

Per UTML §VII.1:

```
Dissipating_global ≡ ( mean(L_step) over last N updates > 0.3 )
                    AND ( ΔK or ΔΞ silently degrading; validation generalization gap widening )
                    AND ( ΔM / mean(L_step) < 0.2 )
```

Multi-head extension:

```
Dissipating_per_head_h ≡ ( mean(L_step_h) > 0.3 )
                        AND ( ΔK_h or ΔΞ_h silently degrading )
                        AND ( head_h_holdout_metric_delta / mean(L_step_h) < 0.2 )
```

Detection: rolling training loss trend vs rolling holdout-correlation trend. If training loss decreasing > 5% over 1000 steps but holdout correlation flat or decreasing → `MEJEPA_DISSIPATION_DETECTED` (futurebuild Phase E, `TASK-HEAL-003`).

Intervention: replay-buffer enrichment + EWC re-anchor + LR annealing/warmup reset + continual backprop reinit + fresh real-data injection (the flywheel handles this naturally).

---

### 11. LoRA wiring + distillation

Source: `lora_wiring.rs`, `distill/mod.rs`.

- **LoRA**: rank `r=16` adapter per head (default); applied on top of active weights during refresh.
- **Distillation**: aux-head distillation back into trunk every 1,000 steps. Source: `aux/mod.rs`. Tracked in `CF_MEJEPA_DISTILL_STEPS`.

---

### 12. Holdout evaluation cadence

| Cadence | What runs |
|---|---|
| Every 1,000 steps | per-(category × language) correlation check; persisted to `CF_MEJEPA_EVAL_REPORTS` |
| Every 10,000 steps | full periodic retrain via heal scheduler (default `training.full_retrain_every_attempts`) |
| Weekly (daemon cron) | full holdout eval + ship-gate status report + active-learning queue surface |

---

### 13. FSV evidence

| FSV | Output |
|---|---|
| Training cert chain (100k steps) | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-train-fsv/training-cert-chain.json` |
| UTML L_step over 100 synthetic examples | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-train-fsv/utml-l-step-evidence.json` |
| Holdout eval | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-train-fsv/holdout-eval-evidence.json` |
| Target collapse leak (negative test) | rejected with `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK`; cert NOT written |
