<a id="11-inference-verify-lifecycle"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 11_inference_verify_lifecycle.md (sha256:1fa0df3764ffcb6486796350c564d1c0f4c28b8affcea462869f5fa2ed93edad, 330 lines) ===== -->

## 11 — Inference + verify() Lifecycle

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/{compiler,conformal,ood,calibration,verify_cli,gates,types,oracle_head}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/eval/`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-tct/src/{gtau,verdict}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/mejepa_tools.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/08_LIVE_PREDICTION_AGENT_API.md`, `docs/futurebuild/15_PREDICTION_TAXONOMY.md`.

---

### 1. `MeJepaCompiler` API

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/compiler.rs`.

| Method | Signature | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `compile` | `compile(patch, context) -> RealityPrediction` | Forward path only (no oracle binding) |
| `verify` | `verify(patch, context) -> Verdict` | Full verdict assembly |
| `observe` | `observe(panel, action, panel_next, oracle_outcome) -> ObserveOutcome` | Online SGD with EWC++ + cert chain |
| `compile_with_panel` | `compile_with_panel(panel) -> RealityPrediction` | Skip chunker; use existing panel |

---

### 2. The 5 Verdict variants

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/types.rs`.

```rust
pub enum Verdict {
    Pass,                // confidence ≥ ship_gate_threshold AND no high-severity Q4 AND every claim reconciles
    Fail,                // ≥1 predicted_failed_test at confidence ≥ failure_threshold
    OutOfDistribution,   // ood_score ≥ ood_threshold OR conformal interval width > width_threshold
    Abstain,             // conformal interval too wide to commit
    GuardRejected,       // ≥1 embedder cosine to centroid < τ_m (TCT Gτ)
}
```

---

### 3. The unified `RealityPrediction` schema

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/types.rs` + `docs/futurebuild/15_PREDICTION_TAXONOMY.md §1`.

```rust
pub struct RealityPrediction {
    // Identity
    pub prediction_id: PredictionId,
    pub witness_hash: WitnessHash,
    pub ts: SystemTime,
    pub session_id: SessionId,
    pub covered_chunks: Vec<ChunkId>,
    
    // Headline
    pub verdict: Verdict,
    pub confidence_interval: ConformalInterval,
    pub ood_score: f32,
    
    // Q3: WHY
    pub predicted_failure_modes: Vec<PredictedFailureMode>,
    pub predicted_failed_tests: Vec<PredictedTestOutcome>,
    pub guard_violations: Vec<GuardViolation>,
    pub closest_exemplars: Vec<ExemplarMatch>,
    
    // Q4: WHAT ELSE — always emitted (empty vec if none above threshold)
    pub predicted_edge_cases: Vec<PredictedEdgeCase>,
    pub predicted_latent_bugs: Vec<PredictedLatentBug>,
    pub predicted_tech_debt_added: Vec<PredictedTechDebt>,
    pub predicted_dead_code: Vec<PredictedDeadCode>,
    pub predicted_redundant_code: Vec<PredictedRedundancy>,
    pub predicted_perf_regressions: Vec<PredictedPerfRegression>,
    pub predicted_security_concerns: Vec<PredictedSecurityConcern>,
    pub predicted_accuracy_degradations: Vec<PredictedAccuracyDegradation>,
    pub predicted_cost_regressions: Vec<PredictedCostRegression>,
    pub predicted_reasoning_class: ReasoningClass,
    
    // Q1: EXISTENCE
    pub claim_graph: AgentClaimGraph,
    pub claim_reconciliation: Vec<ClaimReconciliation>,
    
    // Q5: REALITY IMPACT
    pub reality_impact: Option<RealityImpact>,
    
    // Provenance
    pub provenance: PredictionProvenance,
}

pub struct PredictionProvenance {
    pub predictor_version: String,        // e.g., "v0.4.2-bf16-cuda13.2"
    pub constellation_version: String,
    pub calibration_version: String,
    pub active_pointer: String,           // weights SHA
}
```

**Every Q4 field is always populated** — empty vec when no concerns above threshold. The absence of a prediction is itself a prediction.

---

### 4. Forward path (full lifecycle)

```
verify(patch, context):
  1. Parse patch (unified diff or file_changes)
  2. For each affected file:
     a. memory::ast::chunk_with_options() → Vec<Chunk>
     b. For each chunk × routed embedders: EmbedderForward::forward()
     c. Compute DDA signals → CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS
  3. PanelBuilder → Panel(5,120-d)
  4. predictor.forward(panel_t-1, panel_t) → HeadOutputs (10 heads)
  5. ConformalPredictor::wrap(output, calibration) → ConformalInterval
  6. ood_score = OodDetector::score(panel, predictor)
  7. mejepa_tct::gtau::gtau_check_chunks(predicted_panel, constellation) → Vec<GuardViolation>
  8. Resolve verdict:
       if guard_violations.len() > 0       → GuardRejected
       else if ood_score > ood_threshold   → OutOfDistribution
       else if conformal_width > width_t   → Abstain
       else if predicted_failed_tests has high-conf entry → Fail
       else if conformal_lower >= ship_gate_threshold AND no high-severity Q4 AND every claim reconciles
                                            → Pass
       else                                 → Fail or Abstain by edge logic
  9. Assemble RealityPrediction with all Q1-Q5 fields
  10. Persist to CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS keyed by session_id (latest + history)
  11. Bind witness_hash to chain
  12. Return Verdict (and full RealityPrediction via include_provenance=true)
```

---

### 5. Conformal predictor (SplitConformal)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/conformal.rs`.

```rust
pub struct ConformalInterval {
    pub lower: f32,
    pub upper: f32,
    pub method: ConformalMethod,             // SplitConformal | Mondrian | Group
    pub coverage_target: f32,                // typically 0.90
    pub empirical_coverage: f32,             // measured on calibration set
}
```

Algorithm:
1. Hold out 10% of training as calibration split.
2. Compute non-conformity score: `score(x) = max_test |predicted - actual|` per `REQ-INFER-03`.
3. Sort non-conformity scores ascending; pick quantile at `1 - α` (default α=0.10 → 90% coverage).
4. Threshold = the picked quantile.
5. At inference: `confidence_interval.lower = prediction - threshold`, `upper = prediction + threshold`.
6. Empirical coverage on next 1000 inferences must stay within `0.88 - 0.92` (per `conformal.coverage_tolerance`).

Per-language stratification: separate calibration split per language. Recalibrate when `ECE > 0.03` sustained over 10k attempts (`heal::calibration::recalibrate`).

---

### 6. OOD detector

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/ood.rs`.

- **Score**: predictor reconstruction error normalized by σ². Higher = more OOD.
- **Soft threshold** (`ood.score_threshold = 0.5`): `Verdict::OutOfDistribution`.
- **Hard threshold** (`ood.hard_threshold = 0.85`): `MEJEPA_VERIFY_OUT_OF_DISTRIBUTION_HARD` — verdict still returned but flagged as untrustworthy.
- **Target AUC**: ≥ 0.85 between in-distribution and synthetic OOD inputs (per `REQ-INFER-08`).

---

### 7. Gτ guard (TCT)

See [`08_dda_tct_enrichment.md §6`](08_dda_tct_enrichment.md). One-line: for each chunk × active embedder, check `cos(predicted_emb_m, centroid_m) ≥ τ_m`. Violation → `GuardRejected` with `violating_embedders` + `violating_chunks`.

---

### 8. Claim graph + reconciliation (Q1)

Source: futurebuild `TASK-PREDICT-007-agent-claim-graph-extractor`. Deterministic rule-based parser over the agent's natural-language response.

```rust
pub enum ClaimKind {
    Added(SymbolRef),
    Modified(SymbolRef),
    Removed(SymbolRef),
    Renamed(SymbolRef, String),
    Tested(TestId),
    Verified(String),
    Refactored(String),
    Documented(SymbolRef),
    Other(String),
}

pub enum ReconciliationStatus {
    Confirmed,              // claim matches bytes
    Missing,                // claim says added X; X not present
    Modified,               // claim says modified X to do Y; X exists but doesn't do Y
    UnexpectedSideEffect,   // X exists but file Z was also modified — not claimed
    SuperficialMatch,       // symbol exists but signature/body diverges
    AmbiguousRef,           // claim references "the helper function" — multiple matches
    Unverifiable,           // claim too vague to bind to bytes
}
```

Extraction: regex/parser over `(added|modified|removed|renamed|tested|verified|refactored|documented) <symbol> in <file>`. **No LLM call.** Free-text claims become `ClaimKind::Other`.

---

### 9. Reality impact reconciler (Q5)

Source: futurebuild `TASK-PREDICT-008-reality-impact-reconciler`.

```rust
pub struct RealityImpact {
    pub observed_shifts: Vec<ShiftEntry>,
    pub predicted_files_changed: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub observed_files_changed: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub unexpected_files_changed: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub predicted_test_outcomes: Vec<PredictedTestOutcome>,
    pub observed_test_outcomes: Vec<ObservedTestOutcome>,
    pub prediction_correctness: PredictionCorrectness,
}

pub enum PredictionCorrectness {
    Aligned, UnderPredicted, OverPredicted, DivergentInClass, Surprise,
}
```

**`Surprise` triggers a SurpriseEvent + active-learning queue entry.**

---

### 10. The MCP read-side surface

| Tool | Purpose | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `mejepa_verify` | Synchronous patch verification | shipped |
| `mejepa_predict_latest` | Latest prediction for session from `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` | shipped |
| `mejepa_predict_what_if` | Hypothetical without applying the edit | futurebuild Phase B |
| `mejepa_explain_prediction` | Per-prediction saliency + contributing signals + counterfactual | futurebuild Phase B |
| `mejepa_constellation_inspect` | TCT cell lookup + force refit | shipped |
| `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` | Agent records `SurpriseEvent` | futurebuild Phase A |

---

### 11. Agent-side example interaction

```
[Agent: Edit src/foo.py]
  → PreToolUse hook records pre_sha
  → Edit returns
  → PostToolUse hook records after_sha + appends shift-log JSONL

~250ms later, daemon's shift subscriber consumes the line:
  → chunks src/foo.py
  → runs neural forwards
  → computes DDA signals
  → builds Panel
  → calls verify(panel, action, context)
  → RealityPrediction lands in CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS

[Next prompt fires UserPromptSubmit hook]
  → Hook reads CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS
  → Renders markdown injection (≤ 4 KB):

  ### Reality prediction
  **Verdict**: Fail (oracle pass confidence 0.07 [0.04, 0.12])
  **Predicted failure modes**:
    1. src/foo.py:62-118 — WrongAlgorithm — predicted FAIL of test_X (0.93)
       violating embedders: E_TypeGraph, E_DataFlow
       closest exemplar: psf__requests-2317
  **Predicted edge cases not covered**:
    - EmptyInput at src/foo.py:12
  **Predicted tech debt**: 1 new TODO without owner
  **Reasoning class**: MostlyCorrect
  **To respond to wrong prediction**: call mejepa_record_agent_feedback with prediction_id=pred_xyz

[Agent reads, decides ME-JEPA is wrong about test_X — test doesn't exist]
[Agent calls mejepa_record_agent_feedback]
  → SurpriseEvent persisted to CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK
  → Active-learning queue entry created (high priority)
  → Sampling weight for src/foo.py chunk_xyz bumped 1.6×

[Agent runs pytest]
  → Bash hook captures test results
  → TestOutcomeEvent persisted to CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS

[Scheduler's next 250ms tick]
  → observe(panel, action, panel_after_test, oracle=Pass)
  → Training cert written; ΔP/ΔK/ΔΩ/ΔΞ logged
```

---

### 12. Error codes

Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §failure_taxonomy`:

| Code | Trigger |
|---|---|
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_INVALID_PATCH` | unified diff could not be parsed |
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_REPO_UNAVAILABLE` | `repo_root` not a git repo |
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_OUT_OF_DISTRIBUTION_HARD` | OOD ≥ hard threshold |
| `MEJEPA_VERIFY_GUARD_REJECTED_HARD` | every chunk fails Gτ |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_OOD_REFUSE` | OOD ≥ soft threshold |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_DIM_MISMATCH` | panel dim ≠ 5,120 |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_INSTRUMENT_MISSING` | required instrument unavailable |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_CONSTELLATION_VIOLATION` | Gτ rejected predicted panel |
| `MEJEPA_INFER_GTAU_STALE_CONSTELLATION` | constellation > max_age |
| `MEJEPA_CALIBRATION_INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES` | < N samples in calibration window |

<a id="12-self-healing-scheduler"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 12_self_healing_scheduler.md (sha256:0e6212c3f07d99c6f8682304936bd37e572226282a36e236d96595502a0693f6, 238 lines) ===== -->

## 12 — Self-Healing Scheduler

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/heal/{drift,ewc,promote,full_retrain,integrity,lora_refresh,calibration,distill,regulate,pipeline,drill,store,cf,fsv,scheduler,scheduler_state}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/learning_signal/{continual_backprop,state_transfer}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/09_SELF_HEALING_AUTONOMY.md`.

> **Status**: All 18 `heal/*` modules **shipped** (Phase 5). Phase A scheduler skeleton with 7 tickers shipped. Phase E full autonomous policy is futurebuild.

---

### 1. The 7 supervised tickers

Source: `heal::scheduler::run_self_optimization_scheduler`.

| Ticker | Period | Action | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `observe_tick` | 250 ms | Drain pending shifts → `observe()` with EWC++; deadline 200ms | `compiler::observe` |
| `drift_tick` | 60 s | Snapshot drift window → classify Soft/Hard/Catastrophic | `heal::drift` |
| `active_learning_tick` | 600 s | Drain top-N high-priority queue entries (`AgentSurprise` fast-tracks observe) | `eval::queue` |
| `promote_tick` | 3600 s | Run pending LoRA refresh + (if scheduled) full A/B/C retrain | `heal::promote` + `heal::full_retrain` |
| `continual_backprop_tick` | 1800 s | Detect dormant units; reinit if `dormant_fraction > 0.15` | `learning_signal::continual_backprop` |
| `constellation_freshness_tick` | 3600 s | Find stale cells (> 90 days); force-refit | `mejepa_tct::freshness` |
| `emergency_eviction_tick` | 600 s | Inspect quota; invoke incremental eviction if > threshold | `hygiene::quota` |

---

### 2. Drift classes

Source: `heal::drift::classify`.

| Class | Range | Intervention | Promotion gate | Operator role |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `None` | ≥ 88% | none | n/a | none |
| `Soft` | 88–85% | LoRA refresh on most-violating head | A/B/C on holdout | none |
| `Hard` | 85–80% | Calibration recalibrate + LoRA refresh | A/B/C stricter | none |
| `Catastrophic` | < 80% | Full A/B/C retrain on rolling corpus | A/B/C multi-window stability | **alert + approve before promote** |

Rolling window: 1,000 attempts. Per-cell stratification is futurebuild Phase E (`TASK-HEAL-002`).

---

### 3. EWC++ regularization

Source: `heal::ewc.rs`. Online Fisher matrix with task-boundary detection.

```rust
pub struct FisherSnapshot {
    pub trunk_fisher: Tensor,                      // shared across heads
    pub per_head_fisher: HashMap<HeadId, Tensor>,  // head-specific (futurebuild Phase B)
    pub witness_hash: WitnessHash,
    pub ts: SystemTime,
    pub source_examples_window: (StepId, StepId),
}
```

EWC++ adds a regularization term to the loss:

```
L_ewc = Σ_i Fisher_i × (θ_i − θ_anchor_i)²
```

When a head fast-tracks (LoRA refresh), only its per-head Fisher is consulted; the trunk Fisher protects the shared body.

---

### 4. A/B/C promotion gate

Source: `heal::promote::run` + `heal::full_retrain::run_periodic`.

- **A** = current active model (the "champion")
- **B** = freshly-retrained model on the rolling corpus + most-recent shifts
- **C** = ensemble of A and B (weighted average)

Each evaluated on holdout:

```
score = holdout_correlation - 0.05 · inference_latency_multiplier
```

Example: `C` wins by 0.94 − 0.05·1.4 = 0.87 vs `B`'s 0.93 − 0.05·1.8 = 0.84 vs `A`'s 0.91 − 0.05·1.0 = 0.86 → `C` promoted.

Active pointer (`CF_MEJEPA_ACTIVE_POINTERS`) atomically updated. Previous active retained as rollback target for 30 days. `mejepa_rollback_to` MCP tool for operator-forced rollback.

**Catastrophic class requires `mejepa_promote_approval` (futurebuild Phase E)** before replacing A; non-Catastrophic promotions are automatic.

---

### 5. Continual backprop

Source: `learning_signal::continual_backprop::{detect_dormant_units, reinit_dormant_units}`. Detector identifies units (ReLU pre-activations, attention heads) near-zero on > 99% of recent batches. Re-init applies a small Gaussian to incoming weights and resets output bias.

| Step | Action |
|---|---|
| 1 | Snapshot recent batch activations |
| 2 | Per unit: compute zero-fraction over recent batches |
| 3 | Flag units with zero_frac > 0.99 |
| 4 | If `dormant_fraction > 0.15`, queue reinit |
| 5 | Reinit + write new weights to `CF_MEJEPA_WEIGHT_BLOBS` |
| 6 | A/B/C evaluates whether reinit helped |

Operational closure of UTML §V.1 effective plasticity (REQ-UTML-07, shipped).

---

### 6. Calibration recalibration

Source: `heal::calibration::recalibrate`. Re-fits the conformal predictor:

```
1. Pull last 1000 (prediction, oracle_outcome) tuples
2. Compute non-conformity scores per SplitConformal
3. Fit new threshold so empirical coverage = stated target (0.90)
4. Persist new ConformalCalibration (versioned)
5. Update active pointer
```

Triggered when ECE > 3% sustained over 10k attempts. Runs in milliseconds.

---

### 7. LoRA refresh

Source: `heal::lora_refresh::run`. Per-head only (the most-violating head at a time):

```
1. Sample N examples weighted by sampling_weight (favors hard recent)
2. Init LoRA adapter (rank r=16; per-head)
3. Fine-tune 1 epoch with EWC++
4. Eval on holdout vs no-LoRA baseline
5. If improved > 1%, promote; else discard
```

---

### 8. Full retrain

Source: `heal::full_retrain::run_periodic`. Triggers:

- Catastrophic drift detected, OR
- 10k attempts since last full retrain (configurable), OR
- Operator: `mejepa_eval_run --force-full-retrain`

```
1. Snapshot current active weights → C variant
2. Train fresh weights from scratch on (Phase 0 corpus + rolling-window + agent-feedback gold)
3. EWC++-regularized fine-tune to anchor on prior consolidated knowledge
4. A/B/C evaluate; promote winner (Catastrophic requires operator approval)
```

---

### 9. Self-healing storage

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/`. See [`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md).

| Pathology | Detection | Intervention |
|---|---|---|
| Storage > 110 GB | quota_status @ 600s tick | Emergency eviction |
| Storage > 115 GB hard | quota emergency | Aggressive cold-tier down |
| Witness chain corruption | integrity @ 10k attempts | Auto-repair attempt; if fail, freeze writes + operator alert |
| Constellation stale > 90d | freshness @ 3600s tick | Force-refit |
| Subscriber lag > 500/min | subscriber_status @ 60s tick | Auto-restart with backoff |
| Scheduler task panic | JoinSet supervisor | Restart with exp. backoff; > 5/5min → `MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING` |
| Pairwise MI > 0.85 (redundancy) | weekly MI audit | Flag slot; operator decides |

---

### 10. Operator-facing tools

| Tool | Purpose | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `mejepa_heal_status` | drift level + queued interventions + last A/B/C result | shipped |
| `mejepa_rollback_to` | force rollback to prior model | shipped |
| `mejepa_promote_approval` | approve Catastrophic A/B/C | futurebuild Phase E |
| `mejepa_active_learning_queue` | view + label queue entries | shipped |
| `mejepa_eval_run` | force holdout eval | shipped |

---

### 11. Dissipation detection (UTML §VII.1)

Per `docs/futurebuild/09_SELF_HEALING_AUTONOMY.md §10` + `TASK-HEAL-003`:

```
Dissipating ≡ ( mean(L_step) > 0.3 )
              AND ( ΔK or ΔΞ silently degrading; validation generalization gap widening )
              AND ( ΔM / mean(L_step) < 0.2 )
```

Detection: rolling training loss trend vs rolling holdout-correlation trend. If training loss falls > 5%/1k steps but holdout flat or decreasing → `MEJEPA_DISSIPATION_DETECTED`.

Intervention: replay-buffer enrichment + EWC re-anchor + LR annealing/warmup reset + continual backprop reinit + flywheel data injection.

---

### 12. Operator's narrowed role

After scheduler ships fully autonomous:

| Operator action | Frequency | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Approve Catastrophic promotions | as triggered (rare) | guard against regression |
| Review weekly eval report | weekly | per-cell ship-gate, drift, agent-surprise summary |
| Triage active-learning top-N | weekly | provide gold labels |
| Inspect pairwise MI when flagged | quarterly | retire / recalibrate / accept |
| Tune scheduler tick periods, thresholds | as needed | policy |
| Respond to high-severity alerts | as triggered | catastrophic drift, witness corruption, flapping |
| `mejepa_rollback_to` if promotion turns out badly | rare | safety override |

**The operator does NOT manually trigger**: LoRA refresh, calibration, constellation refit, continual backprop, nightly GC, weekly eval, non-catastrophic promotions.

---

### 13. Falsifiers

Per `docs/futurebuild/09_SELF_HEALING_AUTONOMY.md §14`:

| Falsifier | Diagnosis |
|---|---|
| Self-healing detects soft drift but A/B/C never produces a winner | LoRA / calibration insufficient; need richer intervention |
| Operator forced to manually trigger A/B/C non-catastrophically | scheduler broken; promotion gate too strict |
| Correlation regresses post-promotion despite passing gate | promotion gate metric wrong; revisit |
| Same drift recurs within 24h of promotion | data distribution shift > model adaptation rate; need shorter τ_decay or higher k_replay |
| Continual backprop reinits cause measurable degradation | dormancy threshold too aggressive; tune up |

---

### 14. FSV evidence

| FSV | Output |
|---|---|
| `mejepa-heal-drill` binary | synthetic drift drill detects within 1,000 obs + promotes mode-winner |
| `phase5_self_healing_fsv` | hard/soft/catastrophic drills; evidence files mode 0600 |
| Invalid max-observations edge | exits with `MEJEPA_HEAL_INVALID_STATE`; no output root |

---

### 15. Cross-references

[`10_training_loop_utml.md`](10_training_loop_utml.md), [`13_data_flywheel.md`](13_data_flywheel.md), [`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md), [`22_operator_runbook.md`](22_operator_runbook.md).

<a id="13-data-flywheel"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 13_data_flywheel.md (sha256:b2fbd6da6b95b7a3b02c7e5f8ab8d035bb70773757ca3d10e068a8516498c9d7, 236 lines) ===== -->

## 13 — Data Flywheel (Shift Subscriber + Reward Channels)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber/src/{lib,runner,subscriber,tail,watermark,cache,shift_transform,models}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/heal/scheduler.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/07_TRAINING_DATA_FLYWHEEL.md`, `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`.

---

### 1. The 5 reward channels

| # | Channel | Source | Frequency | Label kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | **Oracle ground truth** | Docker tests + live `pytest`/`cargo test` | per invocation | hard: per-test pass/fail |
| C2 | **Shift channel** | every Edit/Write → pre/after SHA + git diff | per file mutation (~250ms) | soft: panel transition for SSL |
| C3 | **Agent-feedback** | `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` calls | event-driven | weak: prediction-correctness with severity |
| C4 | **Operator-override** | active-learning queue label | event-driven | hard: gold-standard |
| C5 | **Static-analysis snapshot deltas** | clippy/pylint/eslint/bandit/cargo-audit before vs after | per shift | soft: warning class deltas → Q4 heads |

---

### 2. Shift subscriber

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber/`.

| Component | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `ShiftLogTail` | Cursor-based JSONL tail at 250ms |
| `WatermarkWriter` | Persists last-consumed byte offset to `CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK` per session |
| `InstrumentCache` | Keyed by `(slot, file_sha)` |
| `ShiftSubscriber::open` | Reconstructs cursor from watermark on restart |
| `ShiftSubscriber::run_until_idle` | Drain pending shifts until none remain |
| `ShiftSubscriber::run_until_shutdown` | Run forever with watch-channel shutdown |
| `shift_to_inference` | Convert shift → Panel → verify() → RealityPrediction |

Latency budget: **p50 < 5s, p99 < 30s**. Lag alert at > 500 unprocessed shifts / 1 min sustained → `MEJEPA_SHIFT_SUBSCRIBER_LAG_ALERT`. Escalate after 5 min.

---

### 3. Per-shift pipeline

```
PostToolUse hook fires → appends JSONL to /mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl

~250ms later (poll_next_line tick):

ShiftSubscriber.poll_next_line() reads line:
  { ts, session_id, file, before_sha, after_sha, line_count_before, line_count_after,
    cargo_check_result, git_diff_summary, hook_id }

materialize_shift(shift) -> PanelMaterializationOutcome:
  1. Read file at after_sha
  2. memory::ast::chunk_with_options(content, language, opts) → Vec<Chunk>
  3. for each chunk:
     a. routing::lookup(chunk.lang, chunk.kind) → embedder set
     b. for each embedder: EmbedderForward::forward(chunk)
     c. compute DDA signals → CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS
  4. PanelBuilder.build() → Panel(5,120-d) → CF_MEJEPA_PANELS
  5. update CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK with consumed shift_id

shift_to_inference(panel, prior_panel, action):
  - predictor.forward → predicted_panel + per-head outputs
  - ConformalPredictor.wrap → interval
  - mejepa_tct::gtau::gtau_check_chunks → violations
  - assemble RealityPrediction
  - persist to CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS keyed by session_id

(asynchronously, when oracle outcome arrives):

observe(panel_t, action, panel_t+1, oracle_outcome):
  - EWC++ regularized SGD step
  - compute ΔP/ΔK/ΔΩ/ΔΞ → L_step
  - update sampling_weight for involved chunks
  - write TrainingCertificate to CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS (witness-chain bound)
  - if drift detector hits Soft/Hard, queue intervention
  - if SurpriseEvent on this prediction, bump weight by 1+2·severity_score
```

---

### 4. Latency budget breakdown

| Stage | Time |
|---|---|
| AST chunking | ~50 ms typical file |
| Embedder forwards (neural, parallelized over chunks) | ~200 ms |
| DDA signals | ~50 ms (cached MI; only fresh cosines) |
| Panel build + persist | ~100 ms |
| Predictor forward | ~50 ms (CUDA, batch=1) |
| Conformal + Gτ | ~10 ms |
| **Total p50** | **~500 ms** |
| **p99** | dominated by embedder forward tail latency |

---

### 5. Test-outcome capture hook (futurebuild `TASK-FLYWHEEL-003`)

PostToolUse on `Bash` captures `pytest`/`cargo test`/`jest` output via regex parsing:

```rust
struct TestOutcomeEvent {
    ts: SystemTime,
    session_id: SessionId,
    test_id: TestId,
    outcome: TestOutcome,  // Pass | Fail | Error | Skip
    duration_ms: u64,
    error_log: Option<String>,
}
```

Persisted to `CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS` keyed by `(session_id, test_id, ts)`. The scheduler's `observe_tick` picks it up on next 250ms tick.

---

### 6. Asynchronous oracle binding (futurebuild `TASK-FLYWHEEL-004`)

Predictions are made BEFORE tests run. Oracle outcomes arrive LATER. The scheduler binds retroactively:

```
On new oracle entry in CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS:
  1. Look up most-recent prediction in CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS for this session
     that includes this test in predicted_failed_tests OR predicted_passed_tests
  2. Look up corresponding panel
  3. Call observe(panel, action, panel_after_test, oracle=outcome)
     - prediction correctness computed
     - if wrong AND severity High/Critical, queue ActiveLearningKind::PredictionMissed
     - panel + outcome → training cert chain entry
```

---

### 7. Agent-feedback path (futurebuild `TASK-PREDICT-002`)

```
Agent calls mejepa_record_agent_feedback(prediction_id, kind, explanation, severity, actual_outcome)

  → SurpriseEvent persisted to CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK keyed by (prediction_id, agent_id, ts)
  → Active-learning queue entry created (kind=AgentSurprise, high priority)
  → On next active_learning_tick (≤ 600s):
       - reads SurpriseEvent
       - calls observe() with sampling weight × (1 + 2 · severity_score)
       - updates calibration-error tracking for the head that made the wrong prediction
  → If severity = Catastrophic AND calibration error > drift threshold:
       - fast-track to lora_refresh OR full_retrain queue
  → On operator side: SurpriseEvent appears in weekly eval report under "agent-flagged misses"
```

---

### 8. The replay buffer (BatchSampler)

Per `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/sampler/`:

```
sampling_weight(ex) = base_weight × L_step(ex)
                    × (1 + 5 · operator_override_flag)
                    × (1 + 2 · agent_surprise_severity_score)
                    × decay^age_in_days
```

| Behavior | Effect |
|---|---|
| High `L_step` (wrong prediction) | over-sampled |
| Operator override | 6× weight |
| Agent-flagged surprise | 1+2·severity weight bump |
| Stale low-L_step | decays out (`decay=0.995`, half-life ~140d) |
| `sampling_weight < 0.05` | drops from current epoch |
| `sampling_weight > 1.0` | guaranteed inclusion |

**Self-curating**: the replay buffer continuously over-fits to hard-and-recent examples.

---

### 9. New CFs added by the flywheel (Phase A, **shipped 2026-05-11**)

| CF | Key | Value | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS` | `(panel_id, chunk_id)` | `DdaSignals` | n·(N+N²) cache |
| `CF_MEJEPA_FAILURE_EXEMPLARS` | `(failure_mode_class, embedder_violation_signature)` | `Vec<ExemplarRef>` | per-failure-mode reverse index |
| `CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK` | `(prediction_id, agent_id, ts)` | `SurpriseEvent` | agent-side feedback ledger |

All registered via `mejepa-cf` and wired through `open_hygiene_rocksdb`. FSV at `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/mejepa-cf-fsv/round_trip.json`.

---

### 10. Failure modes the flywheel must survive

| Failure | Detection | Response |
|---|---|---|
| Subscriber lag > 500/min | `mejepa_subscriber_status` | `MEJEPA_SHIFT_SUBSCRIBER_LAG_ALERT`; daemon escalates > 5 min |
| Scheduler tokio panic | `tokio::JoinSet` supervisor | restart with exp. backoff (cap 60s); `MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING` > 5 restarts/5 min |
| Catastrophic drift | `heal::drift::classify` | A/B/C retrain alert; pause prediction emission |
| Storage overflow | `hygiene::quota::quota_status` | emergency eviction; if still overflowing, alert + halt panel writes |
| Embedder digest mismatch | startup digest check | refuse to start; `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_DIGEST_MISMATCH` |
| Oracle outcome rate drops | rolling-window arrival rate | log warning; observe() falls back to prediction-only confidence |
| RocksDB write contention | latency telemetry | auto-shrink batch size; if persistent, alert |

---

### 11. MCP daemon mode

Source: `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/daemon.rs`. Started by:

```bash
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mcp -- \
    --daemon-server-only \
    --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph \
    --daemon-port 3100 \
    --http-port 3101
```

Or via systemd: `systemctl --user enable --now mejepa-daemon`.

| Supervised task | What it runs |
|---|---|
| Shift subscriber | `ShiftSubscriber::run_until_shutdown` |
| Self-optimization scheduler | `heal::scheduler::run_self_optimization_scheduler` |
| Nightly GC scheduler | `hygiene::gc::gc_run_nightly` at 03:00 local |
| Weekly evaluation scheduler | `eval::runner::run_holdout_eval` weekly |

All under `tokio::JoinSet` supervisor; exponential backoff; flap detection.

---

### 12. Operator-visible health tools

| Tool | Status |
|---|---|
| `mejepa_subscriber_status` | shipped |
| `mejepa_heal_status` | shipped |
| `mejepa_quota_status` | shipped |
| `mejepa_vram_budget_report` | shipped |
| `mejepa_daemon_status` (aggregator) | futurebuild Phase F |

---

### 13. Cross-references

[`07_instruments_panel.md`](07_instruments_panel.md) (panel materialization), [`12_self_healing_scheduler.md`](12_self_healing_scheduler.md) (scheduler tickers), [`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md) (storage budget), [`20_hooks_layer.md`](20_hooks_layer.md) (shift-log generation).

<a id="14-corpus-phase0"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 14_corpus_phase0.md (sha256:cccc1a6b9547d5d5ae252faa1d8c0c9c3b36686c1235fcc45c8c6032634e3a06, 239 lines) ===== -->

## 14 — Phase 0 Corpus (Mutation Operators + Docker Oracle)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-corpus/src/{lib,bin/mejepa-corpus,generate,oracle,split,swebench,python_lex,patch_mutation,source_patch,source_patch_support,prng,util,known_good,subtle_flip,off_by_one,swap_variable,delete_test_call,wrong_file,over_engineer,compile_error,generic_language_ops}.rs`, `tasks/swebench-lite/*.json`, `docs/futurebuild/04_DDA_TCT_CODE_ENRICHMENT.md §7`, `MEMORY.md` Phase 0 entries.

---

### 1. Purpose

Generate the **300×8 SWE-bench Lite mutation corpus** that trains the TCT constellation + provides ground-truth labels for the predictor's Oracle head. Each of 300 Python SWE-bench Lite tasks × 8 mutation categories = **2,400 entries**.

> **Status (2026-05-11)**: Python operators all shipped; Docker oracle bridge shipped; one-instance Docker FSV passing. The 2,400-row run is **partial** — 1,200/2,400 rows persisted at `/mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus/swebench-lite-python-300x8-v1-20260511/` across 4 categories (`KnownGood`, `SubtleFlip`, `OffByOne`, `SwapVariable`). Hung on `django__django-15790` source-prep at 17:02 UTC; killed 22:04 UTC. Root cause: `Command::new("git").*.output()` in `source_patch_support.rs` has no per-subprocess timeout. **Resuming requires the timeout fix first** ([#5](https://github.com/ChrisRoyse/contextgraph/issues/5)).

---

### 2. The 8 mutation categories

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-corpus/src/{known_good,subtle_flip,off_by_one,swap_variable,delete_test_call,wrong_file,over_engineer,compile_error}.rs`.

| # | Category | Operator behavior | Expected oracle |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `KnownGood` | Echo upstream gold patch verbatim | Pass |
| 2 | `SubtleFlip` | Swap `==`↔`!=`, `<`↔`>=`, `<=`↔`>`, `&&`↔`\|\|` per language | Fail (logic flip) |
| 3 | `OffByOne` | Perturb integer literals on integer-typed sites only | Fail (boundary) |
| 4 | `SwapVariable` | Rename a local variable; introduce use of wrong name | Fail (variable confusion) |
| 5 | `DeleteTestCall` | Remove one `assert` / `assert_eq` / `expect` call | Pass (over-confident; test deletion) |
| 6 | `WrongFile` | Apply gold patch to an adjacent sibling file | Fail (mislocated) |
| 7 | `OverEngineer` | Append an unused helper function/method | Pass (waste, no functional change) |
| 8 | `CompileError` | Introduce a parse error | Fail (hard, syntax) |

---

### 3. Operator implementation

Each operator is a thin file under `crates/context-graph-mejepa-corpus/src/<operator>.rs`:

```rust
pub fn mutate(source: &str, seed: u64, opts: &MutationOpts) -> Result<Mutation, MutationError>;

pub struct Mutation {
    pub category: MutationCategory,
    pub original_source: String,
    pub mutated_source: String,
    pub site: MutationSite,
    pub original_text: String,
    pub mutated_text: String,
    pub seed: u64,
    pub witness_hash: WitnessHash,
}

pub struct MutationSite {
    pub byte_range: (usize, usize),
    pub line_range: (u32, u32),
}
```

**All operators are deterministic** via SplitMix64 PRNG (`prng.rs`). Same input + same seed = byte-equal output.

**All operators are fail-closed**:
- `MEJEPA_CORPUS_INVALID_INPUT` — source has parse errors
- `MEJEPA_CORPUS_OPERATOR_FAILED` — mutation site bounds invalid or original_text mismatch

---

### 4. Real-Python lexing (`python_lex.rs`)

A **triple-quote / f-string-aware Python masker** so docstrings and f-strings don't get mutated as ordinary code. Caught 2 real bugs:

1. `off_by_one` was extracting fractional parts of floats — fixed at root cause + regression test.
2. `subtle_flip` was picking `>` from Python `->` arrow — fixed at root cause + regression test.

---

### 5. Docker oracle bridge

Source: `swebench.rs::run_swebench_lite_oracle`. Invokes `python -m swebench.harness.run_evaluation` for ONE instance (gold or custom mode).

```rust
pub fn run_swebench_lite_oracle(
    task_id: &str,
    instance_dir: &Path,
    run_id: &str,             // Docker-name-safe regex; rejects `+`, spaces, control chars
    mode: SwebenchMode,       // Gold | Custom { patch }
    timeout: Duration,
) -> Result<OracleVerdict, OracleError>;

pub struct OracleVerdict {
    pub resolved: bool,
    pub fail_to_pass: Vec<TestId>,    // F2P
    pub pass_to_pass: Vec<TestId>,    // P2P
    pub fail_to_fail: Vec<TestId>,    // F2F
    pub pass_to_fail: Vec<TestId>,    // P2F
    pub duration_ms: u64,
}
```

Parses per-instance `report.json` → `OracleVerdict` per `swebench/harness/grading.py` semantics. End-to-end FSV against real `swebench/sweb.eval.x86_64.sympy_1776_sympy-20590:latest` Docker image: gold mode 23.7s `resolved=true` 22-tests-all-Pass.

**Docker-name-safe run_id validator** — caught a real bug (Docker container-name regex rejects `+`).

---

### 6. Repo-atomic / Instance-atomic split (`split.rs`)

```rust
pub enum SplitMode {
    InstanceAtomic,            // DEFAULT — clean [240, 30, 30] split
    RepoAtomic,                // bounded by repo concentration (django=38%, sympy=25.7%)
}

pub struct SplitAssignment {
    pub train: Vec<TaskId>,    // 80% (240 default)
    pub calibration: Vec<TaskId>, // 10% (30 default)
    pub holdout: Vec<TaskId>,  // 10% (30 default)
}
```

Algorithm: Stoer-Wagner mincut (`crates/context-graph-mincut`) + LPT pre-sort + lowest-current/target-ratio bin-pack.

Caught 3 real bugs during shipping:
1. Greedy starves small buckets — fixed via LPT pre-sort.
2. Assertion mismatch on 320 vs 300 task plan — fixed at assertion.
3. f64 JSON round-trip 1-ULP shift — fixed via canonical decimal serialization.

`assert_no_test_patch_leakage` helper added for split correctness.

---

### 7. The 300 SWE-bench Lite tasks

Source: `tasks/swebench-lite/*.json`. 300 Python task manifests:

```jsonc
{
  "task_id": "astropy__astropy-12907",
  "instance_id": "astropy__astropy-12907",
  "repo": "astropy/astropy",
  "base_commit": "abc...",
  "problem_statement": "...",
  "patch": "...",                  // upstream gold patch
  "test_patch": "...",
  "FAIL_TO_PASS": ["test_..."],
  "PASS_TO_PASS": ["test_..."]
}
```

These manifests are the **input to the Phase 0 mutation generator**. They are NOT for the (retired) inner LLM.

---

### 8. CLI: `context-graph-mejepa-corpus`

| Subcommand | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `generate` | Generate corpus under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus/<corpus-id>/` |
| `verify` | Re-run Docker oracle verification on stored rows |
| `stats` | Per-category + per-repo stats |
| `prepare-images` | Pre-pull SWE-bench Docker images |
| `cleanup` | Remove stale corpus runs |

```bash
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mejepa-corpus -- generate \
    --corpus-id swebench-lite-python-300x8-v1-20260511 \
    --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph \
    --tasks-dir tasks/swebench-lite \
    --output-root /mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus
```

Generates: `index.json`, `stats.json`, `verify-evidence.json`, `oracle-store/` (RocksDB with `CF_MEJEPA_MUTATION_CORPUS` + `CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS` + `CF_MEJEPA_CORPUS_PROVENANCE`).

---

### 9. Per-language extension (10 non-Python, futurebuild Phase C)

`TASK-CORPUS-001`. Port the 8 operators to:

| Language | Notes |
|---|---|
| Rust | `SubtleFlip` swaps `==`↔`!=`, `<=`↔`>=`, `&&`↔`\|\|`. `OffByOne` perturbs integer literals. `DeleteTestCall` removes one `assert!` / `assert_eq!`. |
| JS/TS | `SubtleFlip` swaps `===`↔`!==` (NOT `==`, to avoid coercion noise). |
| Go | `SubtleFlip` swaps `==`↔`!=`, `<`↔`>=`. `DeleteTestCall` removes one `t.Errorf` / `t.Fatal`. |
| Java | `SubtleFlip` swaps `==`↔`!=` for primitives, `.equals` ↔ `!.equals` for objects. |
| C / C++ / C# | Per-language operators with similar semantics. |
| Ruby / PHP | Same. |

Each per-language operator has a **real-language compiler/parser FSV** (e.g., `cargo check` for Rust, `tsc --noEmit` for TS, `py_compile` for Python).

After Phase C: 300 SWE-bench-like × 8 categories × 11 languages = **26,400 training entries** at full scale (the actual SWE-bench Lite is Python-only; non-Python extension is via synthetic per-language corpora + CI scrape data).

---

### 10. Storage layout

Per `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`:

```
/mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus/swebench-lite-python-300x8-v1-<date>/
├── index.json                # corpus index + provenance
├── stats.json                # per-category + per-repo counts
├── verify-evidence.json      # Docker oracle re-verification proof
└── oracle-store/             # RocksDB
    ├── CF_MEJEPA_MUTATION_CORPUS    # mutated source per (task_id, category)
    ├── CF_MEJEPA_ORACLE_VERDICTS    # Docker oracle outcomes
    └── CF_MEJEPA_CORPUS_PROVENANCE  # corpus_sha + frozen_at + code_version
```

**The generator rejects output paths outside `/mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus/`** — no `/tmp`, no C-drive, no sibling directories. Enforced via `context-graph-paths`.

---

### 11. Performance budget

| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Total entries (Phase 0 full) | 2,400 (Python) |
| Disk size (raw) | ~5 GB |
| Wall time (full run, 8-core parallel) | ~16 hours (Docker-bound; ~20–30s per task × 2,400) |
| Checkpoint per row | atomic JSON + RocksDB write |
| Resumable | yes (from RocksDB / index source of truth, post-timeout-fix) |
| Memory footprint | bounded ~1 GB per parallel worker |

Phase C (per-language) adds another ~500 tasks × 8 × 10 langs = ~40,000 entries with shorter wall times (per-language toolchain instead of full Docker).

---

### 12. FSV evidence

| FSV | Output |
|---|---|
| 8 categories on 1 SWE-bench Lite instance | Docker round-trip with real image `swebench/sweb.eval.x86_64.sympy_1776_sympy-20590:latest`, 23.7s gold, 23.0s custom, `resolved=true` |
| Split [240, 30, 30] InstanceAtomic | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-corpus-fsv/split-evidence.json` (canonical) → `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/` per locked decision |
| Split [195, 77, 28] RepoAtomic (bounded by django/sympy concentration) | per-cell evidence |
| Python operator bug fixes | 2 real bugs caught + regression tests |
| Mutation determinism | byte-equal output given same seed |

---

### 13. Cross-references

<a id="15-witness-chain"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 15_witness_chain.md (sha256:b4b2a966758253871842b962488e399e9d74cb3627d6bfbc5500c5c5d12d3133, 124 lines) ===== -->

## 15 — Witness Chain (SHAKE-256 + ed25519 + Merkle)

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-witness/src/{lib,cert,cert_merkle,cert_tests,sign}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/witness_compress.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/reality_loop/witness_chain.rs`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §security_requirements`.

> **Clean-room reimplementation.** No code copied, no upstream tracking.

---

### 1. Purpose

Tamper-evident append-only provenance chain binding every persistent artifact (panel, training cert, prediction, feedback event, recommendation) to a cryptographic hash chain.

---

### 2. Architecture

| Component | Purpose | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| **Chain entry** | 73-byte SHAKE-256 record | `lib.rs::ChainEntry` |
| **Stage 0 certificate** | Initial corpus / model genesis seal | `cert.rs::Stage0Cert` |
| **Stage F certificate** | Final attestation per cycle | `cert.rs::StageFCert` |
| **Merkle compression** | Roll-up of segments > 90 days old | `cert_merkle.rs` + `hygiene::witness_compress` |
| **ed25519 segment signing** | Operator-controlled keyring signing | `sign.rs` (feature `ed25519`) |

---

### 3. Chain entry (73 bytes)

```
[ 64 bytes SHAKE-256(parent_hash || payload) | 8 bytes timestamp (u64 BE) | 1 byte op_type ]
```

| Op type | Meaning |
|---|---|
| 0x01 | Panel append |
| 0x02 | Training cert |
| 0x03 | Prediction |
| 0x04 | Agent feedback (SurpriseEvent) |
| 0x05 | Recommendation |
| 0x06 | Harness transition |
| 0x07 | A/B/C promotion |
| 0x08 | Constellation refit |
| 0x09 | GC / hygiene operation |
| 0x0a | Stage 0 (genesis) |
| 0x0f | Stage F (cycle attestation) |
| 0xff | Merkle compression marker |

Hash chain: `entry_hash_{n+1} = SHAKE-256(entry_hash_n || op_type || timestamp || payload_hash)`.

---

### 4. Stage 0 / Stage F certificates

Source: `cert.rs`.

| Cert | Trigger | Binds |
|---|---|---|
| **Stage 0** | Genesis — corpus locked + initial weights | `corpus_sha`, `embedder_versions`, `frozen_at`, `code_version`, `operator_id` |
| **Stage F** | End of training cycle | full cycle metrics + parent witness hash + final theta SHA |

---

### 5. Merkle compression (`hygiene::witness_compress`)

Source: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/witness_compress.rs`.

After 90 days, the chain rolls up. Each ~256-entry segment becomes one Merkle root entry:

```
witness_compress_old_segments(db, max_age_days=90):
  - Find segments older than 90 days
  - For each segment of ~256 entries:
      - Build binary Merkle tree over entry hashes
      - Persist Merkle root as a single entry with op_type=0xff
      - Original entries archived to /mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/witness/compressed/
  - verify_witness_integrity() validates round-trip
```

MCP tool: `mejepa_witness_compress`. FSV at `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/examples/merkle_compression_fsv`.

---

### 6. ed25519 segment signing (feature-gated)

Source: `sign.rs` (Cargo feature `ed25519`).

- **Ed25519 keypair** stored in operator-controlled keyring (e.g., GPG agent, `~/.ssh/`).
- **Private key NEVER leaves operator hardware** per `SEC-MEJEPA-03`.
- **Signs every Stage F + every Merkle compression marker** with ed25519-dalek 2.x.
- **Verification public key** stored in `config/witness_pubkey.pem`.

---

### 7. Integrity verification

| Tool / function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `optimizer_witness_chain_verify` | Verify chain integrity + Merkle commitments; detect tampering |
| `optimizer_witness_chain_diff` | Diff segment between two offsets |
| `optimizer_witness_chain_repair_legacy` | One-time migration from pre-v2 format |
| `audit_verify_old_entry` | Verify a single old entry against the Merkle-compressed proof |
| `verify_witness_integrity` | Full chain replay; called every 10,000 attempts by `heal::integrity` |

`heal::integrity` triggers a verify every 10,000 attempts. On corruption: attempt auto-repair → if fail, **freeze panel writes** + `MEJEPA_OBSERVE_INTEGRITY_VIOLATION` alert.

---

### 8. Storage layout

```
/mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/witness/
├── witness-chain.bin                  # active append-only chain (recent ≤90d)
├── compressed/
│   └── segment-<N>-merkle-<root>.bin  # Merkle-compressed old segments
└── stage-certs/
    ├── stage0-<corpus_sha>.json       # genesis
    └── stagef-<cycle>-<theta_sha>.json # per-cycle attestation
```

---

### 9. Cross-references

[`04_database_schema.md`](04_database_schema.md) (every CF binds witness_hash), [`10_training_loop_utml.md`](10_training_loop_utml.md) (training cert chain), [`12_self_healing_scheduler.md`](12_self_healing_scheduler.md) (integrity check), [`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md) (Merkle compression).


<!-- ===== END: 15_witness_chain.md ===== -->

<a id="16-hygiene-tier-compression"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 16_hygiene_tier_compression.md (sha256:46aa96a715f64ab9c1558256f1fa3708a4f7c0ec3890e545affeaf5cdb83b237, 179 lines) ===== -->

## 16 — Hygiene + Tier Compression

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa-hygiene/src/{lib,gc,quota,quota_types,tier,tier_frozen,tier_ops,witness_compress,ops,mcp,reports,storage,entry,categories,error}.rs`, `crates/context-graph-tier-compression/src/{lib,tests}.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`.

---

### 1. Tier compression algorithm

Source: `crates/context-graph-tier-compression/src/lib.rs`. **8/7/5/3-bit groupwise linear scalar quantization** with 22-byte magic+version header.

| Tier | Bits | Compression ratio | Cosine retention (768-d Gaussian) | Bytes for 768-d vec |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hot | f32 raw | 1× | 1.000 | 3,072 |
| Warm | 8-bit groupwise | 3.89× | 0.99999 | 790 |
| Cool | 5-bit groupwise | 6.12× | 0.99960 | 502 |
| Cold | 3-bit groupwise | 9.91× | 0.99192 | 310 |
| Frozen | 1-bit / mark | — | — | minimum |

Header: magic + version + per-group `(min, max, scale)` so dequantization is exact (no hardcoded `[-1, 1]` — fixed pq-8 bug per `MEMORY.md`).

`tier_for_score(access_count_30d) → Hot/Warm/Cool/Cold/Frozen`. `decode_from_tier` round-trips losslessly except for the quantization noise above.

---

### 2. Per-CF tier policy

| CF | Hot | Warm | Cool | Cold |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS` | 8-bit | 7-bit | 5-bit | 3-bit |
| `CF_MEJEPA_DDA_SIGNALS` | 8-bit | 7-bit | 5-bit | 3-bit; ttl 365d |
| `CF_MEJEPA_FAILURE_EXEMPLARS` | 8-bit | 8-bit (operator-pinned) | 5-bit | 3-bit; ttl never |
| `CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK` | 8-bit | 8-bit | 5-bit | never aged out without operator override |
| `CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS` | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a (small JSON; not tier-compressed) |
| `CF_MEJEPA_WEIGHT_BLOBS` | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a (binary; not quantized) |
| Witness chain | n/a | n/a | n/a | Merkle compress > 90d |

---

### 3. Quota enforcement

Source: `quota.rs`. **115 GB hard cap** on `/mnt/d/contextgraph/`.

```rust
pub fn quota_status(d_root: &Path) -> Result<QuotaReport, QuotaError>;
pub fn quota_check_and_evict(d_root: &Path, target_gb: f64) -> Result<EvictionReport, QuotaError>;
```

| Threshold | Behavior |
|---|---|
| > 100 GB | Warning log |
| > 110 GB | Begin emergency eviction (`emergency_eviction_tick` @ 600s) |
| > 115 GB | Hard cap — refuse panel writes; alert operator (`MEJEPA_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`) |

Override via `CG_MEJEPA_DISK_QUOTA_GB` env var.

---

### 4. Quota allocation (115 GB cap)

| Slice | Budget | Tier policy |
|---|---|---|
| `storage/contextgraph-rocksdb/` (101 CFs) | 70 GB hot + warm; cool ~25 GB | 8/7/5/3 |
| `runtime/cgreality-shift-log/` | 5 GB rolling | per-session GC @ 30 days |
| `corpus/` | operationally large; managed outside the hot 115 GB cap when full runs active | archived after replacement verified |
| `storage/witness/` | 2 GB hot + Merkle-compressed >90d | per Phase 6 REQ-HYG-03 |
| `storage/cold/` | up to 25 GB cold blobs | 3-bit / 1-bit frozen |
| `storage/replay/` | 5 GB | replay-buffer policy |
| `logs/` | 2 GB rolling (daily rotation, 30-day retention) | logrotate |
| `fsv/` | 1 GB | per-crate FSV size cap |
| `exports/` | 2 GB rolling (90-day retention) | weekly snapshots |
| `state/` + `state/gold-labels/` | 100 MB | tiny but pinned |
| Models (`/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/`) | separate budget | not in 115 GB cap |
| **Total cap** | **115 GB** | hard ceiling |

---

### 5. Nightly GC (`gc_run_nightly`)

Source: `gc.rs::gc_run_nightly` → `GcReport`. Runs at 03:00 local (configurable). Operations:

```
gc_run_nightly:
  1. tier_demote_all: for each CF that participates, decay scores and demote
     panels whose access_count_30d dropped
  2. compact: rocksdb::compact_range for each CF
  3. evict: drop entries below sampling_weight floor (0.05) per replay policy
  4. prune: remove shift-log JSONLs > 30 days old
  5. verify: integrity check on a sample of recent witness entries
```

Writes `GcReport` to `/mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/gc/<date>/report.json`. FSV `gc_stress_fsv` proves 10,000-entry stress run.

---

### 6. Emergency eviction (`quota_check_and_evict`)

```rust
quota_check_and_evict(d_root, target_gb):
  if current_gb > target_gb:
    1. tier_demote_all (cool blobs → cold)
    2. Drop oldest cold-tier entries by `last_accessed`
    3. Prune shift-log JSONLs > 7 days old (aggressive)
    4. If still over, drop oldest replay-buffer entries
    5. Alert operator if eviction insufficient
```

`emergency_eviction_tick` runs every 600s in daemon mode.

---

### 7. Witness Merkle compression

Source: `witness_compress.rs::witness_compress_old_segments`. See [`15_witness_chain.md §5`](15_witness_chain.md).

| Function | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `witness_compress_old_segments(db, max_age_days=90)` | Roll up ≥256-entry segments into Merkle roots |
| `verify_witness_integrity(db)` | Full chain replay including compressed segments |
| `audit_verify_old_entry(entry_id, proof)` | Single-entry verification against compressed Merkle proof |

---

### 8. MCP tools (3, all shipped)

Source: `mcp.rs`.

| Tool | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `mejepa_gc_run` | Force `gc_run_nightly`; supports `--target-cfs` + `--force-eviction` |
| `mejepa_quota_status` | Per-slice breakdown + total vs cap |
| `mejepa_witness_compress` | Force compression of segments older than `up_to_offset` |

Plus 5 lower-level ops via `ops.rs::*` (programmatic, not MCP-callable directly):
`gc_run_nightly`, `quota_check_and_evict`, `heal_detect_drift`, `heal_retrain_if_needed`, `witness_compress_old_segments`, `witness_verify_integrity`, `tier_demote`, `tier_promote`.

---

### 9. Tier transition rules

Source: `tier.rs::tier_for_score`.

```rust
fn tier_for_score(access_count_30d: u32) -> Tier {
    match access_count_30d {
        0..=5     => Tier::Cold,    // 3-bit
        6..=30    => Tier::Cool,    // 5-bit
        31..=100  => Tier::Warm,    // 7-bit  (or 8-bit operator-pinned for failure exemplars)
        _         => Tier::Hot,     // 8-bit raw
    }
}

fn decayed_score(score: f32, days: u32) -> f32;  // decay^days
fn score_after_read(score: f32) -> f32;          // bump on read
fn encode_for_tier(vec: &[f32], tier: Tier) -> Vec<u8>;
fn decode_from_tier(bytes: &[u8], tier: Tier) -> Result<Vec<f32>>;
fn tier_demote(item) -> NewTier;
fn tier_promote(item) -> NewTier;
fn tier_demote_all(db) -> DemoteReport;
fn tier_promote_all(db) -> PromoteReport;
```

`tier_frozen.rs` handles the 1-bit / `frozen=true` mark for constellation cells past freshness + locked baselines.

---

### 10. FSV evidence

| FSV | Output |
|---|---|
| Tier compression round-trip | `/tmp/contextgraph-tier-compression-fsv/blob_{8,7,5,3}.bin` (790/694/502/310 bytes; cosine 0.99999/0.99997/0.99960/0.99192) |
| 10k-entry GC stress | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-hygiene-fsv/gc_stress_fsv/` mode 0600 |
| Merkle compression | `merkle_compression_fsv/` |
| Quota overflow | `quota_overflow_fsv/` |

---

### 11. Cross-references

[`04_database_schema.md`](04_database_schema.md) (CF-level tier policy), [`15_witness_chain.md`](15_witness_chain.md) (Merkle compression details), [`12_self_healing_scheduler.md`](12_self_healing_scheduler.md) (scheduler tickers), [`22_operator_runbook.md`](22_operator_runbook.md) (operator hygiene actions).

<a id="17-prediction-taxonomy-reference"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md (sha256:391050784e322c1623041900f208ac09b25ff9de2d9dc45fa22671704b7f1eed, 350 lines) ===== -->

## 17 — Prediction Taxonomy Reference

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/types.rs`, `docs/futurebuild/15_PREDICTION_TAXONOMY.md`.

> **Catalog of every prediction ME-JEPA emits.** The system always emits every category, every time — empty vecs when nothing is above threshold. Absence is itself a prediction.

---

### 1. The unified output schema

See [`11_inference_verify_lifecycle.md §3`](11_inference_verify_lifecycle.md) for the full `RealityPrediction` struct.

### 2. The 5 verdicts

| Verdict | Trigger |
|---|---|
| `Pass` | confidence ≥ ship-gate AND no high-severity Q4 AND every claim reconciles |
| `Fail` | ≥1 predicted_failed_test at confidence ≥ failure_threshold |
| `OutOfDistribution` | ood_score ≥ ood_threshold OR conformal width > width_threshold |
| `Abstain` | conformal interval too wide |
| `GuardRejected` | ≥1 embedder cosine to centroid < τ_m (TCT) |

---

### 3. Q3 — WHY (when verdict != Pass)

#### 3.1 `FailureModeClass` (27 variants + `Other`)

```rust
WrongAlgorithm, OffByOne, NullDeref, ResourceLeak, RaceCondition,
DeadlockPotential, InfiniteLoop, StackOverflow,
TypeError, SignatureMismatch, ImportMissing, SyntaxError, SemanticError,
AssertionViolation, ContractViolation, ApiMisuse, ConfigDrift,
EncodingError, DateTimeError, NumericalInstability, SerializationError,
SchemaMigrationGap, DependencyVersionConflict, PlatformAssumption,
CompilerVersionGap, PermissionsError, WrongTestUpdated,
Other(String),  // promoted to first-class variant when seen ≥10 times
```

#### 3.2 `RootCauseClass` (9 variants)

```rust
LogicError, StateError, ResourceError, ConcurrencyError, InterfaceError,
EnvironmentError, ConfigurationError, TestQualityError, Other,
```

#### 3.3 `PredictedFailureMode` struct

```rust
pub struct PredictedFailureMode {
    pub failure_class: FailureModeClass,
    pub chunk: ChunkId,
    pub line_range: (u32, u32),
    pub confidence: f32,
    pub severity: Severity,
    pub explanation: String,
    pub contributing_embedders: Vec<EmbedderId>,
    pub root_cause_class: RootCauseClass,
}
```

#### 3.4 `PredictedTestOutcome`

```rust
pub struct PredictedTestOutcome {
    pub test_id: TestId,
    pub current_outcome: TestOutcome,
    pub predicted_outcome: TestOutcome,
    pub delta_kind: TestDeltaKind,           // PassToFail | FailToPass | NewFailure | NewPass | NowSkipped | NowFlaky
    pub confidence: f32,
    pub why: PredictedFailureMode,           // back-reference
}
```

#### 3.5 `GuardViolation`

```rust
pub struct GuardViolation {
    pub embedder: EmbedderId,
    pub chunk: ChunkId,
    pub centroid_id: CentroidId,
    pub cosine: f32,
    pub threshold_tau_m: f32,
    pub deficit: f32,
}
```

#### 3.6 `ExemplarMatch`

```rust
pub struct ExemplarMatch {
    pub task_id: SwebenchTaskId,
    pub mutation_kind: MutationKind,
    pub similarity_score: f32,
    pub diff_summary: String,
    pub oracle_outcome: OracleVerdict,
    pub witness_hash: WitnessHash,
}
```

Retrieved from `CF_MEJEPA_FAILURE_EXEMPLARS` keyed by `(failure_mode_class, embedder_violation_signature)`.

---

### 5. Q1 — Existence reconciliation

#### 5.1 `ClaimKind` (9 variants)

```rust
Added(SymbolRef), Modified(SymbolRef), Removed(SymbolRef),

```

### 6. Q5 — Reality impact

#### 6.1 `PredictionCorrectness` (5 variants)

```rust
Aligned, UnderPredicted, OverPredicted, DivergentInClass, Surprise,
```

`Surprise` triggers a `SurpriseEvent` + active-learning queue entry.

---

### 7. Severity scale

```rust
pub enum Severity {
    Info,      // FYI; default at low confidence
    Low,       // worth knowing; ignore-by-default in agent UI
    Medium,    // surface in agent's prompt
    High,      // block "I'm done" until addressed
    Critical,  // block + escalate to operator alert
}
```

---

### 8. `ConformalInterval`

```rust
pub struct ConformalInterval {
    pub lower: f32,
    pub upper: f32,
    pub method: ConformalMethod,             // SplitConformal | Mondrian | Group
    pub coverage_target: f32,                // typically 0.90
    pub empirical_coverage: f32,             // current measured on calibration set
}
```

---

### 9. Per-head label sources

| Head | Primary label source | Secondary signal |
|---|---|---|
| Panel | frozen-instrument target on observed panel[t=2] | VICReg regularization |
| Oracle | Docker test results + CI runs | E_Test agreement |
| Failure-mode | rule-based extraction from pytest/cargo-test/jest tracebacks | patch-similarity nearest exemplar |
| Edge-case | coverage diff + symbolic-execution proxy on AST | per-language pattern catalog |
| Tech-debt | clippy / pylint / eslint warning delta + radon / lizard complexity | duplicate detector |
| Perf | pytest-benchmark / cargo bench / valgrind massif deltas | per-axis regression head |
| Security | bandit / cargo-audit / eslint-security / semgrep + CVE-db on dep deltas | per-class severity floor |
| Accuracy | test-output metric regex extraction | E_Reasoning ↔ E_Oracle agreement |
| Cost | CI minute counters + agent token telemetry + build-script timing + dep counter | per-axis regression head |
| Reasoning | E_Reasoning ⟂ E_Diff agreement / E_Reasoning vs E_Oracle | weak-supervised |

---

### 10. Rendering (markdown injection)

When `RealityPrediction` is injected into UserPromptSubmit context, rendered as compact markdown (≤ 4 KB cap):

```markdown
### Reality prediction (chunks affected since last prompt: 3)

**Verdict:** Fail (oracle pass confidence 0.07 [0.04, 0.12])

**Predicted failure modes (ranked):**
1. `astropy/modeling/separable.py:62-118` — **WrongAlgorithm**
   - predicted FAIL of `test_separability_compound_models` (confidence 0.93)
   - root cause: WrongAlgorithm (left-vs-right child indexing inverted)
   - violating embedders: E_TypeGraph, E_DataFlow
   - closest exemplar: psf__requests-2317 (similar inverted-traversal class)

**Predicted edge cases not covered by tests:**
- `EmptyInput`: empty CompoundModel branch (current code path raises IndexError)
- `DeepNesting`: mutually-recursive CompoundModel (predicted hang)

**Predicted latent bugs:** none above threshold.

**Predicted tech debt added (Medium):** 1 new TODO without owner at line 78.

**Predicted dead code (Low):** function `_legacy_separable` unreachable from any test.

**Predicted perf / security / accuracy / cost regressions:** none above threshold.

**Reasoning class:** MostlyCorrect (explanation matches diff except "I removed the recursion limit" — limit at line 91 still present)

**Claim reconciliation:**
- ✅ "I added function `_walk_compound`" — confirmed at line 142
- ⚠️ "I removed the recursion limit" — Modified (limit at line 91 still present)

**Reality impact:** 1 file changed as predicted; 0 unexpected; 2 tests would flip PASS→FAIL as predicted.

**To respond to a wrong prediction:** call `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` with `prediction_id=pred_01HXYZ...`.
```

---

### 11. Operating principles

1. **Always emit. Never elide.** Empty vec = "no concerns above threshold" — that's a prediction.
2. **Confidence-calibrated, not threshold-binarized.** Every prediction has a confidence; UI may filter by severity.
3. **Citation-grounded.** Every prediction names chunk_id + line_range + contributing embedders.
4. **Falsifiable.** Every prediction testable via oracle / coverage / static-analysis / benchmark.
5. **Provenance-bound.** Every prediction binds `witness_hash`; feedback events bind back.
6. **Schema-stable.** Adding new prediction class extends an enum; old fields don't change shape.
7. **Generic across languages.** Per-language realizations are config, not schema.

---

### 12. Cross-references

[`09_predictor_multi_head.md`](09_predictor_multi_head.md), [`11_inference_verify_lifecycle.md`](11_inference_verify_lifecycle.md), [`18_mcp_tool_reference.md`](18_mcp_tool_reference.md).

## 18 — MCP Tool Reference

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/dispatch.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/names.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/`, `docs/futurebuild/08_LIVE_PREDICTION_AGENT_API.md`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/_README.md`, `.claude/loop.md §4.2`.

> **206 MCP tools total** registered in `dispatch.rs:137-375`. 19 are ME-JEPA-specific (the live surface); the rest cover memory, embedder, search, learning, learner-state, DynamicJEPA, reality-loop / ccreality, autoresearch, provenance, daemon.

---

### 1. ME-JEPA tools (19 live, all shipped)

| Tool | Behavior | Key parameters | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mejepa_verify` | Take patch + context, return full `RealityPrediction` (Q1–Q5) | `patch` (unified diff or file_changes), `context { session_id, task_id?, repo_root, agent_response? }`, `include_provenance`, `include_explanations` | shipped |
| `mejepa_predict_latest` | Latest prediction for session from `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` | `session_id`, `max_age_seconds?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_constellation_inspect` | TCT cell lookup + force refit | `constellation_id?`, `category?`, `language?`, `force_refit`, `force_allow_stale` | shipped |
| `mejepa_heal_status` | Drift level + queued interventions + last A/B/C result | — | shipped |
| `mejepa_rollback_to` | Operator-forced rollback to prior model variant | `witness_offset`, `reason?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_load_learner_state` | Load saved learner-state (E15–E21) snapshot | `learner_id`, `checkpoint_id?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_routing_lookup` | Query (Language × EntityType) → embedder set | `language`, `entity_type` | shipped |
| `mejepa_vram_budget_report` | GPU VRAM accounting vs budget | — | shipped |
| `mejepa_gc_run` | Force nightly GC run | `target_cfs?`, `force_eviction?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_quota_status` | Disk usage vs 115 GB cap + per-slice breakdown | — | shipped |
| `mejepa_witness_compress` | Force Merkle compression of segments > 90 days | `up_to_offset` | shipped |
| `mejepa_observe_shift` | Manually trigger observe() over a specific shift_id | `shift_id`, `run_id` | shipped |
| `mejepa_subscriber_status` | Shift subscriber lag + latency p50/p99 + last panic | — | shipped |
| `mejepa_capture_audit` | Bind outer-loop SWE-bench attempt to corpus | `run_id`, `attempt` | shipped |
| `mejepa_eval_run` | Force holdout-eval; per-(category × language) correlation | `dataset_id?`, `model_id?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_ship_gate_status` | Per-cell ≥0.95 ship-gate status with shrinkage | — | shipped |
| `mejepa_active_learning_queue` | High-priority queue contents | `top_n?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_eval_build_graph` | Rebuild patch-similarity graph for cross-task transfer | `patch_ids?` | shipped |
| `mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi` | Weekly pairwise-MI audit with CSV readback | — | shipped |

Returns `{ sha256_before, sha256_after, lines_added, lines_removed, git_diff_stat, harness_transition_path, witness_chain_segment }`. Errors: `CCREALITY_EDIT_LOCK_HELD`. |

## 19 — CLI Reference

Source files covered: `crates/context-graph-cli/src/main.rs`, `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/`, `crates/context-graph-cli/src/bin/`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-corpus/src/bin/mejepa-corpus.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-train/src/bin/mejepa-train.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/bin/mejepa-heal-drill.rs`.

> **15 top-level commands; ~150 sub-subcommands.** Three binaries: `context-graph-cli` (main), `context-graph` (symlink alias), `blackbox` (BlackBoxFix ledger).

---

### 1. Global flags

| Flag | Type | Default | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `-v` / `--verbose` | count (0–3+) | 0 | Log level: 0→warn, 1→info, 2→debug, 3+→trace |

---

### 2. Command tree (top-level)

| Group | Sub-subcommands | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | 2 | Session state persistence |
| `hooks` | 8 | Claude Code hook entrypoints |
| `memory` | 4 | Context injection + memory capture |
| `topic` | 3 | HDBSCAN topic clustering |
| `divergence` | 1 | Divergence detection |
| `dynamicjepa` | 44 | DynamicJEPA full surface |
| `learning` | 7 | UTL learning events |
| `utl` | 7 | UTL learner-state |
| `setup` | (direct) | Initialize Claude Code hooks |
| `warmup` | (direct) | Pre-load 14 embedding models |
| `watch` | (direct) | Markdown file watcher |
| `export` | 2 | Parquet / JSONL exports |
| `backfill-e14` | (direct) | Backfill E14 BGE-M3 vectors |
| `blackbox` | 44+ | BlackBoxFix Phase 1 ledger |
| `ccreality` | 1 | Post-edit harness transition |

---

### 3. `session`

| Subcommand | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `restore-identity` | Restore session state from storage (TASK-SESSION-12) |
| `persist-identity` | Persist session state to storage (TASK-SESSION-13) |

### 4. `hooks`

Each invokes the corresponding `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/*.rs` handler. Bounded timeouts.

| Subcommand | Timeout | Flags |
|---|---|---|
| `session-start` | 5,000 ms | `--session-id`, `--previous-session-id?`, `--stdin`, `--format` |
| `pre-tool` | 500 ms FAST PATH (NO DB) | `--session-id` (req), `--tool-name`, `--stdin`, `--fast-path`, `--format` |
| `post-tool` | 3,000 ms | `--session-id` (req), `--tool-name`, `--success`, `--stdin`, `--format` |
| `prompt-submit` | 2,000 ms | `--session-id` (req), `--prompt`, `--stdin`, `--format` |
| `session-end` | 30,000 ms | `--session-id` (req), `--duration-ms`, `--stdin`, `--generate-summary`, `--format` |
| `pre-compact` | 20,000 ms | `--session-id` (req), `--stdin`, `--format` |
| `task-completed` | 20,000 ms | `--session-id` (req), `--stdin`, `--format` |
| `generate-config` | n/a | `--output-dir`, `--force`, `--hooks` (csv), `--shell` |

---

### 5. `memory`

| Subcommand | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `inject-context` | Inject context from memory store (UserPromptSubmit / SessionStart hook backend); `query?`, `--budget` |
| `inject-brief` | < 200-token brief for PreToolUse hook; reads `TOOL_DESCRIPTION` / `TOOL_NAME` env |
| `capture-memory` | Capture hook description (`HookDescription` source) |
| `capture-response` | Capture Claude responses (`ClaudeResponse` source) |

---

### 6. `topic`

| Subcommand | Flags |
|---|---|

#### 18.2 `mejepa-train` (in `context-graph-mejepa-train`)

Training driver. Reads dataset shards → trunk + heads forward + multi-loss + cert chain + holdout eval. Flags include batch size, LR, epochs, checkpoint output.

#### 18.3 `mejepa-heal-drill` (in `context-graph-mejepa`)

Synthetic drift drill: injects 90% → 84% drift over 1000 obs; scheduler detects within 1k more obs; A/B/C runs; winner promoted. Used as FSV harness.

#### 18.4 `mejepa-verify` (in `context-graph-mejepa`)

Inference CLI: `verify <patch> --context <ctx>` → `Verdict`.

## 20 — Hooks Layer

Source files covered: `.claude/settings.json`, `scripts/cc/hook_session_start.sh`, `scripts/cc/hook_pre_tool_use_edit.sh`, `scripts/cc/hook_post_tool_use_edit.sh`, `scripts/cc/hook_user_prompt_submit.sh`, `scripts/cc/hook_stop.sh`, `scripts/cc/hook_pre_compact.sh`, `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/`, `docs/futurebuild/08_LIVE_PREDICTION_AGENT_API.md §3`, `docs/futurebuild/06_REALITY_LOOP_REFACTOR.md §7`.

> **Hooks are the bridge from Claude Code's runtime to the daemon's continuous learning loop.** Every Edit/Write is captured. Every UserPromptSubmit gets an injected prediction (futurebuild). Every turn must end with `optimizer_record_recommendation`.

---

### 1. The 6 hook types

| Hook | Trigger | Timeout | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SessionStart` | conversation start | 5,000 ms | Inject active runtime root (D-drive) + reality-boundary rules |
| `UserPromptSubmit` | every user prompt | 2,000 ms | Inject last 3 shift entries + latest `RealityPrediction` markdown |
| `PreToolUse` Edit/Write | before each edit | 500 ms (FAST PATH, NO DB) | Record pre-edit SHA |
| `PostToolUse` Edit/Write | after each edit | 3,000 ms | Record after_sha + cargo check (Rust files) + git diff + shift-log JSONL append |
| `Stop` | end of turn | (synchronous) | Require `optimizer_record_recommendation` — block turn end |
| `PreCompact` | context compaction | 20,000 ms | SMAP narrative dump |

Additional Claude Code hooks (not currently used by ME-JEPA): `SessionEnd`, `TaskCompleted`.

---

### 2. `.claude/settings.json`

```jsonc
{
  "hooks": {
    "SessionStart": [
      {
        "matcher": ".*",
        "hooks": [{ "type": "command", "command": "scripts/cc/hook_session_start.sh", "timeout": 5000 }]
      }
    ],
    "UserPromptSubmit": [
      {
        "matcher": ".*",
        "hooks": [{ "type": "command", "command": "scripts/cc/hook_user_prompt_submit.sh", "timeout": 2000 }]
      }
    ],
    "PreToolUse": [
      {
        "matcher": "Edit|Write",
        "hooks": [{ "type": "command", "command": "scripts/cc/hook_pre_tool_use_edit.sh", "timeout": 500 }]
      }
    ],
    "PostToolUse": [
      {
        "matcher": "Edit|Write",
        "hooks": [{ "type": "command", "command": "scripts/cc/hook_post_tool_use_edit.sh", "timeout": 3000 }]
      }
    ],
    "Stop": [
      {
        "matcher": ".*",
        "hooks": [{ "type": "command", "command": "scripts/cc/hook_stop.sh" }]
      }
    ],
    "PreCompact": [
      {
        "matcher": ".*",
        "hooks": [{ "type": "command", "command": "scripts/cc/hook_pre_compact.sh", "timeout": 20000 }]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

---

### 3. `SessionStart`

| Input (env) | Output (injection) |
|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` | "ContextGraph reality loop is not currently running. Active runtime root: not set." (when no daemon) OR daemon-attached runtime root |

Behavior:
1. Resolve active runtime root: read `/mnt/d/contextgraph/state/cgreality/active_runtime_root` if present.
2. Emit reality-boundary rules: "never edit .env files, swebench official evaluators, tests/oracle_**, reward_specs, or verifiers."
3. Inject into Claude Code's session-init context.

Implementation: `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/session_start.rs`.

---

### 4. `PreToolUse` Edit/Write (FAST PATH, NO DB)

| Input (env) | Output |
|---|---|
| `TOOL_NAME`, `TOOL_INPUT` (JSON with `file_path`), `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` | (stderr line emitted as system-reminder): `Pre-edit reality: file=<rel-path> current_sha=<sha> current_line_count=<n>. Governed-path gate: REMOVED 2026-05-07; SHA recorded for provenance only.` |

Behavior:
1. **Read current file SHA-256** (cheap, no DB).
2. **Record line count**.
3. Emit pre-edit reality line for post-edit delta computation.
4. **DO NOT touch DB**. The governed-path gate was removed 2026-05-07 ("the AI needs to be able to do anything it needs to do").

Implementation: `scripts/cc/hook_pre_tool_use_edit.sh` + `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/pre_tool.rs`.

---

### 5. `PostToolUse` Edit/Write

| Input | Output |
|---|---|
| Pre-edit SHA from PreToolUse, current after-edit state | (stderr): `Post-edit reality: file=<path> after_sha=<sha>. No active runtime root exists, so no harness transition was recorded. Active-root source of truth: file:/mnt/d/contextgraph/state/cgreality/active_runtime_root.` |

Behavior (per `scripts/cc/hook_post_tool_use_edit.sh`):
1. **Compute after_sha** of the just-edited file.
2. **Run cargo check** for `crates/context-graph-cli/*.rs` files (release profile).
3. **Run `git diff`** (status, stat).
4. **Append shift-log JSONL line** to `/mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl`:

```jsonc
{
  "ts": "2026-05-11T22:05:00Z",
  "session_id": "01HXYZ...",
  "file": "docs/mejepasystem/00_INDEX.md",
  "before_sha": "missing",
  "after_sha": "eef12ba064723d7eb0d851a037c04ce37b8821a5efa939efe13364d27717c44a",
  "line_count_before": 0,
  "line_count_after": 173,
  "cargo_check_result": null,
  "git_diff_summary": null,
  "hook_id": "post-edit-2026-05-11T22-05-00Z"
}
```

5. **Record harness transition** (if active runtime root is set) via `context-graph-cli ccreality record-harness-transition`.

Implementation: `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/post_tool.rs` + `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/ccreality/mod.rs`.

---

### 6. `UserPromptSubmit`

| Input | Output (injection) |
|---|---|
| `CLAUDE_USER_PROMPT`, `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`, `TOOL_DESCRIPTION?` | Injected markdown into next agent prompt (≤ 4 KB cap per `agent-79-...md` decision) |

Current behavior (shipped, partial):
1. Read last 3 shift-log JSONL entries for this session.
2. Emit them as a compact summary.

**Futurebuild extension `TASK-PREDICT-001`** (Phase A):
1. Read `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` for this session.
2. If a prediction exists and is < 60s old, render markdown per [`17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md §10`](17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md):

```markdown
### Reality prediction (chunks affected since last prompt: N)

**Verdict:** Fail (oracle pass confidence 0.07 [0.04, 0.12])
...
**To respond to a wrong prediction:** call `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` with `prediction_id=...`.
```

3. Bound to ≤ 4 KB; if exceeding, truncate to top-5 per Q4 category. Full prediction queryable via `mejepa_predict_latest`.

---

### 7. `Stop`

Synchronous block: turn cannot end unless `optimizer_record_recommendation` MCP tool was called during the turn.

Behavior (per `scripts/cc/hook_stop.sh`):
1. Inspect ledger / decision artifacts for this turn.
2. If no recommendation recorded → emit error "Stop hook: recommendation required".
3. Otherwise → pass.

`optimizer_record_recommendation` schema requires: `run_id`, `attempt`, `claude_session_id`, `turn_number`, `status`, `target_code_unit`, `recommendation_type`, `hypothesis`, `confidence_score`, `test_plan`, `rollback_plan?`.

---

### 8. `PreCompact`

Context-compaction event. Dumps the current narrative for SMAP capture so post-compact agent inherits.

Behavior:
1. Read live conversation buffer.
2. Extract `decisions`, `discoveries`, `handoffs` into `./memory/journal/<date>--compaction-snapshot.md`.
3. Ensure all SMAP namespaces have recent entries (per CLAUDE.md §6 SMAP discipline).

---

### 9. Hook script implementation pattern

Each `scripts/cc/hook_*.sh` is a thin wrapper:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# Read JSON input from stdin (Claude Code convention)
input=$(cat)

# Forward to the CLI handler
context-graph-cli hooks <subcommand> --stdin "$input" --session-id "$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID" --format json
```

The CLI handler in `crates/context-graph-cli/src/commands/hooks/*.rs` does the actual work.

---

### 10. Output paths

Per `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`:

| Output | Path |
|---|---|
| Shift-log JSONLs | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl` |
| Daemon-side state | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/state/cgreality/active_runtime_root` |
| Hook logs (per-tick) | stderr → captured by Claude Code; rotated by host |

**`TASK-LOOP-007`**: Hook output paths migration from `/tmp/contextgraph-rust-reality-loop/cgreality-shift-log/` to `/mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/` — code shipped 2026-05-11; audit doc updated.

---

### 11. Retired hook behaviors

| Behavior | Status |
|---|---|
| Governed-path gate in `PreToolUse` | REMOVED 2026-05-07 (operator directive); current state correct — gutted to "reject empty/control-char paths only" |
| Drip a fresh `reality_run_attempt` | RETIRED — engine binary deleted; `CCREALITY_ENGINE_RETIRED` |
| Champion / promotion of best inner attempt | RETIRED post-pivot |
| Mode A/B switching in `loop.md` | RETIRED — see [`21_loop_contract.md`](21_loop_contract.md) |

---

### 12. Cross-references

[`13_data_flywheel.md`](13_data_flywheel.md) (shifts feed subscriber), [`18_mcp_tool_reference.md`](18_mcp_tool_reference.md) (`optimizer_record_recommendation`), [`21_loop_contract.md`](21_loop_contract.md) (per-turn flow).
