<!-- ===== BEGIN: 21_loop_contract.md (sha256:521d72cf2a91befd4ddb2781623c33153549bac54e7ec18564e2365765e69f76, 222 lines) ===== -->

## 21 — Loop Contract (Single Outer Loop Around Claude Code)

Source files covered: `.claude/loop.md` (current), `docs/futurebuild/14_NEW_LOOP_MD.md`, `docs/futurebuild/06_REALITY_LOOP_REFACTOR.md`, `CLAUDE.md §11`.

> **Per-tick contract**. Each `/loop` firing is one Claude Code turn. The turn (a) reads SMAP + futurebuild + spec status, (b) picks the highest-priority open work item (build OR capture), (c) executes end-to-end with FSV evidence, (d) records `optimizer_record_recommendation` + a SMAP `./memory/reports/...md` report, (e) ends.

---

### 1. Two work classes (no mode ceremony)

#### 1.1 Build (futurebuild deliverable)

Ship one item from `docs/futurebuild/13_TASK_INDEX.md` — typically lowest-numbered `status: open` task in the active phase per `12_IMPLEMENTATION_PHASES.md`. Each task file under `docs/futurebuild/tasks/` is atomic per `prdtospec.md` Level 4.

Deliverables per turn:
- Code + tests + FSV evidence
- Docs / spec / task status update if behavior or roadmap state changes
- `cargo build --release -p <crate>` + `cargo test -p <crate>` + `cargo clippy -p <crate>`
- No mocks; real synthetic inputs only; fail-closed errors

#### 1.2 Capture (attempt a SWE-bench Lite task)

Pick the next unresolved task from `tasks/swebench-lite/*.json` — lexicographic order; first task with no `mejepa_capture_audit` entry. **OR** when all 300 have audit, the task with lowest current ME-JEPA correlation on its (category × language) cell.

Attempt directly with Claude Code's Edit/Write/Bash — no inner LLM.

Each Edit → shift-log entry → daemon shift subscriber materializes Panel within 250ms → ME-JEPA `verify()` runs → `RealityPrediction` in `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` → next UserPromptSubmit hook injects markdown.

**Park rule**: 3+ consecutive non-resolved attempts → park; pick next. Parked tasks unblock when active-learning queue surfaces them.

---

### 2. Priority order

1. **Daemon health degraded** (subscriber lag > 500/min, scheduler flapping, drift catastrophic, witness corruption, quota near hard cap) → that takes precedence; surface as `blocked` if not fixable this turn.
2. **Phase-A futurebuild tasks** (Operational Unblock) until Phase A done.
3. **Phase-B/C/D/E/F futurebuild tasks** in order.
4. **Phase-G capture work** (SWE-bench attempts) in parallel with B–F when no urgent build item.
5. **Phase-H cleanup tasks** as opportunistic fillers.

If everything `status: done` and no capture work → `no_change + surface_to_operator: "phases A-H complete; choose scaling/domain target"`.

---

### 3. Per-tick orientation (the FIRST four actions)

```
1. cat ./memory/context/current-phase.md ./memory/context/mission-brief.md
2. cat docs/futurebuild/02_CURRENT_STATE_HONEST.md docs/futurebuild/12_IMPLEMENTATION_PHASES.md docs/futurebuild/13_TASK_INDEX.md
   cat docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/_README.md
3. cat ./memory/decisions/ ./memory/blockers/ (skip status:resolved) ./memory/handoff/
   read latest ./memory/reports/
4. mcp__cgreality__mejepa_subscriber_status + mejepa_heal_status + mejepa_quota_status
```

If a daemon health check fails → that may take precedence over the highest-priority task.

---

### 4. The 5-step iteration procedure

| Step | Action |
|---|---|
| 5.1 Orient | SMAP + futurebuild docs + spec status; daemon health |
| 5.2 Plan | Short internal plan; what ships? what's FSV? |
| 5.3 Execute | Implement task; OR attempt SWE-bench task. Subagents for parallel research; do file-reading + synthesis personally |
| 5.4 Verify | FSV per `docs/futurebuild/specs/FSV-PROTOCOL.md`: SoT readback + 3 boundary cases + cargo build/test/clippy |
| 5.5 Record | Dual-write: `optimizer_record_recommendation` + `./memory/reports/agent-{turn}-{role}--turn-{turn}-final-report.md` |

---

### 5. Core invariants (8)

| # | Invariant |
|---|---|
| 1 | **Reality-shift principle**: no alteration without current state; no alteration without delta back into perception. Detect external drift with `mcp__cgreality__reality_shift_compare_to_my_view`. |
| 2 | **GENERIC-ONLY** (ME-JEPA training): no per-task overfit; primitives must generalize across every SWE-bench task and language. You can reason task-specifically when attempting; never bake task-specifics into the model. |
| 3 | **No mocks**: real synthetic data; real Docker; real RocksDB; real tree-sitter; fail-closed errors with explicit codes. |
| 4 | **No HTTP from runtime**: MCP is the only network surface. `WebSearch` / `WebFetch` are research-only (read-only). |
| 5 | **No inner LLM** (post-2026-05-09): no `llama-cpp-2`, Qwen, Hermes, Ollama. Claude Code is the only agent. |
| 6 | **D-drive only** for bulk state (`docs/futurebuild/16`); C-drive only for code/docs/configs; `/tmp/` only for ephemeral test scratch. |
| 7 | **Self-healing / self-learning**: every primitive shipped preserves or strengthens daemon's continuous self-optimization. Reject changes that make ME-JEPA less updateable. |
| 8 | **Multi-head prediction always emits everything**: every `RealityPrediction` includes all Q1–Q5 surfaces; empty vecs populated. |

---

### 6. Output contract (what THIS turn MUST produce)

| Required output | Location |
|---|---|
| ✅ ONE `optimizer_record_recommendation` call | Stop hook enforces (status: `changed` / `no_change` / `blocked`) |
| ✅ ONE SMAP report | `./memory/reports/agent-{turn}-{role}--turn-{turn}-final-report.md` |
| ✅ Zero or more SMAP artifacts (write IMMEDIATELY on trigger) | `./memory/discoveries/`, `decisions/`, `blockers/`, `patterns/`, `handoff/` |
| ✅ Zero or more shift-log entries (auto-minted by hooks) | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/runtime/cgreality-shift-log/<session>.jsonl` |
| ✅ (if build) code + tests + FSV evidence + spec/task update | crate source + `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/<crate>-fsv/` |
| ✅ (if capture) `mejepa_capture_audit` MCP call + attempt summary | `tasks/swebench-lite/<task_id>` workspace |

Timestamps: `date -u +%FT%TZ` (ISO 8601 UTC).

---

### 7. SMAP discipline (`./memory/`)

8 namespaces:

| Namespace | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `context/` | mission, current phase, constraints — read first every turn |
| `decisions/` | locked architectural choices (do not contradict) |
| `discoveries/` | constraints, gotchas, edge cases learned during work |
| `handoff/` | concrete deliverables passed turn-to-turn |
| `journal/` | append-only chronological execution log |
| `blockers/` | unresolved issues with severity (resolved = update status, never delete) |
| `patterns/` | reusable conventions and standards |
| `reports/` | each turn's final report |

Every `.md` file requires YAML frontmatter:

```yaml
---
namespace: {context|decisions|discoveries|handoff|journal|blockers|patterns|reports}
agent_id: agent-{N}-{role}
timestamp: 2026-05-11T22:05:00Z
status: {active|resolved|superseded}
tags: comma-separated
---
```

Lifecycle: READ → ORIENT → WORK → WRITE → REPORT.

Per CLAUDE.md §6:
- **Resolved blockers stay** (`status: resolved`).
- **Superseded decisions stay** (`status: superseded`); link via `superseded_by:`.
- **Reports are append-only**; never delete.

---

### 8. Hard rules (the 10 most important)

1. Do what the operator asked, nothing more, nothing less.
2. Read `./memory/context/`, `./memory/decisions/`, futurebuild docs (02, 12, 13), specs `_README.md` at start of every turn.
3. Pick exactly one work item per priority order.
4. GENERIC-ONLY in the trained model; never bake task-specifics in.
5. No HTTP. No destructive git. No editing the deny set in `CLAUDE.md §8`.
6. No mocks; real data; fail-closed errors; FSV under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/`.
7. D-drive bulk only; C-drive code/docs/configs only; `/tmp/` ephemeral only.
8. Pair every recommendation JSON with a SMAP report.
9. Resolved blockers stay; superseded decisions stay; reports append-only.
10. Never silently violate a locked decision. If overriding, supersede explicitly.

---

### 9. Self-healing properties the loop preserves

Per `docs/futurebuild/14_NEW_LOOP_MD.md §7`:

| Property | Mechanism |
|---|---|
| Self-healing under drift | scheduler drift_tick + LoRA refresh + A/B/C |
| Self-learning from new shifts | scheduler observe_tick @ 250ms |
| Self-validating outputs | conformal interval + Gτ guard + `Verdict::GuardRejected` |
| Self-bounding storage | nightly GC + tier compression + 115 GB hard quota |
| Self-auditing provenance | witness chain + ed25519 + training certs |
| Self-correcting via active-learning | scheduler active_learning_tick consumes high-priority queue |
| Self-optimizing per-prediction | agent-feedback bumps sampling_weight |

Every commit message should mention which property it strengthens. Weakening any without an explicit pivoted-design note → recommendation must surface in `remaining_risks`.

---

### 10. Operator-driven skills

| Skill | Status |
|---|---|
| `/loop` | canonical per-tick skill (replaces all ccreality skills) |
| `/ccreality-smoke` | KEPT — verifies daemon + subscriber + GC boot |
| `/ccreality-readback` | KEPT — drift detection on past recommendations |
| `/ccreality-iterate` | RETIRED — superseded by `/loop` |
| `/ccreality-trigger-check` | RETIRED — inner-actor drip gone |

---

### 11. What the loop does NOT do anymore

| Behavior | Status |
|---|---|
| Drip `reality_run_attempt` | RETIRED (`CCREALITY_ENGINE_RETIRED`) |
| Spawn `cc-reality-harness-editor` | RETIRED |
| Diagnose via `cc-reality-diagnostician` | RETIRED |
| Mode A / Mode B switch | RETIRED — single loop, priority pick |
| Per-attempt wallclock budget for inner actor | RETIRED (inner actor gone) |
| Champion-attempt promotion | RETIRED (A/B/C now owned by heal scheduler) |

---

### 12. When `/loop` runs unattended

Defaults:
- Pick highest-priority Phase-A futurebuild task.
- If Phase A done, pick from Phase B in parallel with capture.
- If all build tasks complete, pick capture work.
- If everything `status: done` and no capture work → `no_change + surface_to_operator: "phases A-H complete; choose scaling/domain target"`.
- Never assume operator watching. Errors produce robust logs + clean blocker writes.

---

### 13. Bootstrap (fresh clone, first turn)

If `./memory/` does not exist:
```bash
mkdir -p ./memory/{context,decisions,discoveries,patterns,blockers,journal,reports,handoff}
```

If `/mnt/d/contextgraph/` missing or not writable: refuse with `MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE`. Daemon will not start.

Write initial `./memory/context/mission-brief.md` + `current-phase.md` reflecting post-pivot state. Re-read `CLAUDE.md` + `docs/futurebuild/00_INDEX.md`. Recommendation = `changed` if SMAP files written; `no_change` if already in place.

---

### 14. Cross-references

[`CLAUDE.md §11`](../../CLAUDE.md), [`docs/futurebuild/14_NEW_LOOP_MD.md`](../futurebuild/14_NEW_LOOP_MD.md), [`22_operator_runbook.md`](22_operator_runbook.md), [`20_hooks_layer.md`](20_hooks_layer.md).


<!-- ===== END: 21_loop_contract.md ===== -->

<a id="22-operator-runbook"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 22_operator_runbook.md (sha256:19bd6855d0627952e2e00ddf99d07c59f8642fb8e8c0e255219b820f9760dfa5, 229 lines) ===== -->

## 22 — Operator Runbook

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/19_OPERATOR_RUNBOOK.md`, `scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service`, `scripts/ensure_daemon.sh`.

> Day-to-day, week-to-week, incident-response operations for ME-JEPA-as-truth-system.

---

### 1. First-time setup (one-time)

```bash
# 1. Mount D drive
mkdir -p /mnt/d   # Windows host should auto-mount D: at /mnt/d under WSL

# 2. Create ContextGraph root tree
mkdir -p /mnt/d/contextgraph/{storage/{contextgraph-rocksdb,replay,cold,witness,train-certs,live-predictions,embedder-cache},runtime/{cgreality-shift-log,test-outcomes},logs,state/{locks,cgreality,gold-labels},fsv,exports/{active-learning,eval,pairwise-mi,load-tests,test-runs}}
mkdir -p /mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models

# 3. Place SHA-pinned model artifacts under /mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/
#    (Operator-provided per the per-embedder digest list)

# 4. Build
cd /home/cabdru/contextgraph
cargo build --release --workspace

# 5. Install systemd unit
cp scripts/systemd/mejepa-daemon.service ~/.config/systemd/user/
systemctl --user daemon-reload
systemctl --user enable --now mejepa-daemon

# 6. Verify
systemctl --user status mejepa-daemon
ls -la /mnt/d/contextgraph/state/locks/
tail /mnt/d/contextgraph/logs/mejepa-daemon.stdout.log

# 7. MCP smoke test
echo '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"tools/call","params":{"name":"mcp__cgreality__mejepa_quota_status","arguments":{}}}' \
    | nc -U /mnt/d/contextgraph/state/locks/mcp.sock
```

---

### 2. Daily ops (2–5 minutes)

```bash
# 2.1 Daemon health
mcp_call mejepa_daemon_status
# subscriber.lag_ms < 5000, heal.last_drift_class != Catastrophic,
# quota.used_gb < 110, vram.peak_gb < budget_gb

# 2.2 Operator alerts
ls /mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/eval/$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/ 2>/dev/null

# 2.3 Active-learning queue
context-graph-cli mejepa active-learning list --top-n 5
```

---

### 3. Weekly ops (15–30 minutes)

```bash
# Mondays
less /mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/eval/$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/weekly.md
# Look for: per-(category × language) ship-gate status, drift events,
# promotions/rollbacks, agent-feedback summary, storage trend

context-graph-cli mejepa active-learning list --top-n 20
# Label or dismiss each entry

# Every other week:
xdg-open /mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/pairwise-mi/$(ls /mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/pairwise-mi/ | tail -1)
# If pair MI > 0.85 sustained 2 audits: retire / recalibrate / accept

du -sh /mnt/d/contextgraph/
mcp_call mejepa_quota_status
```

---

### 4. Monthly ops (30–60 minutes)

```bash
ls -la /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/  # backups < 7 days old
context-graph-cli mejepa hygiene cold-audit
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_verify --depth full
# Quarterly soak test
git log --since="1 month ago" --oneline -- docs/futurebuild/ docs/ruvectorfindings/
```

---

### 5. Incident response

#### 5.1 `MEJEPA_DAEMON_FLAPPING` (> 5 restarts/5 min)

```bash
journalctl --user -u mejepa-daemon -n 200
tail -100 /mnt/d/contextgraph/logs/mejepa-daemon.stderr.log
```

Identify the flapping task, investigate, write blocker if unknown, restart.

#### 5.2 `MEJEPA_WITNESS_CHAIN_CORRUPTION`

```bash
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_diff --since-last-verified
# If auto-repair failed:
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_repair_legacy --force
# Last resort: restore last known-good RocksDB checkpoint
```

#### 5.3 `MEJEPA_DRIFT_CATASTROPHIC`

```bash
mcp_call mejepa_heal_status
less /mnt/d/contextgraph/exports/eval/.../weekly.md
# If retrain reasonable:
mcp_call mejepa_promote_approval --promotion-id <id> --approve true
# If suspicious:
mcp_call mejepa_promote_approval --promotion-id <id> --approve false --reason "..."
```

#### 5.4 `MEJEPA_QUOTA_EXCEEDED`

```bash
mcp_call mejepa_quota_status
mcp_call mejepa_gc_run --force-eviction true
context-graph-cli mejepa hygiene aggressive-cold-tier-down
```

#### 5.5 OOM / RSS climbing

```bash
systemctl --user restart mejepa-daemon
# Profile with valgrind during repro
# Write high-severity blocker
```

#### 5.6 `MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE`

```bash
mount | grep /mnt/d
# If unmounted: remount; restart daemon
# If corrupted: see 23_disaster_recovery.md §3
```

#### 5.7 Silently wrong predictions

```bash
mcp_call mejepa_ship_gate_status
# If specific cell regressing:
mcp_call mejepa_constellation_inspect --force-refit --category <c> --language <l>
# If pervasive: file blocker; consider full retrain
```

---

### 6. Configuration tuning

| Knob | Default | Where | When to change |
|---|---|---|---|
| Drift thresholds | 88/85/80% | daemon config | when production calibrates |
| Subscriber tick | 250 ms | daemon config | rarely; tighten if lag > 5s p50 |
| Promote tick | 3600 s | daemon config | rarely |
| Storage hard quota | 115 GB | daemon config | hardware change |
| GC schedule | nightly 03:00 local | systemd timer | operator preference |
| Active-learning surface | top-N=10, period=600s | daemon config | operator capacity |
| Per-head λ_h | per `10 §8` defaults | trained-in | only via supersession |

Configuration in `~/.config/mejepa/daemon.toml` (futurebuild). Edits require daemon restart.

---

### 7. Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Likely cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Daemon won't start | D-drive missing | mount + verify with `ls /mnt/d/contextgraph/` |
| Daemon won't start | model file missing | check digest report; place under `/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/` |
| Daemon won't start | pid lock held | `pkill -f context-graph-mcp.*--daemon` |
| Predictions never appear | subscriber lagged | `mejepa_subscriber_status`; check shift-log dir; check tokio runtime |
| Predictions appear but agent doesn't see them | injection hook misconfigured | check `.claude/settings.json hooks.UserPromptSubmit`; test manually |
| `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` rate-limited | > 100/hr/agent | wait or use different agent_id |
| Sampling weight not bumping | scheduler not consuming queue | check active-learning tick firing; check CF |
| Disk filling fast | shift-log retention broken | check GC logs; `mcp_call mejepa_gc_run` |
| Wrong predictions on Python, Rust fine | per-language drift | `mejepa_ship_gate_status --by-language python`; per-language LoRA refresh |
| `nvidia-smi` reports N/A | WSL passthrough | per `TECH-OBSERVABILITY §5` |

---

### 8. Every operator MCP tool (reference)

See [`18_mcp_tool_reference.md`](18_mcp_tool_reference.md). Key tools: `mejepa_daemon_status`, `mejepa_subscriber_status`, `mejepa_heal_status`, `mejepa_quota_status`, `mejepa_vram_budget_report`, `mejepa_eval_run`, `mejepa_ship_gate_status`, `mejepa_active_learning_queue`, `mejepa_eval_build_graph`, `mejepa_audit_pairwise_mi`, `mejepa_constellation_inspect`, `mejepa_gc_run`, `mejepa_witness_compress`, `mejepa_promote_approval`, `mejepa_rollback_to`, `optimizer_witness_chain_verify`, `optimizer_recall_recommendations`.

---

### 9. Every operator CLI command (reference)

```bash
context-graph-cli mejepa active-learning list [--top-n N]
context-graph-cli mejepa active-learning label <id> <label> <reason>
context-graph-cli mejepa active-learning dismiss <id> <reason>
context-graph-cli mejepa hygiene cold-audit
context-graph-cli mejepa hygiene aggressive-cold-tier-down
context-graph-cli mejepa rollback --version <version>
context-graph-cli mejepa eval run --force
context-graph-cli mejepa eval ship-gate
context-graph-cli mejepa daemon status
context-graph-cli mejepa daemon restart
context-graph-cli mejepa daemon stop
```

---

### 10. Escalation path

If §5 doesn't resolve within 1 day:

1. Write high-severity blocker in `memory/blockers/<date>--<short-name>.md` with severity, reproducible scenario, attempted fixes.
2. If user-facing degradation: `mcp_call mejepa_pause_predictions --duration-mins 60`; agent gets `Verdict::Abstain` until resumed.
3. Investigate root cause; fix; resume.
4. Systemic: file project-repo issue with daemon logs, training certs, failing prediction's witness hash.

---

### 11. Cross-references

[`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md), [`12_self_healing_scheduler.md`](12_self_healing_scheduler.md), [`23_disaster_recovery.md`](23_disaster_recovery.md), [`18_mcp_tool_reference.md`](18_mcp_tool_reference.md).

<a id="23-disaster-recovery"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 23_disaster_recovery.md (sha256:24016e850a0bcc7262ff39d2bb86470e73062db4a0b6e9c8010da5719d394309, 216 lines) ===== -->

## 23 — Disaster Recovery + Backup

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/20_DISASTER_RECOVERY_AND_BACKUP.md`, `docs/futurebuild/21_COLD_START_AND_BOOTSTRAP.md`, `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`.

> Backup strategy + restore procedures + DR drill for the D-drive state.

---

### 1. What lives on D-drive (recovery scope)

All bulk durable state per [`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md §4`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md) and `docs/futurebuild/16_STORAGE_LOCATION_POLICY.md`:

| Class | Recoverability | Backup priority |
|---|---|---|
| RocksDB workspace | Critical — predictor weights + corpus + train certs + witness chain + agent feedback | **DAILY** |
| Witness chain (`storage/witness/`) | Sacred — cryptographic provenance | **DAILY** + verified |
| Phase 0 mutation corpus (`corpus/`) | Regenerable from `tasks/swebench-lite/*.json` + operators | **WEEKLY** |
| Operator gold labels (`state/gold-labels/`) | Critical — permanent operator labels | **DAILY** |
| Embedder models (`/mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/`) | Recoverable via SHA-pinned re-download | **WEEKLY** (or on-change) |
| Shift logs (`runtime/cgreality-shift-log/`) | Recent recoverable; older expendable | rolling 7 days |
| FSV evidence (`fsv/`) | Regenerable via re-running tests | not backed up (rolling) |
| Eval reports (`exports/eval/`) | Historical — keep for trend analysis | **WEEKLY** |
| Daemon logs (`logs/`) | Operational history | rolling 30 days |

---

### 2. Backup strategy

#### 2.1 Daily snapshot

```bash
# Nightly cron (operator-scheduled)
rsync -aL --delete \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/ \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/storage/

rsync -aL --delete \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph/state/gold-labels/ \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/gold-labels/

rsync -aL --delete \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph/state/cgreality/ \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/cgreality/

# Verify after copy
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_verify --depth full
```

Daily snapshots kept 30 days; one weekly snapshot kept 1 year.

#### 2.2 RocksDB checkpoint

Use RocksDB's atomic `CreateCheckpoint` API:

```rust
let checkpoint = rocksdb::checkpoint::Checkpoint::new(&db)?;
checkpoint.create_checkpoint(&backup_path)?;
```

This creates hardlinks where possible (cheap, atomic).

#### 2.3 Witness chain integrity verification post-backup

```bash
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_verify --backup-path /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/$(date +%Y-%m-%d)/storage/witness/
```

Must return `valid=true`; else discard backup + alert.

#### 2.4 Corpus backup (weekly)

```bash
tar -cz --use-compress-program="zstd -9" \
    -f /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/$(date +%Y-%V)/corpus-swebench-lite.tar.zst \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus/swebench-lite-python-300x8-v1-*/
```

---

### 3. Restore procedures

#### 3.1 Full restore (D-drive failure)

```bash
# 1. Stop daemon
systemctl --user stop mejepa-daemon

# 2. Replace /mnt/d/contextgraph/ with last good snapshot
rsync -aL --delete \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph-backups/<date>/storage/ \
    /mnt/d/contextgraph/storage/

# 3. Verify witness chain
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_verify --depth full

# 4. Restart daemon
systemctl --user start mejepa-daemon

# 5. Verify health
mcp_call mejepa_daemon_status
```

#### 3.2 Single-CF corruption

```bash
# Stop daemon
systemctl --user stop mejepa-daemon

# Restore single CF from RocksDB checkpoint
# (requires offline rocksdb tool)
rocksdb_repair <path>

# Or selectively restore
# Replace specific CF directory in checkpoint with snapshot version

systemctl --user start mejepa-daemon
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_verify
```

#### 3.3 Model artifact missing

```bash
# Daemon refuses to start with MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_DIGEST_MISMATCH
# Re-download SHA-pinned model:
operator_provided_script <embedder_id> <expected_sha>
# Place under /mnt/d/cabdru/contextgraph/models/<embedder_id>/
systemctl --user start mejepa-daemon
```

#### 3.4 Corpus regeneration

```bash
# Phase 0 corpus deterministic via SplitMix64 seed
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mejepa-corpus -- generate \
    --corpus-id <new-corpus-id> \
    --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph \
    --tasks-dir tasks/swebench-lite \
    --output-root /mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus \
    --seed <seed-from-provenance>
```

---

### 4. Cold start (fresh deploy, no history)

Per `docs/futurebuild/21_COLD_START_AND_BOOTSTRAP.md`:

| State | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `CF_MEJEPA_PANELS` empty | Subscriber-empty path; `mejepa_predict_latest` returns deterministic placeholder verdict |
| `CF_MEJEPA_WEIGHT_BLOBS` empty | Load baseline weights from `models/baseline-mejepa-checkpoint/` (operator-provided); seed Stage 0 cert |
| Constellation empty | Refuse Gτ guard checks; `Verdict::OutOfDistribution` until first constellation built (after corpus generated) |
| Conformal calibration N=0 | Refuse with `MEJEPA_CALIBRATION_INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES`; pause prediction emission |
| No SMAP `./memory/` | Auto-create on first turn (per loop.md §9) |
| No `tasks/swebench-lite/` | Recoverable from upstream SWE-bench Lite release (per `BOOT-002`) |

**Cold-start widening**: per `TASK-BOOT-001`, the conformal interval is **artificially widened** during the first 1000 attempts until calibration accumulates. Predictions are made but flagged `confidence_interval.empirical_coverage = cold_start_placeholder`.

---

### 5. DR drill (quarterly)

Per `docs/futurebuild/20_DISASTER_RECOVERY_AND_BACKUP.md §5`:

```
1. Pick a recent snapshot (1-7 days old)
2. Provision a fresh D-drive volume
3. Restore from snapshot
4. Boot daemon
5. Verify:
   - witness chain valid
   - mejepa_predict_latest returns answers
   - mejepa_ship_gate_status returns reasonable per-cell status
   - 100 synthetic verify() calls succeed
   - drift detector reports valid baseline
6. Compare restored state vs source: should be byte-equivalent (RocksDB) or hash-equivalent (everything else)
```

Schedule: 1st Monday of each quarter. Document outcome in `memory/journal/<date>--dr-drill.md`.

---

### 6. Operator gold labels (special handling)

`state/gold-labels/` is **permanent + critical**. Operator-confirmed labels (per active-learning queue) are gold-standard:

- Always backed up daily.
- Never auto-deleted (`gold-labels.ttl = never` regardless of hygiene policy).
- Sampling weight × 6 in BatchSampler.
- Loss against operator-override examples gets Fisher-protected.
- Treat as the bedrock of model behavior.

---

### 7. Witness chain auto-repair

`optimizer_witness_chain_repair_legacy` handles pre-v2 format migration. For active-format corruption:

```bash
# 1. Identify corruption point
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_verify
# Returns issues: [{offset, error}]

# 2. Attempt auto-repair
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_repair_legacy --offset <corruption_offset> --dry_run true
# If safe to apply:
mcp_call optimizer_witness_chain_repair_legacy --offset <corruption_offset> --dry_run false

# 3. If repair fails, restore from backup
```

---

### 8. Cross-references

[`15_witness_chain.md`](15_witness_chain.md), [`16_hygiene_tier_compression.md`](16_hygiene_tier_compression.md), [`22_operator_runbook.md`](22_operator_runbook.md), [`14_corpus_phase0.md`](14_corpus_phase0.md).


<!-- ===== END: 23_disaster_recovery.md ===== -->

---

<a id="24-security-adversarial"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 24_security_adversarial.md (sha256:6e8a4076600565ddcdc283f64c17b9c446b5b3eb3460b8d8c9bb19505555f29d, 164 lines) ===== -->

## 24 — Security + Adversarial Robustness

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/22_ADVERSARIAL_ROBUSTNESS.md`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §security_requirements`, `CLAUDE.md §7-§8`.

---

### 1. Security rules (immutable)

| Rule | Source | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `SEC-MEJEPA-01` | constitution | Validate all MCP tool inputs at boundary (panel dim, finite floats, file path canonicalization) |
| `SEC-MEJEPA-02` | constitution | Embedder weights load from pinned SHA-256 digests; refuse on mismatch |
| `SEC-MEJEPA-03` | constitution | Witness-chain entries signed with ed25519 keypair in operator-controlled keyring; private key never leaves operator hardware |
| `SEC-MEJEPA-04` | constitution | Constellation centroids loaded with `corpus_sha + frozen_at`; refuse stale > 90 days without operator override |
| `SEC-MEJEPA-05` | constitution | Docker oracle invocations sanitize task_id + run_id (Docker-name-safe regex; reject `+`, spaces, control chars) |
| `SEC-MEJEPA-06` | constitution | No prompt injection from training corpus into MCP tool responses; CoT artifacts encoded as opaque embeddings via E17, never echoed verbatim |
| `SEC-MEJEPA-07` | constitution | FSV evidence files mode 0o600; never world-readable |

---

### 2. CLAUDE.md path discipline

| Rule | Source |
|---|---|
| Never commit secrets / credentials / `.env` files | `CLAUDE.md §7` |
| Validate input at system boundaries | `CLAUDE.md §7` |
| Sanitize file paths to prevent directory traversal | `CLAUDE.md §7` |
| Path-policy gate REMOVED 2026-05-07 (operator directive) | `CLAUDE.md §8` — path discipline is now BEHAVIORAL |
| Never edit: `.env*`, `swebench/**`, `tests/oracle_**/**`, `crates/context-graph-benchmark/datasets/swe_bench_*/**`, `crates/*/src/reward_specs/**`, `crates/*/src/verifiers/**`, `*.pem`, `*.key`, `**/credentials.json` | `CLAUDE.md §8` |

---

### 3. Adversarial threat model

Per `docs/futurebuild/22_ADVERSARIAL_ROBUSTNESS.md`:

| Threat | Detection | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| **Adversarial patch** crafted to fool predictor into `Pass` | Gτ guard (TCT) + OOD score + multi-head agreement check | `Verdict::GuardRejected` or `OutOfDistribution`; predictor cannot Goodhart against the constellation (no gradient flow through centroids) |
| **Hardcoded secret in patch** | E_StaticAnalysis + secret-detection regex (`SEC-MEJEPA-pred-secret`) | `predicted_security_concerns.HardcodedSecret`; redact in injection (per `D3`) |
| **Prompt injection via training corpus** | E17 encodes prose as opaque embedding; never echoes verbatim | `SEC-MEJEPA-06` |
| **Patch with malicious dependency** | E_StaticAnalysis + cargo-audit/cargo-deny + CVE-db | `predicted_security_concerns.*` |
| **Coverage-game** (claim test passes by deleting assertions) | `DeleteTestCall` mutation category in corpus trains predictor to flag | `FailureModeClass::WrongTestUpdated` |
| **Cross-language label injection** | label gating per language (`TASK-PREDICT-LABEL-011`) | reject cross-lang labels at trainer boundary |
| **Two-person rule bypass attempt** (single-operator catastrophic promote) | `mejepa_promote_approval` requires operator action | `SEC-005` two-person rule for Catastrophic promotions |
| **Witness chain tamper** | hash chain + ed25519 + Merkle | `optimizer_witness_chain_verify` periodic (10k attempts) |

---

### 4. Secret redaction pre-embed

Per `TASK-PREDICT-010-secret-redaction-pre-embed`. Before chunks reach embedders:

1. Detect potential secrets via regex/entropy heuristics:
   - High-entropy strings (≥ 4.5 bits/char) of length ≥ 16
   - Known patterns: `AWS_*=*`, `AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}`, `ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}`, `xoxb-*`, JWT, private key headers (`-----BEGIN`)
2. Replace with `<REDACTED_SECRET_TYPE>` token before embedding.
3. Log redaction event to `CF_AUDIT_LOG` keyed by `(memory_uuid, ts)`.
4. **Never emit the secret in `RealityPrediction.predicted_security_concerns.explanation`** — only "hardcoded secret detected at chunk X line Y".

---

### 5. Cross-language label gating

Per `D10`, `TASK-PREDICT-LABEL-011-cross-language-label-gating`. Issue: a Python failure mode (e.g., `DefaultValueLeakage` — mutable default arg) applied via patch-similarity graph to a Rust patch is **noise**.

Gate at trainer boundary: when a label is propagated from corpus exemplar X to current patch Y via patch-similarity, require `language(X) == language(Y)`. Reject cross-language propagation with `MEJEPA_TRAIN_CROSS_LANG_LABEL_REJECTED`.

---

### 6. Concurrent agent edits (race conditions)

Per `D11`, `23_multi_agent_isolation.md §3`. Shift log is per-session JSONL; external mtime conflicts across sessions detected via `reality_shift_compare_to_my_view`:

```rust
mcp_call reality_shift_compare_to_my_view {
    session_id: "my-session",
    files: ["src/foo.py"],
}
// returns: { external_drift_detected: bool, divergences: [...] }
```

If `external_drift_detected=true`, re-perceive before next edit. Continued editing on stale state is a violation of the reality-shift principle.

---

### 7. Audit trail integrity

| Surface | Protection |
|---|---|
| Witness chain (`storage/witness/witness-chain.bin`) | Append-only; SHAKE-256 hash chain; ed25519 segment signing; Merkle compression > 90d |
| `CF_AUDIT_LOG` | Append-only; bound by `tool_use_id` via `CF_TOOL_CALL_INDEX` |
| `CF_MERGE_HISTORY` | Permanent (never expires); operator-pinned |
| `CF_IMPORTANCE_HISTORY` | Per-(memory, ts) delta log |
| `CF_ENTITY_PROVENANCE` | Per-entity extraction provenance |
| Training certs (`CF_MEJEPA_TRAIN_CERTS`) | Hash-chained via `parent_witness_hash → self_hash` |
| Agent feedback (`CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK`) | Bound by `witness_hash` to original prediction; both sides immutable |
| Active-pointer changes (`CF_MEJEPA_ACTIVE_POINTERS`) | Atomic + witness-chain entry on every promotion |

---

### 8. Operator authorization model

| Action | Authorization |
|---|---|
| Non-Catastrophic A/B/C promotion | automatic via promotion gate |
| Catastrophic A/B/C promotion | requires `mejepa_promote_approval` from operator |
| Force constellation refit | operator-only (`mejepa_constellation_inspect --force-refit`) |
| Force allow stale constellation | operator-only (`--force-allow-stale`) |
| Force rollback to prior model | operator-only (`mejepa_rollback_to`) |
| Retire/recalibrate embedder slot | operator (after MI audit) |
| Disable retired E5/E11 slots | hardcoded; bypass requires explicit operator override flag |
| Force GC eviction | operator (`mejepa_gc_run --force-eviction true`) |
| Pause predictions | operator (`mejepa_pause_predictions --duration-mins`) |
| Mark `OperatorOverride` on active-learning entry | operator (×6 sampling weight + Fisher-protected) |

---

### 9. Privacy considerations

| Concern | Treatment |
|---|---|
| Sensitive code in agent's patches | E17 affect-text encodes prose as opaque embedding; secrets redacted pre-embed (§4) |
| Operator labels in `state/gold-labels/` | Daily backup; permanent; mode 0o600 |
| Daemon logs | Rotated 30d; live under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/logs/` (operator-private) |
| Witness chain | Cryptographic; signed by operator keyring; private key never in code |
| Models | Loaded from operator-controlled D-drive; not auto-fetched from HF |

---

### 10. CI security lints

| Lint | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `TASK-OBS-005` C-drive path lint | Forbid `/home/`, `~/.cache/`, `/tmp/contextgraph-` in code |
| `TASK-SEC-006` markdown hardening | Sanitize markdown in prediction-injection rendering (no script tags) |
| `TASK-SEC-007` D-drive permissions | Enforce `0700` on `state/gold-labels/`, `0600` on FSV evidence files |
| `TASK-SEC-008` hook script lint | Validate `scripts/cc/hook_*.sh` follow set -euo pipefail + JSON-stdin pattern |
| `cargo audit` (CI) | Per-PR check for dep CVEs |
| `cargo deny` (CI) | License + dep-allowlist enforcement |

---

### 11. Reality-boundary enforcement

`CLAUDE.md §8` — though the deny gate is REMOVED from code, the rule stands behaviorally:

| Path | Reason |
|---|---|
| `.env`, `.env.*` | secrets |
| `swebench/**` | upstream evaluator (DO NOT MODIFY) |
| `tests/oracle_**/**` | benchmark fixtures |
| `crates/context-graph-benchmark/datasets/swe_bench_*/**` | benchmark data |
| `crates/*/src/reward_specs/**` | reward specs |
| `crates/*/src/verifiers/**` | verifiers |
| `*.pem`, `*.key`, `**/credentials.json` | secrets |

Editing one of these for legit operator-directed task: ask operator to confirm first.

---

### 12. Cross-references

[`15_witness_chain.md`](15_witness_chain.md), [`25_multi_agent_isolation.md`](25_multi_agent_isolation.md), [`22_operator_runbook.md`](22_operator_runbook.md) (incident response).


<!-- ===== END: 24_security_adversarial.md ===== -->

<a id="25-multi-agent-isolation"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 25_multi_agent_isolation.md (sha256:4bb3ad5327f5a49240ab3b7f8d9360cf16bdb348ad8f2cf379bcab2022dcadac, 144 lines) ===== -->

## 25 — Multi-Agent Isolation

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/23_MULTI_AGENT_ISOLATION.md`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber/`, `crates/context-graph-mcp/src/handlers/tools/reality_loop/shift_log.rs`.

> Multi-tenant + multi-session isolation for concurrent agents sharing the same daemon.

---

### 1. Isolation primitives

| Surface | Isolation key |
|---|---|
| Shift log JSONL | `session_id` (per-session file) |
| `CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` | `session_id` (latest) + `(session_id, prediction_id)` (history) |
| `CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK` | `session_id` (byte offset per JSONL) |
| `CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK` | `(prediction_id, agent_id, ts)` |
| EditLock | `session_id` (per-session, in `harness_apply_line_window_edit`) |
| `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` rate limit | `agent_id` (100/hour) |
| Active-learning queue entries | `(prediction_id, agent_id)` tagged |
| BlackBox ledger | `tenant_id` (BlackBoxFix-level) |

---

### 2. Session ID lifecycle

```
[Claude Code session start]
   → SessionStart hook generates UUID-style session_id (`01HXY...`)
   → Persisted in /mnt/d/contextgraph/state/cgreality/active_runtime_root (per active session)

[Each tool call]
   → CLAUDE_SESSION_ID env var passed through

[On hook fire]
   → context-graph-cli hooks <subcommand> --session-id <id>
```

`session_id` is the cross-cutting key for shift log, predictions, EditLock, watermark, and active-learning entries.

---

### 3. Concurrent edits to same file across sessions

Per `docs/futurebuild/22 §6` + `docs/futurebuild/23 §3`:

| Scenario | Detection | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| Agent A edits `foo.py` at SHA `x` → after_sha `y`; Agent B then reads `foo.py` and sees `y` (current). | Normal flow. | A's prediction lands first; B sees current state via PreToolUse SHA. |
| Agent A and Agent B concurrently start an Edit on `foo.py`. | `harness_apply_line_window_edit` acquires per-session EditLock. | One waits via `CCREALITY_EDIT_LOCK_HELD`. |
| Agent A's session crashes mid-edit, leaving stale lock. | Lock has TTL (default 5 min); auto-released. | Subsequent A or B acquires lock. |
| External-to-system file change (manual edit, IDE save) between sessions. | `reality_shift_compare_to_my_view {session_id, files}` returns `external_drift_detected=true`. | Both sessions must re-perceive before continuing per reality-shift principle. |

---

### 4. Per-agent rate limits

| Tool | Limit | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` | 100/hour/agent_id | anti-DoS; agents shouldn't be feedback-spamming |
| `mejepa_verify` | unbounded; CUDA queue serializes | predictor forward GPU-bound; serialization is at GPU |
| `mejepa_predict_latest` | unbounded | pure RocksDB read |
| `mejepa_explain_prediction` | unbounded | RocksDB read + light rerank |

Rate limit data structure: per-`agent_id` rolling-window counter in `CF_MEJEPA_AGENT_FEEDBACK` index.

---

### 5. Per-session shift subscriber consumption

The shift subscriber is **NOT per-session** — there's one supervised tokio task that tails ALL session JSONLs. Per-session state lives in:

- `CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK` keyed by `session_id` (byte offset per JSONL)
- `InstrumentCache` keyed by `(slot, file_sha)` — shared across sessions (file SHA collisions imply same content)

On restart: `ShiftSubscriber::open` reconstructs ALL session cursors from CF_MEJEPA_SHIFT_WATERMARK and resumes tailing from the persisted byte offsets.

---

### 6. Per-session predictions

`CF_MEJEPA_LIVE_PREDICTIONS` has dual indexing:

```
key                                            → value
(session_id, latest)                          → RealityPrediction (overwritten on each verify)
(session_id, prediction_id, ts)               → RealityPrediction history
```

`mejepa_predict_latest` reads the `latest` key. `mejepa_inspect_prediction` (futurebuild) reads from history by `prediction_id`.

---

### 7. Operator session

The **operator** doesn't have a Claude Code `session_id`. Operator actions go through:
- MCP tools called directly (via `nc -U /mnt/d/contextgraph/state/locks/mcp.sock` or CLI wrappers)
- CLI commands (`context-graph-cli mejepa ...`)

Operator labels in `state/gold-labels/` are tagged `operator_override=true` but not tied to a session ID.

---

### 8. Cross-session leakage prevention

| Path | Leakage prevention |
|---|---|
| Shift subscriber → observe() | every shift carries `session_id`; observe() does NOT discriminate per session (training data unified) — predictions are unified BUT served per-session |
| Predictions back to agent | strictly per-session via `mejepa_predict_latest(session_id)` |
| Agent feedback | bound to `(prediction_id, agent_id)`; agent A cannot record feedback against agent B's prediction (validated against `RealityPrediction.session_id`) |
| Active-learning queue | shared queue; operator can label entries from any session; no per-session filtering needed |

---

### 9. Multi-node / multi-machine deployment (out of scope)

Per `docs/futurebuild/23 §6`: implicitly single-machine v1 deployment. Multi-node is future work; would require:

- Distributed RocksDB (or replication)
- Cross-machine witness chain consensus
- Shared embedder model store (CDN + SHA pinning)
- Cross-machine quota enforcement

Not blocking v1 (single-machine is the design point).

---

### 10. Tenant isolation (operator-level, BlackBoxFix)

The `blackbox` binary (separate from MCP daemon) is multi-tenant via `tenant_id`:

| Surface | Per-tenant |
|---|---|
| BlackBox ledger | per-tenant SQLite under `~/.blackbox/<tenant>/` |
| Episode export | per-tenant |
| Enterprise controls | per-tenant |
| SIEM export | per-tenant audit trail |

ME-JEPA itself is **single-tenant** (single operator hardware); tenancy is BlackBoxFix Phase 1 only.

---

### 11. Cross-references

[`13_data_flywheel.md`](13_data_flywheel.md) (shift subscriber), [`18_mcp_tool_reference.md`](18_mcp_tool_reference.md) (per-tool isolation), [`24_security_adversarial.md`](24_security_adversarial.md) (rate limits + auth).


<!-- ===== END: 25_multi_agent_isolation.md ===== -->

---

<a id="26-testing-strategy"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 26_testing_strategy.md (sha256:8438ee6e5eb47558502830d6634a38c9bae9840fb607d44e24cea2aef4ade388, 202 lines) ===== -->

## 26 — Testing Strategy

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/18_INTEGRATION_AND_E2E_TESTING.md`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §testing_requirements`, `docs/futurebuild/specs/FSV-PROTOCOL.md`, `crates/*/examples/*_fsv.rs`, `crates/context-graph-test-utils/`.

> Testing is **fail-closed with real data**. No mocks. Every shipped primitive ships an FSV harness. Tests that pass while the project is broken are forbidden.

---

### 1. Coverage minimums

| Surface | Coverage minimum |
|---|---|
| Shipped crates | 80% line coverage |
| Safety-critical paths (`verify()`, `observe()`, `Gτ`, witness chain) | 90% |

---

### 2. Test categories

| Category | Purpose | Where |
|---|---|---|
| **Unit** | Per-instrument `encode()`, per-helper logic | `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` inline + `tests.rs` |
| **Integration** | Cross-module flows (panel materialization across 11 languages) | `tests/` directory + `cargo test --workspace` |
| **FSV (Forensic Source Verification)** | Read source-of-truth back; assert SHA matches expectation | `examples/<name>_fsv.rs` per crate |
| **Per-language oracle round-trip** | real source → AST chunker → instruments → panel → predictor → Gτ; oracle outcome compared against language toolchain | `examples/ast_chunker_multilang_fsv` |
| **UTML L_step validation** | 100 synthetic examples spanning ΔP/ΔK/ΔΩ/ΔΞ corner cases | `train_edge_cases_fsv` |
| **Constellation Gτ FP rate** | ≤ 5% on held-out KnownGood per language | `crates/context-graph-mejepa-tct/examples/` |
| **Drift drill** | Inject 70% empirical conformal coverage; detect within 1,000 obs; trigger A/B/C | `mejepa-heal-drill` binary + `phase5_self_healing_fsv` |
| **EWC++ non-degeneracy** | After 10 task boundaries, weight protection has full rank | `crates/context-graph-mejepa/heal/ewc_tests.rs` |
| **Witness chain replay** | End-to-end on 100,000-entry chain | `crates/context-graph-witness/cert_tests.rs` |
| **Shift subscriber latency** | p50 < 5s, p99 < 30s on synthetic shift stream | `crates/context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber/examples/shift_subscriber_fsv` |
| **Cross-cutting integration (futurebuild)** | provenance integrity + watermark restart-safety + scheduler+drift+heal end-to-end | `TASK-TEST-001` |
| **Chaos (futurebuild)** | random task kills, disk-full injection, embedder digest swap | `TASK-TEST-005` |
| **Load (futurebuild)** | 100k synthetic shifts/min sustained; p99 latency | `TASK-TEST-006` |
| **Soak (futurebuild)** | 72-hour continuous operation; memory + state stability | `TASK-TEST-007` (quarterly) |

---

### 3. FSV protocol (`docs/futurebuild/specs/FSV-PROTOCOL.md`)

Inherited contract for every TASK-* file:

| Step | Action |
|---|---|
| **§1 SoT readback** | Identify source-of-truth (file path / RocksDB CF / git SHA); read back after action and assert match |
| **§2 ≥3 boundary cases** | empty input, maximum input, malformed input — print state before + after |
| **§3 NO mocks** | Real Python / cargo / jest oracles; real Docker; real RocksDB; real tree-sitter |
| **§4 NO backwards compatibility** | Pre-production; wipe DB on schema change |
| **§5 Fail-closed errors** | Every fallible op returns `Result<T, ContextGraphError>` with structured error + `.code()` tag |
| **§6 Evidence artifacts** | Per-FSV evidence file under `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/<crate>-fsv/` (mode 0600) |
| **§7 ASSUME THE TASK IS WRONG** | Audit every path, symbol, CF reference against the live tree before implementing |

A task is not done without all §1–§6. Regardless of `cargo test` results.

---

### 4. FSV evidence locations

Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md §testing_requirements`:

| Subsystem | FSV path |
|---|---|
| Phase 0 corpus | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/context-graph-mejepa-corpus/` |
| Instruments + Panel | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-instruments-fsv/` → `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/` (migration in `TASK-OBS-006`) |
| Predictor | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-predictor-fsv/` |
| Training | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-train-fsv/` |
| TCT constellation | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-tct-fsv/` |
| Shift subscriber | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-shift-subscriber-fsv/` |
| Per-language AST chunkers | `/tmp/contextgraph-{python,rust,c,cpp,java,csharp,go,js,ts,ruby,php}-ast-chunker-fsv/` |
| ME-JEPA-cf CF round-trip | `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/mejepa-cf-fsv/round_trip.json` |
| Eval (Phase 8) | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-phase8-eval-fsv/` (17 evidence files) |
| Hygiene GC stress | `/tmp/contextgraph-mejepa-hygiene-fsv/gc_stress_fsv/` |
| Tier compression | `/tmp/contextgraph-tier-compression-fsv/` |

All paths migrate to `/mnt/d/contextgraph/fsv/` per `TASK-OBS-006`.

---

### 5. How to run tests

```bash
# Full workspace
cargo test --workspace

# Single crate
cargo test -p context-graph-mejepa-corpus
cargo test -p context-graph-mejepa-tct chunk_gtau --lib

# FSV harness binaries
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mejepa --example predictor_fsv
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mejepa-train --example train_edge_cases_fsv
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mejepa --example phase5_self_healing_fsv
cargo run --release -p context-graph-mejepa --example phase8_eval_fsv
cargo run --release -p context-graph-core --example ast_chunker_multilang_fsv

# Heal drill (binary)
cargo run --release --bin mejepa-heal-drill -- --drift soft

# Lint
cargo clippy --workspace -- -D warnings

# Format
cargo fmt --check
```

---

### 6. Key fixtures + test utilities

Source: `crates/context-graph-test-utils/`. **NO MOCK DATA** — real synthetic only.

| Utility | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `fingerprints.rs` | Real fingerprint generators (E1–E14 vectors) |
| `stores.rs` | Store factory helpers (real RocksDB, real HNSW) |

Tests must NOT mock embedders. Use real model outputs OR real synthetic deterministic vectors.

---

### 7. Integration test surfaces (futurebuild Phase F)

Per `docs/futurebuild/18_INTEGRATION_AND_E2E_TESTING.md`:

| Test | Surface |
|---|---|
| `TASK-TEST-001` | Cross-cutting: provenance integrity end-to-end |
| `TASK-TEST-002` | Watermark restart-safety: kill mid-tail, restart, verify cursor resumes |
| `TASK-TEST-003` | Scheduler + drift + heal: inject drift, verify intervention + promote |
| `TASK-TEST-004` | Hook → shift-log → subscriber → predict round-trip |
| `TASK-TEST-005` | Chaos: random task kills + disk-full injection + embedder digest swap |
| `TASK-TEST-006` | Load: 100k synthetic shifts/min sustained; p99 latency report |
| `TASK-TEST-007` | Soak: 72-hour continuous; memory + state stability (quarterly) |
| `TASK-TEST-008` | Multi-language oracle round-trip (all 11 langs) |
| `TASK-TEST-009` | A/B/C promotion: simulate hold-out wins, ensure correct pointer flip |
| `TASK-TEST-010` | Agent-feedback path: synthetic SurpriseEvent → sampling weight bump verification |
| `TASK-TEST-011` | DR drill: backup + restore + verify witness chain |

---

### 8. Negative tests (must fail closed)

| Test | Expected failure |
|---|---|
| Panel with dim ≠ 5,120 | `MEJEPA_INFER_DIM_MISMATCH` |
| Instrument output non-finite | `MEJEPA_PANEL_NON_FINITE` |
| Embedder digest mismatch | `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_DIGEST_MISMATCH` — daemon refuses start |
| Constellation > 90 days stale | `MEJEPA_INFER_GTAU_STALE_CONSTELLATION` |
| Conformal calibration N=0 | `MEJEPA_CALIBRATION_INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES` |
| Corpus output to `/tmp` | rejected by `context-graph-paths` |
| Tree-sitter parse error | `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_PARSE_FAILED` with byte-offset diagnostics |
| Mixed-language source | `MEJEPA_CHUNKER_MIXED_LANGUAGE` |
| Mutation site bounds invalid | `MEJEPA_CORPUS_OPERATOR_FAILED` |
| ΔΞ.target_collapse > 0 | `MEJEPA_INSTRUMENT_GRADIENT_LEAK` (critical bug) |
| Call to retired E5 | `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_RETIRED` |
| Call to retired E11 | `MEJEPA_EMBEDDER_RETIRED` |
| Call to `reality_run_attempt` | `CCREALITY_ENGINE_RETIRED` |
| `mejepa_record_agent_feedback` > 100/hr | `MEJEPA_FEEDBACK_RATE_LIMITED` |
| Feedback against non-existent prediction | `MEJEPA_FEEDBACK_PREDICTION_NOT_FOUND` |
| Patch unparseable | `MEJEPA_VERIFY_INVALID_PATCH` |
| OOD score > hard threshold | `MEJEPA_VERIFY_OUT_OF_DISTRIBUTION_HARD` |
| Every chunk fails Gτ | `MEJEPA_VERIFY_GUARD_REJECTED_HARD` |
| D-drive missing | `MEJEPA_D_DRIVE_UNAVAILABLE` |
| > 5 scheduler restarts/5min | `MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING` |
| Subscriber lag > 500/min | `MEJEPA_SHIFT_SUBSCRIBER_LAG_ALERT` |

---

### 9. Reproducibility tests

| Test | Verifies |
|---|---|
| Mutation operator determinism | Same input + same seed → byte-equal output |
| Witness chain replay | 100k-entry chain hash-verifies start-to-end |
| Training cert replay | step N's `self_hash` == `SHAKE-256(parent_witness_hash || cert_body)` |
| Embedder digest pinning | Model load failure on wrong SHA |
| Per-(predictor_version × calibration × constellation × theta_sha) | Same triple produces byte-equal `RealityPrediction` |

---

### 10. Quarterly soak test (futurebuild)

Per `TASK-TEST-007`. 72-hour continuous operation on synthetic shift stream:

```
- Inject 100 synthetic shifts/minute (Python + Rust files)
- Daemon runs end-to-end (subscriber + scheduler + GC + eval)
- Capture: RSS over time, VRAM over time, RocksDB SST size, lag p50/p99, A/B/C events
- Assertions:
  - RSS does not grow > 2× steady-state
  - VRAM does not exceed budget
  - Lag p99 < 30s
  - At least 1 A/B/C cycle completes
  - Witness chain valid at end
  - No MEJEPA_SCHEDULER_FLAPPING
```

---

### 11. Cross-references

[`docs/futurebuild/specs/FSV-PROTOCOL.md`](../futurebuild/specs/FSV-PROTOCOL.md), [`docs/ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md`](../ruvectorfindings/specs/constitution.md), [`29_verification_report.md`](29_verification_report.md).


<!-- ===== END: 26_testing_strategy.md ===== -->

---

<a id="27-api-stability-versioning"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 27_api_stability_versioning.md (sha256:3a04c726935e530955e4f4db87ad5e8413bae87525dbe2d1c735b12c1f8b18b0, 170 lines) ===== -->

## 27 — API Stability + Versioning

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/24_API_STABILITY.md`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/provenance.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa/src/types.rs`, `crates/context-graph-mejepa-cf/src/lib.rs`.

> Semver + deprecation cycle + replay reproducibility for MCP tools, `RealityPrediction` schema, and RocksDB CFs.

---

### 1. Semver policy

Pre-1.0 (current): **schema changes wipe DB** per `memory/feedback_no_backwards_compat.md`. No migration shims. Synthetic data only.

Post-1.0 (futurebuild `TASK-API-002`): semver with:

| Version bump | Cause |
|---|---|
| MAJOR | Breaking change to a stable MCP tool signature; removal of a CF; removal of a head from `RealityPrediction` |
| MINOR | New MCP tool; new optional field on `RealityPrediction`; new CF; new prediction class enum variant |
| PATCH | Bug fix; performance improvement; doc-only |

---

### 2. MCP tool stability

Per `TASK-API-001`. Tools fall into three classes:

| Class | Tools | Stability |
|---|---|---|
| **Stable** | `mejepa_verify`, `mejepa_predict_latest`, `mejepa_record_agent_feedback`, `mejepa_subscriber_status`, `mejepa_heal_status`, `mejepa_quota_status`, `mejepa_eval_run`, `mejepa_ship_gate_status`, `mejepa_active_learning_queue`, `mejepa_constellation_inspect`, `optimizer_record_recommendation` | MAJOR bump to break |
| **Beta** | `mejepa_predict_what_if`, `mejepa_explain_prediction`, `mejepa_observe_shift`, `mejepa_capture_audit`, all futurebuild additions | MINOR bump to break in dev cycles |
| **Internal** | `optimizer_witness_chain_verify`, `optimizer_witness_chain_diff`, `_repair_legacy`, `optimizer_compute_influence`, `optimizer_bandit_*` | no stability guarantee |

---

### 3. Schema extension policy

Per `RealityPrediction`:

- **Adding fields**: always additive (new optional field with default `[]` or `None`). Old callers see same response shape + new fields they can ignore.
- **Adding enum variants**: additive. Old serialized values still deserialize (Rust's `#[serde(other)]` catches new variants).
- **Removing fields**: requires MAJOR bump + deprecation cycle (mark `#[deprecated]` for 2 minor versions before removal).
- **Renaming fields**: forbidden; add new field + deprecate old.
- **Reshaping a field's type**: requires MAJOR bump.

Per `RocksDB CF`:

- **Adding a CF**: additive; `create_missing_column_families=true` handles first-start.
- **Removing a CF**: data orphaned on disk (RocksDB tolerates extra CFs); MAJOR bump.
- **Changing CF key/value layout**: pre-1.0 wipe; post-1.0 requires migration tool (`TASK-API-002`).

---

### 4. `provenance.predictor_version`

Every `RealityPrediction` and `TrainingCertificate` carries:

```rust
pub struct PredictionProvenance {
    pub predictor_version: String,        // "v0.4.2-bf16-cuda13.2"
    pub constellation_version: String,    // CF_MEJEPA_CONSTELLATION's version_id
    pub calibration_version: String,      // conformal calibration version
    pub active_pointer: String,           // weights SHA
}
```

Clients that need cross-prediction version comparison use these as version keys. Replay tool (`TASK-OBS-011-prediction-replay-tool`, futurebuild) reproduces a prediction byte-for-byte from `(predictor_version, calibration_version, constellation_version, active_pointer)`.

---

### 5. `code_version` provenance

Every persistent artifact (panel, training cert, prediction, agent feedback) binds:

```jsonc
{
  "code_version": "git-sha-<hex>",
  "frozen_at": "2026-05-11T22:05:00Z",
  "corpus_sha": "<sha>",
  "embedder_versions": { "E1": "...", "E7": "...", ... },
  "witness_hash": "shake256:73:..."
}
```

This enables:
- Byte-equal reproduction of a past prediction given the same `(code_version, embedder_versions, corpus_sha, calibration_version, constellation_version, active_pointer)` tuple.
- Forensic root-cause: "what code version produced this prediction?"

---

### 6. Deprecation cycle (post-1.0)

```
Version N:        feature ships; stable
Version N+1:      deprecation announced via #[deprecated] attribute + docs warning
Version N+2:      deprecation continues; emit runtime warning when called
Version N+3:      removed (MAJOR bump)
```

For MCP tools: stub remains in place returning `MEJEPA_*_DEPRECATED` for at least one minor version before removal.

---

### 7. Backward-compatibility shim policy

Per `memory/feedback_no_backwards_compat.md`: **NEVER add backwards-compat shims unless operator explicitly requests.**

Pre-production. Synthetic data only. Wipe DB on schema change. Never write migration shims.

Post-1.0 (when shims become necessary): each shim is `TASK-API-NNN`-tracked + documented in `docs/futurebuild/24_API_STABILITY.md`.

---

### 8. Replay reproducibility

The `provenance` block on every `RealityPrediction` is the audit-substrate closure. A future client can:

1. Lookup `provenance.active_pointer` in `CF_MEJEPA_WEIGHT_BLOBS` → load weights
2. Lookup `provenance.constellation_version` in `CF_MEJEPA_CONSTELLATION` → load centroids
3. Lookup `provenance.calibration_version` in calibration storage → load thresholds
4. Run `mejepa_verify(<patch>, <context>)` against the same inputs → byte-equal `RealityPrediction`

This is the **`TASK-OBS-011 prediction-replay-tool`** (futurebuild Phase F).

---

### 9. Changelog

Per `TASK-API-005`. `CHANGELOG.md` at workspace root, maintained per release. Categories:

```
## [vX.Y.Z] - YYYY-MM-DD

### Added
- New MCP tool: ...
- New CF: ...
- New prediction class variant: ...

### Changed
- (additive only pre-1.0; SemVer post-1.0)

### Deprecated
- ...

### Removed
- ...

### Fixed
- bug XYZ in ...

### Security
- vulnerability fix
```

---

### 10. MCP protocol-level versioning

| Protocol | Version |
|---|---|
| JSON-RPC | 2.0 (frozen) |
| MCP | per Anthropic spec |
| ME-JEPA tool family | namespaced `mcp__cgreality__mejepa_*` (versioned via tool-set version field in `get_capability_matrix`) |

Operator can call `get_capability_matrix` to read the current tool roster + their semver tags.

---

### 11. Cross-references

[`04_database_schema.md §3`](04_database_schema.md) (schema versioning), [`15_witness_chain.md`](15_witness_chain.md) (provenance binding), [`17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md`](17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md) (schema-stable enum extension).

<a id="28-future-domains-mejepa-x"></a>

<!-- ===== BEGIN: 28_future_domains_mejepa_x.md (sha256:fafff77b03399c6baec88ee894861783363c8928e739128ea13dfe13d6a5613f, 139 lines) ===== -->

## 28 — Future Domains (ME-JEPA-X Template)

Source files covered: `docs/futurebuild/25_FUTURE_DOMAINS_MEJEPA_X.md`, `docs/ruvectorfindings/13_TELEOLOGICAL_CONSTELLATION_TRAINING_MEJEPA.md`, `crates/context-graph-dynamicjepa/`.

> Template for adapting the ME-JEPA architecture to non-code modalities. The architecture is **language-agnostic and domain-agnostic** at the framework level; modality-specific work is in the instrument set + label sources.

---

### 1. ME-JEPA-Code is the code-domain instance of TCT

Per `docs/ruvectorfindings/13_TELEOLOGICAL_CONSTELLATION_TRAINING_MEJEPA.md §6`. The TCT (Teleological Constellation Training) framework is the abstract pattern:

```
Domain X requires:
  - Frozen externally-defined instruments (E_X1, E_X2, ...) that map typed events to fixed-dim vectors
  - A deterministic reality oracle (the test runner / ground truth source for domain X)
  - A constellation of per-(category × entity × language × embedder) centroids
  - A Gτ guard test
  - A predictor mapping (panel_t-1, panel_t) → predicted_panel
  - 4 UTML factors ΔP / ΔK / ΔΩ / ΔΞ with target_collapse ≡ 0
  - A continuous self-optimization loop
  - An agent-feedback channel
```

Code domain (ME-JEPA-Code) is the first concrete instantiation. Voice / Image / etc. follow the same template.

---

### 2. Modality-specific work per domain

| Domain | Frozen instruments | Reality oracle | Chunker | Embedders |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **ME-JEPA-Code** (shipped) | E_AST, E_CFG, E_DataFlow, E_TypeGraph, E_Test, E_Trace, E_Diff, E_Witness, E_Oracle, E_Problem, E_CommitMsg, E_StaticAnalysis, E_Runtime, E_Reasoning, Scalars | Docker test runner (SWE-bench) | tree-sitter 11-language | E1, E7, E8, E10, E12, E13, E14, E17 |
| **ME-JEPA-Voice** (future) | E_Phoneme, E_Pitch, E_Cadence, E_Spectrum, E_VAD, E_TTS_Reference, E_Speaker_ID, ... | TTS faithfulness oracle (waveform MSE + perceptual loss) | Word-segmented + voiced-region chunker | Wav2Vec2, HuBERT, Whisper-encoder |
| **ME-JEPA-Image** (future) | E_Object, E_Saliency, E_OCR, E_Color, E_Composition, E_Style, E_Edge, ... | Vision-task oracle (classification accuracy / segmentation IoU / detection mAP) | Object-segment / region-based chunker | CLIP, DINO, SAM, Stable-Diffusion-VAE-encoder |
| **ME-JEPA-Robotics** (future) | E_Pose, E_Force, E_TorqueTrace, E_VisionFlow, E_SafetyEnvelope, ... | Real-world / sim oracle (task completion + constraint violation) | Action-bracketed time-window chunker | RT-1 / RT-2 features, PhysCLIP |
| **ME-JEPA-Math** (future) | E_AST_FormalProof, E_TypeContext, E_UnifierTrace, E_TacticPath, ... | Coq / Lean / Isabelle proof-checker | Tactic-bounded chunker | math-domain embedders |

---

### 3. Reusable across all domains

The following crates are **modality-agnostic** and can be reused unchanged:

| Crate | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `context-graph-witness` | Witness chain (SHAKE-256 + ed25519 + Merkle) |
| `context-graph-tier-compression` | 8/7/5/3-bit groupwise quantization |
| `context-graph-mincut` | Stoer-Wagner global mincut for splits |
| `context-graph-paths` | D-drive durable path resolver |
| `context-graph-solver` | MMR + conjugate gradient + conformal + PageRank + Granger F-test |
| `context-graph-mejepa-cf` | Canonical CF name registry (extended per domain) |
| `context-graph-mejepa-train` (trunk + UTML carriers) | Training loop, AdamW, cosine LR, cert chain, BatchSampler, ΔP/ΔK/ΔΩ/ΔΞ |
| `context-graph-mejepa-tct` | Constellation + Gτ guard (parameterized by domain's centroid layout) |
| `context-graph-mejepa-hygiene` | Tier transitions + quota + GC + Merkle compression |
| `context-graph-mejepa-shift-subscriber` | Tokio JSONL tail + watermark + cache |
| `context-graph-mejepa::compiler` | `compile / verify / observe` API |
| `context-graph-mejepa::eval` | Holdout eval + active-learning + patch-similarity graph |
| `context-graph-mejepa::heal` | Drift / EWC++ / A/B/C / continual backprop |
| Daemon supervisor + scheduler | per `context-graph-mcp::daemon` |
| Hook layer (`.claude/`) | UserPromptSubmit + PreToolUse + PostToolUse + Stop + PreCompact |
| SMAP (`./memory/`) | Cross-turn team narrative |

---

### 4. Per-domain work

For a new domain D:

| Step | Work |
|---|---|
| 1 | Define D's typed event taxonomy (analog of `Chunk` for code) |
| 2 | Implement D's chunker (analog of `memory::ast::chunk_with_options`) |
| 3 | Select D's frozen embedders (the 13 active for code → some N for D) |
| 4 | Define D's instrument slots (the 15 for code → some M for D, summing to e.g. 5,120-d or different) |
| 5 | Implement D's reality oracle bridge (Docker → D's deterministic verifier) |
| 6 | Define D's mutation operators (8 for code → some K for D) |
| 7 | Define D's `EntityType` + `MutationCategory` enums |
| 8 | Generate D's corpus (analog of 300×8 SWE-bench) |
| 9 | Build D's constellation (per-(category × entity_kind × subdomain × embedder)) |
| 10 | Calibrate D's Gτ thresholds |
| 11 | Define D's prediction taxonomy heads (Q3/Q4 for D) |
| 12 | Train D's predictor (reusing trunk architecture) |
| 13 | Wire D's MCP tools (`mcp__cgreality__mejepa_<D>_*`) |
| 14 | Establish D's ship gate (≥0.95 per-cell correlation)) |

Most of these are **structural copies** of the code-domain analogs in different crates: `context-graph-mejepa-voice-corpus`, `context-graph-mejepa-voice-instruments`, etc.

---

### 5. Plugin architecture for prediction heads

Per `D8`. Today's heads are hard-coded enum variants. For modality plug-in:

```rust
pub trait PredictionHead: Send + Sync {
    fn head_id(&self) -> HeadId;
    fn output_dim(&self) -> usize;
    fn forward(&self, trunk_output: &Tensor, panel: &Panel) -> Result<HeadOutput, HeadError>;
    fn label_source(&self) -> Box<dyn LabelSource>;
    fn loss_weight_lambda(&self) -> f32;
}

pub trait LabelSource: Send + Sync {
    fn extract_labels(&self, observation: &Observation) -> Result<Vec<Label>, LabelError>;
}
```

Per-domain plug-in registers heads via `HeadRegistry::register_head(domain: Domain, head: Box<dyn PredictionHead>)`. Today's code-domain heads (Panel, Oracle, FailureMode, EdgeCase, TechDebt, Perf, Security, Accuracy, Cost, Reasoning) become the canonical implementations for `Domain::Code`.

---

### 6. Cross-domain transfer

Per the `dynamicjepa_cross_domain_transfer` MCP tool (already shipped at code level): patches/states/observations in one domain can predict in another via **shared trunk** + per-domain projections.

The shared trunk learns modality-agnostic dynamics; per-domain heads + per-domain instruments provide modality-specific signal. Transfer experiments quantify how much code-domain training helps math-domain or voice-domain prediction.

Operationally: `dynamicjepa cross-domain-transfer --source counter_world --target gridworld_5x5` exists today as a pilot. Future-domain ME-JEPA-X work will use a similar mechanism scaled up.

---

### 7. Status

| Domain | Status |
|---|---|
| ME-JEPA-Code | **active build** (Phase A–H; futurebuild plan) |
| ME-JEPA-Voice | template documented; concrete build deferred per operator |
| ME-JEPA-Image | template documented; concrete build deferred per operator |
| ME-JEPA-Robotics | template documented; concrete build deferred per operator |
| ME-JEPA-Math | template documented; concrete build deferred per operator |

Per `docs/futurebuild/17_BLIND_SPOTS_AND_MISSING_CAPABILITIES.md §O4`: concrete domain builds wait for operator priority. Template + plugin architecture (`D8`) make subsequent domains primarily configuration + instrument-set work, not architecture work.

---

[`07_instruments_panel.md`](07_instruments_panel.md) (the instrument trait — generic interface), [`09_predictor_multi_head.md`](09_predictor_multi_head.md) (per-domain heads), [`08_dda_tct_enrichment.md`](08_dda_tct_enrichment.md) (constellation framework), [`17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md`](17_prediction_taxonomy_reference.md) (taxonomy template).

### 14. Verifiable bottom line

Per `docs/futurebuild/02_CURRENT_STATE_HONEST.md §6`:

If you `cargo build --release --workspace` from a fresh clone of `ccreality-cutover` branch:

- ✅ **Builds**. Workspace compiles.
- ✅ **Tests**. `cargo test --workspace` passes (~6.5k+ tests on the consolidated tree).
- ✅ **MCP starts**. `cargo run --release -p context-graph-mcp` boots, exposes 19 ME-JEPA tools (+ 187 other tools = 206 total).
- ✅ **Daemon mode boots**. `--daemon-server-only --d-root /mnt/d/contextgraph` starts long-running supervised tasks when D-root validation passes.
- ✅ **`mejepa_verify` answers**. Returns `Verdict` (likely `OutOfDistribution` because no production training has run).
- ✅ **`mejepa_predict_latest` answers**. Returns deterministic placeholder or real predicted panel.

What does NOT happen out of the box:

- ❌ Predictions are NOT *automatically* injected into Claude Code's prompts (no UserPromptSubmit-from-MCP-state path yet — `TASK-PREDICT-001` futurebuild Phase A).
- ❌ Shift subscriber + self-optim scheduler start under daemon mode (code shipped; operator-verified first-time install proof pending).
- ❌ 300×8 corpus not durable on D-drive (`/tmp` run lost after reboot; rerun in progress at `/mnt/d/contextgraph/corpus/swebench-lite-python-300x8-v1-20260511/`; 1,200/2,400 rows complete; blocked on `source_patch_support.rs` timeout fix).
- ❌ Ship gate ≥ 0.95 not met (predictor not yet trained on full corpus).

The futurebuild plan (Phases A–H) turns the ❌ list into ✅.
